CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[Archive_AUDIT_LOGS] (@NoOfMonths INT )
AS
BEGIN
	declare @time nvarchar(100)
	declare @curdt varchar(10)
	SET @time='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message],[Level],[TimeStamp],[Exception],[LogEvent])
	VALUES ('Archival Audit Log Data ' + @time,'Information',GETUTCDATE(),null,@time)
BEGIN TRY
    INSERT INTO ALIPS_Archive.[dbo].[AuditLogs] 
	(
	CreationTime,
	CreationUser,
	Id,
	ActivityCategory,
	ActivitySubCategory,
	Level,
	ActivityMessage,
	ActivityMessageDesc,
	UserIpAddress,
	QueryUrl,
	FileName,
	CustomerNo,
	InvoiceNo,
	ShipmentId,
	Country,
	SLIC,
	AccountNumber,
	OldValue,
	NewValue
	)
SELECT 
      CreationTime,
	CreationUser,
	Id,
	ActivityCategory,
	ActivitySubCategory,
	Level,
	ActivityMessage,
	ActivityMessageDesc,
	UserIpAddress,
	QueryUrl,
	FileName,
	CustomerNo,
	InvoiceNo,
	ShipmentId,
	Country,
	SLIC,
	AccountNumber,
	OldValue,
	NewValue
	FROM [dbo].[AuditLogs]  WITH(NOLOCK)
	WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	delete from [dbo].[AuditLogs] 
	WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	select 'True' as [status]
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @curdt='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message], [Level], [TimeStamp], [Exception], [LogEvent] )
		VALUES ('Archive Data AuditLogs Table, AuditLogs: Date='+@curdt+'' , 'Error', GETUTCDATE(), null, ERROR_MESSAGE())
		select ERROR_MESSAGE() as [status]
	END CATCH
	END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON

	  FROM [dbo].[CustomerProfiles] CP WITH(NOLOCK)
	  where  NOT EXISTS(select 1 from ALIPS_Archive.[dbo].[CustomerProfiles] ARCHIVECP WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE CP.ACCOUNTNUMBER=ARCHIVECP.ACCOUNTNUMBER 
	  AND CP.CUSTOMERCOUNTRYCODE=ARCHIVECP.CUSTOMERCOUNTRYCODE)
	select 'True' as [status]
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @curdt='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message], [Level], [TimeStamp], [Exception], [LogEvent] )
		VALUES ('Archive Data CustomerProfiles Table, CustomerProfiles: Date=' +@curdt+'' , 'Error', GETUTCDATE(), null, ERROR_MESSAGE())
		select ERROR_MESSAGE() as [status]
	END CATCH
	END

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[Archive_CustomerCountries] ( @NoOfMonths INT )
AS
BEGIN
	declare @time nvarchar(100)
	declare @curdt varchar(100)
	SET @time='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message],[Level],[TimeStamp],[Exception],[LogEvent])
	VALUES ('Archival Customer Countries Data ' + @time,'Information',GETUTCDATE(),null,@time)
BEGIN TRY
     INSERT INTO [ALIPS_Archive].[dbo].[CustomerCountries]
	 (
	  [CreationTime]
      ,[CreationUser]
      ,[LastModificationTime]
      ,[LastModificationUser]
      ,[DeletionTime]
      ,[IsDeleted]
      ,[DeletionUser]
      ,[Id]
      ,[Code]
      ,[DisplayText]
      ,[Description]
	  )
	  SELECT 
	  [CreationTime]
      ,[CreationUser]
      ,[LastModificationTime]
      ,[LastModificationUser]
      ,[DeletionTime]
      ,[IsDeleted]
      ,[DeletionUser]
      ,[Id]
      ,[Code]
      ,[DisplayText]
      ,[Description]
	  FROM [dbo].[CustomerCountries] WITH(NOLOCK)
	  where Id not in (select Id from [ALIPS_Archive].[dbo].[CustomerCountries] WITH(NOLOCK))
	select 'True' as [status]
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		set  @curdt =CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message], [Level], [TimeStamp], [Exception], [LogEvent] )
		VALUES ('Archive Data Customer Countries Table, CustomerCountries: Date=' + @curdt+' ', 'Error', GETUTCDATE(), null, ERROR_MESSAGE())
	select ERROR_MESSAGE() as [status]
	END CATCH
	END

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[Archive_Delete_Data] (@NoOfMonths INT )
AS
BEGIN
	declare @time nvarchar(100)
	declare @curdt varchar(10)
	SET @time='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message],[Level],[TimeStamp],[Exception],[LogEvent])
	VALUES ('Archival Data Delete from ARC DB ' + @time,'Information',GETUTCDATE(),null,@time)
BEGIN TRY
	delete from ALIPS_Archive.[dbo].[AuditLogs]	WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	delete from ALIPS_Archive.[dbo].[BilledInvoiceShipments] WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	delete from ALIPS_Archive.[dbo].[BilledInvoiceInfos] WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	delete from ALIPS_Archive.[dbo].[InvoiceCharges] WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	delete from ALIPS_Archive.[dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	delete from ALIPS_Archive.[dbo].[InvoiceShipments] WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	delete from ALIPS_Archive.[dbo].[InvoiceInfos] WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	select 'True' as [status]
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @curdt='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message], [Level], [TimeStamp], [Exception], [LogEvent] )
		VALUES ('Archival Data Delete from ARC DB Err: Date='+@curdt+'' , 'Error', GETUTCDATE(), null, ERROR_MESSAGE())
		select ERROR_MESSAGE() as [status]
	END CATCH
	END

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[Archive_REFRENCES] ( @NoOfMonths INT )
AS
BEGIN
	declare @time nvarchar(100)
	declare @curdt varchar(100)
	SET @time='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message],[Level],[TimeStamp],[Exception],[LogEvent])
	VALUES ('Archival References Data ' + @time,'Information',GETUTCDATE(),null,@time)
BEGIN TRY
     INSERT INTO [ALIPS_Archive].[dbo].[References]
	 (
	  CreationTime,
	  CreationUser,
	  LastModificationTime,
	  LastModificationUser,
	  DeletionTime,
	  [DeletionUser],
	  [IsDeleted],
	  [Id],
	  [Type],
	  [Code],
	  [DisplayText],
	  [Description]
	  )
	  SELECT 
	  CreationTime,
	  CreationUser,
	  LastModificationTime,
	  LastModificationUser,
	  DeletionTime,
	  [DeletionUser],
	  [IsDeleted],
	  [Id],
	  [Type],
	  [Code],
	  [DisplayText],
	  [Description]
	  FROM [dbo].[References] WITH(NOLOCK)
	  where Id not in (select Id from [ALIPS_Archive].[dbo].[References] WITH(NOLOCK))
	select 'True' as [status]
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		set  @curdt =CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message], [Level], [TimeStamp], [Exception], [LogEvent] )
		VALUES ('Archive Data References Table, References: Date=' + @curdt+' ', 'Error', GETUTCDATE(), null, ERROR_MESSAGE())
	select ERROR_MESSAGE() as [status]
	END CATCH
	END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[AUDIT_LOGS] (@NoOfMonths INT )
AS
BEGIN
	declare @time nvarchar(100)
	SET @time='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
	INSERT INTO [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message],[Level],[TimeStamp],[Exception],[LogEvent])
	VALUES ('Archival Audit Log Data ' + @time,'Information',GETUTCDATE(),null,@time)
BEGIN TRANSACTION
BEGIN TRY
SET IDENTITY_INSERT ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[AuditLogs] ON;
    INSERT INTO ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[AuditLogs] 
	(
	CreationTime,
	CreationUser,
	Id,
	ActivityCategory,
	ActivitySubCategory,
	Level,
	ActivityMessage,
	ActivityMessageDesc,
	UserIpAddress,
	QueryUrl,
	FileName,
	CustomerNo,
	InvoiceNo,
	ShipmentId,
	Country,
	SLIC,
	AccountNumber,
	OldValue,
	NewValue
	)
SELECT 
      CreationTime,
	CreationUser,
	Id,
	ActivityCategory,
	ActivitySubCategory,
	Level,
	ActivityMessage,
	ActivityMessageDesc,
	UserIpAddress,
	QueryUrl,
	FileName,
	CustomerNo,
	InvoiceNo,
	ShipmentId,
	Country,
	SLIC,
	AccountNumber,
	OldValue,
	NewValue
	FROM ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[AuditLogs] 
	WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	delete from ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[AuditLogs] 
	WHERE CreationTime <=  Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	SET IDENTITY_INSERT ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[AuditLogs] OFF;
	COMMIT TRANSACTION
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION
		INSERT INTO ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message], [Level], [TimeStamp], [Exception], [LogEvent] )
		VALUES ('Archive Data AuditLogs Table, AuditLogs: Date=' + GETUTCDATE(), 'Error', GETUTCDATE(), null, ERROR_MESSAGE())
	END CATCH
	END

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[ChecklistReport]
AS
BEGIN
    SELECT MAX(cdate) AS PLatestdate, cny_cd AS PCountry  
    FROM InvoiceInfos 
    GROUP BY cny_cd
    SELECT MAX(cdate) AS TLatestdate, cny_cd AS TCountry  
    FROM BilledInvoiceInfos 
    GROUP BY cny_cd
    SELECT MAX(cdate) AS DLatestdate, cny_cd AS DCountry  
    FROM BilledInvoiceInfos 
    WHERE IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 1 
    GROUP BY cny_cd
    SELECT top 1 Max(inv_dt) as InvoiceDate, status
    FROM TaxInvoices 
	GROUP BY id, inv_dt, status
    ORDER BY id DESC
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[ChinaSummaryPage]
	@inv_no  Varchar(250)
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	if OBJECT_ID('tempdb..#chinasummary') is not null
		drop table #chinasummary
	if OBJECT_ID('tempdb..#chinasummary1') is not null
		drop table #chinasummary1
	if OBJECT_ID('tempdb..#chinasummary2') is not null
		drop table #chinasummary2
	SELECT billedinvoiceshipments.recid
                               AS
                               RecId,
                               billedinvoiceshipments.track_no
                               AS
                               TrackingNo,
                               billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type
                               AS
                               InvoiceType,
                               billedinvoiceshipments.recids
                               AS
                               RecIds,
                               CASE
                                 WHEN billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type NOT IN (
                                      SELECT
                                      transactiontype
                                      FROM
                                      invoicetransactions with(nolock)
                                      WHERE
                                      invoicetype =
                                      billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd) THEN 'R003_01'
                                 ELSE invoicetransactions.china
                               END
                               AS
                                      InvoiceTransactions,
                               billedinvoiceinfos.inv_no
                               AS
                               InvoiceNo,
                               billedinvoiceshipments.frt_taxapl
                               AS
                               FrtTaxapl,
                               CASE
                                 WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R008_01'
                                       OR invoicetransactions.china IS NULL THEN
                                 0
                                 WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
                                      AND ( Row_number()
                                              OVER (
                                                partition BY
billedinvoiceshipments.track_no, billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type
  ORDER BY billedinvoiceshipments.recids) = 2 ) THEN( -1 *
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_201 )
ELSE billedinvoiceshipments.acc_201
END                                                      AS
        Taxable2DataAcc201,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R003_01'
OR billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT
   transactiontype
                                    FROM   invoicetransactions with(nolock)
                                    WHERE  invoicetype =
billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd) THEN billedinvoiceshipments.acc_201
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               NonTaxable1DataAcc201,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R004_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_201
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Taxable1DataDataAcc201,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R005_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_201
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Adjustment2DataAcc201,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R008_01'
OR invoicetransactions.china IS NULL THEN 0
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
AND ( Row_number()
OVER (
  partition BY
billedinvoiceshipments.track_no, billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type
  ORDER BY billedinvoiceshipments.recids) = 2 ) THEN (
-billedinvoiceshipments.acc_205 )
ELSE billedinvoiceshipments.acc_205
END                                                      AS
        Taxable2DataAcc205,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R003_01'
OR billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT
   transactiontype
                                    FROM   invoicetransactions with(nolock)
                                    WHERE  invoicetype =
billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd) THEN billedinvoiceshipments.acc_205
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               NonTaxable1DataAcc205,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R004_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_205
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Taxable1DataDataAcc205,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R005_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_205
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Adjustment2DataAcc205,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R008_01'
OR invoicetransactions.china IS NULL THEN 0
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
AND ( Row_number()
OVER (
  partition BY
billedinvoiceshipments.track_no, billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type
  ORDER BY billedinvoiceshipments.recids) = 2 ) THEN( -1 *
billedinvoiceshipments.vartaxamt )
ELSE billedinvoiceshipments.vartaxamt
END                                                      AS
               Taxable2DataVarTaxAmt,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R003_01'
OR billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT
   transactiontype
                                    FROM   invoicetransactions with(nolock)
                                    WHERE  invoicetype =
billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd) THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.vartaxamt
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               NonTaxable1DataVartaxamt,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R004_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.vartaxamt
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Taxable1DataDataVartaxamt,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R005_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.vartaxamt
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Adjustment2DataAvartaxamt,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R008_01'
OR invoicetransactions.china IS NULL THEN 0
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
AND ( Row_number()
OVER (
  partition BY
billedinvoiceshipments.track_no, billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type
  ORDER BY billedinvoiceshipments.recids) = 2 ) THEN( -1 *
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_adc )
ELSE billedinvoiceshipments.acc_adc
END                                                      AS
        Taxable2DataAccAdc,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R003_01'
OR billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT
   transactiontype
                                    FROM   invoicetransactions with(nolock)
                                    WHERE  invoicetype =
billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd)THEN billedinvoiceshipments.acc_adc
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               NonTaxable1DataAccAdc,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R004_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_adc
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Taxable1DataDataAccAdc,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R005_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_adc
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Adjustment2DataAccAdc,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R008_01'
OR invoicetransactions.china IS NULL THEN 0
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
AND ( Row_number()
OVER (
  partition BY
billedinvoiceshipments.track_no, billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type
  ORDER BY billedinvoiceshipments.recids) = 2 ) THEN( -1 *
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_chg )
ELSE billedinvoiceshipments.acc_chg
END                                                      AS
        Taxable2DataAccChg,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R003_01'
OR billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT
   transactiontype
                                    FROM   invoicetransactions with(nolock)
                                    WHERE  invoicetype =
billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd) THEN billedinvoiceshipments.acc_chg
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               NonTaxable1DataAccChg,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R004_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_chg
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Taxable1DataDataAccChg,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R005_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.acc_chg
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Adjustment2DataAccChg,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R008_01'
OR invoicetransactions.china IS NULL THEN 0
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
AND ( Row_number()
OVER (
  partition BY
billedinvoiceshipments.track_no, billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type
  ORDER BY billedinvoiceshipments.recids) = 2 ) THEN( -1 *
billedinvoiceshipments.freight )
ELSE billedinvoiceshipments.freight
END                                                      AS
        Taxable2DataFreight,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R003_01'
OR billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT
   transactiontype
                                    FROM   invoicetransactions with(nolock)
                                    WHERE  invoicetype =
billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd) THEN billedinvoiceshipments.freight
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               NonTaxable1DataFreight,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R004_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.freight
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Taxable1DataDataFreight,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R005_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.freight
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Adjustment2DataFreight,
( CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R008_01'
OR invoicetransactions.china IS NULL THEN 0
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
AND ( Row_number()
  OVER (
    partition BY
billedinvoiceshipments.track_no, billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type
ORDER BY billedinvoiceshipments.recids) = 2 ) THEN( -1 *
billedinvoiceshipments.discount )
ELSE billedinvoiceshipments.discount
END )                                                    AS
        Taxable2DataDiscount
               ,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R003_01'
OR billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT
   transactiontype
                                    FROM   invoicetransactions with(nolock)
                                    WHERE  invoicetype =
billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd) THEN billedinvoiceshipments.discount
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               NonTaxable1DataDiscount,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R004_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.discount
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Taxable1DataDataDiscount,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R005_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.discount
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Adjustment2DataDiscount,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R008_01'
OR invoicetransactions.china IS NULL THEN 0
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
AND ( Row_number()
OVER (
  partition BY
billedinvoiceshipments.track_no, billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type
  ORDER BY billedinvoiceshipments.recids) = 2 ) THEN( -1 *
billedinvoiceshipments.tier_inct )
ELSE billedinvoiceshipments.tier_inct
END                                                      AS
        Taxable2DataTierInct
               ,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R003_01'
OR billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT
   transactiontype
                                    FROM   invoicetransactions with(nolock)
                                    WHERE  invoicetype =
billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd) THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.tier_inct
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               NonTaxable1DataTierInct,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R004_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.tier_inct
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Taxable1DataDataTierInct,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R005_01' THEN
billedinvoiceshipments.tier_inct
ELSE 0
END                                                      AS
               Adjustment2DataTierInct,
billedinvoiceinfos.bill_amt - billedinvoiceinfos.tax_var AS
        Amount,
CASE
WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R003_01' THEN vartaxamt
ELSE 0
END                                                      VAT
into #ChinaSummary
 FROM   billedinvoiceinfos WITH(nolock)
        JOIN billedinvoiceshipments WITH(nolock)
          ON billedinvoiceinfos.cny_cd =
             billedinvoiceshipments.cny_cd
             AND billedinvoiceinfos.billed_inv_no =
                 billedinvoiceshipments.billed_inv_no
        LEFT JOIN invoicetransactions WITH(nolock)
               ON billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd =
                  invoicetransactions.invoicetype
                  AND billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type =
                      invoicetransactions.transactiontype
 WHERE  billedinvoiceinfos.cny_cd = 'CN'
        AND billedinvoiceinfos.inv_no = @inv_no
		select * into #ChinaSummary1 from (SELECT CASE
                         WHEN ( invoicetype != 'MIS'
                                 OR invoicetype != 'ADC' ) THEN 'Transportation'
                         WHEN invoicetype = 'ADC' THEN 'Address Correction'
                         WHEN invoicetype = 'MIS' THEN 'BLANK'
                       END  AS ChargeType,
                       NULL ccode,
                       frttaxapl,
                       invoicetype,
                       vat  AS VAT,
                       CASE
                         WHEN ( invoicetype != 'MIS'
                                 OR invoicetype != 'ADC' )
                              AND 'Y' = 'N'THEN ( adjustment2datafreight
                                                  + nontaxable1datafreight
                                                  + taxable1datadatafreight
                                                  + taxable2datafreight )
                         WHEN invoicetype = 'ADC'
                              AND 'Y' = 'N' THEN ( adjustment2dataaccadc
                                                   + nontaxable1dataaccadc
                                                   + taxable1datadataaccadc +
                                                   taxable2dataaccadc
                                                 )
                         WHEN invoicetype = 'MIS'
                              AND 'Y' = 'N' THEN ( adjustment2dataaccchg
                                                   + nontaxable1dataaccchg
                                                   + taxable1datadataaccchg +
                                                   taxable2dataaccchg
                                                 )
                         ELSE 0
                       END  AS NonTaxableCharges,
                       CASE
                         WHEN ( invoicetype != 'MIS'
                                 OR invoicetype != 'ADC' )
                              AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN ( adjustment2datafreight
                                                   + nontaxable1datafreight
                                                   + taxable1datadatafreight
                                                   + taxable2datafreight )
                         WHEN invoicetype = 'ADC'
                              AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN ( adjustment2dataaccadc
                                                   + nontaxable1dataaccadc
                                                   + taxable1datadataaccadc +
                                                   taxable2dataaccadc
                                                 )
                         WHEN invoicetype = 'MIS'
                              AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN ( adjustment2dataaccchg
                                                   + nontaxable1dataaccchg
                                                   + taxable1datadataaccchg +
                                                   taxable2dataaccchg
                                                 )
                         ELSE 0
                       END  AS TaxableCharges,
                       CASE
                         WHEN invoicetype != 'MIS' THEN
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           + nontaxable1datadiscount
                           + taxable1datadatadiscount
                           + taxable2datadiscount ) + ( adjustment2datatierinct
                                                        +
                         nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                        +
                         taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                        + taxable2datatierinct )
                         )
                         ELSE 0
                       END  AS Discount,
                       CASE
                         WHEN invoicetype = 'ADC' THEN
                         ( adjustment2dataaccadc
                         + nontaxable1dataaccadc
                         + taxable1datadataaccadc + taxable2dataaccadc ) - (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           + nontaxable1datadiscount
                           +
                         taxable1datadatadiscount
                                                                               +
                           taxable2datadiscount )
                         +
                         ( adjustment2datatierinct
                           + nontaxable1datatierinct
                           + taxable1datadatatierinct
                           + taxable2datatierinct ) )
                         WHEN invoicetype = 'ADC'
                              AND invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 2 ) THEN
                         -1 * ( adjustment2dataaccadc
                              + nontaxable1dataaccadc
                              + taxable1datadataaccadc + taxable2dataaccadc )
                         - ( (
                         adjustment2datadiscount
                         + nontaxable1datadiscount
                         +
                         taxable1datadatadiscount
                         +
                         taxable2datadiscount ) + ( adjustment2datatierinct
                                                    + nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                    + taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                    + taxable2datatierinct ) )
                         WHEN invoicetype = 'MIS'
                              AND invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 2 ) THEN -1 * (
                         adjustment2dataaccchg
                         + nontaxable1dataaccchg
                         + taxable1datadataaccchg + taxable2dataaccchg )
                         WHEN invoicetype = 'MIS' THEN
                         adjustment2dataaccchg
                         + nontaxable1dataaccchg
                         + taxable1datadataaccchg + taxable2dataaccchg
                         WHEN ( invoicetype != 'MIS'
                                 OR invoicetype != 'ADC' )
                              AND invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 2 ) THEN
                         -1 * (
                         ( adjustment2datafreight
                           + nontaxable1datafreight
                           + taxable1datadatafreight
                           + taxable2datafreight ) -
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           +
                         nontaxable1datadiscount
                                                         +
                         taxable1datadatadiscount
                                                         + taxable2datadiscount
                         )
                         + (
                                                       adjustment2datatierinct
                                                       + nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                       +
                           taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                       + taxable2datatierinct )
                         ) )
                         WHEN ( invoicetype != 'MIS'
                                 OR invoicetype != 'ADC' ) THEN
                         ( adjustment2datafreight
                           + nontaxable1datafreight
                           + taxable1datadatafreight
                           + taxable2datafreight ) -
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           +
                         nontaxable1datadiscount
                                                         +
                         taxable1datadatadiscount
                                                         + taxable2datadiscount
                         )
                         + (
                                                       adjustment2datatierinct
                                                       + nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                       +
                           taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                       + taxable2datatierinct )
                         )
                       END  AS NetCharges,
                       CASE
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 1 ) THEN
                         ( adjustment2datafreight
                           + nontaxable1datafreight
                           + taxable1datadatafreight
                           + taxable2datafreight ) -
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           +
                         nontaxable1datadiscount
                                                         +
                         taxable1datadatadiscount
                                                         + taxable2datadiscount
                         )
                         + (
                                                       adjustment2datatierinct
                                                       + nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                       +
                           taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                       + taxable2datatierinct )
                         )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 2 ) THEN
                         -1 * (
                         ( adjustment2datafreight
                           + nontaxable1datafreight
                           + taxable1datadatafreight
                           + taxable2datafreight ) -
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           +
                         nontaxable1datadiscount
                                                         +
                         taxable1datadatadiscount
                                                         + taxable2datadiscount
                         )
                         + (
                                                       adjustment2datatierinct
                                                       + nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                       +
                           taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                       + taxable2datatierinct )
                         ) )
                         ELSE 0
                       END  AS taxable2NetChargesTotal,
                       CASE
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 1 )
                              AND ( invoicetype != 'MIS'
                                     OR invoicetype != 'ADC' )
                              AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN ( adjustment2datafreight
                                                   + nontaxable1datafreight
                                                   + taxable1datadatafreight
                                                   + taxable2datafreight )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 2 )
                              AND ( invoicetype != 'MIS'
                                     OR invoicetype != 'ADC' )
                              AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN -1 * ( adjustment2datafreight
                                                        + nontaxable1datafreight
                                                        +
                         taxable1datadatafreight
                                                        + taxable2datafreight )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 1 )
                              AND invoicetype = 'ADC'
                              AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN ( adjustment2dataaccadc
                                                   + nontaxable1dataaccadc
                                                   + taxable1datadataaccadc +
                                                   taxable2dataaccadc
                                                 )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 2 )
                              AND invoicetype = 'ADC'
                              AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN -1 * (
                         adjustment2dataaccadc
                         + nontaxable1dataaccadc
                         + taxable1datadataaccadc + taxable2dataaccadc )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 1 )
                              AND invoicetype = 'MIS'
                              AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN ( adjustment2dataaccchg
                                                   + nontaxable1dataaccchg
                                                   + taxable1datadataaccchg +
                                                   taxable2dataaccchg
                                                 )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 2 )
                              AND invoicetype = 'MIS'
                              AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN -1 * (
                         adjustment2dataaccchg
                         + nontaxable1dataaccchg
                         + taxable1datadataaccchg + taxable2dataaccchg )
                         ELSE 0
                       END  AS transportationTaxable2Total,
                       CASE
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 1 )
                              AND invoicetype != 'MIS' THEN
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           + nontaxable1datadiscount
                           + taxable1datadatadiscount
                           + taxable2datadiscount ) + ( adjustment2datatierinct
                                                        +
                         nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                        +
                         taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                        + taxable2datatierinct )
                         )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 2 )
                              AND invoicetype != 'MIS' THEN -1 *
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           + nontaxable1datadiscount
                           + taxable1datadatadiscount
                           + taxable2datadiscount ) + ( adjustment2datatierinct
                                                        +
                         nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                        +
                         taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                        + taxable2datatierinct )
                         )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 1 )
                              AND invoicetype != 'MIS' THEN
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           + nontaxable1datadiscount
                           + taxable1datadatadiscount
                           + taxable2datadiscount ) + ( adjustment2datatierinct
                                                        +
                         nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                        +
                         taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                        + taxable2datatierinct )
                         )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 2 )
                              AND invoicetype != 'MIS' THEN -1 *
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           + nontaxable1datadiscount
                           + taxable1datadatadiscount
                           + taxable2datadiscount ) + ( adjustment2datatierinct
                                                        +
                         nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                        +
                         taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                        + taxable2datatierinct )
                         )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 1 )
                              AND invoicetype != 'MIS' THEN
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           + nontaxable1datadiscount
                           + taxable1datadatadiscount
                           + taxable2datadiscount ) + ( adjustment2datatierinct
                                                        +
                         nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                        +
                         taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                        + taxable2datatierinct )
                         )
                         WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                              AND ( Row_number()
                                      OVER (
                                        partition BY trackingno, invoicetype
                                        ORDER BY recids) = 2 )
                              AND invoicetype != 'MIS' THEN -1 *
                         (
                         ( adjustment2datadiscount
                           + nontaxable1datadiscount
                           + taxable1datadatadiscount
                           + taxable2datadiscount ) + ( adjustment2datatierinct
                                                        +
                         nontaxable1datatierinct
                                                        +
                         taxable1datadatatierinct
                                                        + taxable2datatierinct )
                         )
                         ELSE 0
                       END  AS transportationDiscountTaxable2Total,
                       recid,
                       recids,
                       trackingno,
                       invoicetransactions,
                       invoiceno,
                       amount
                FROM  #ChinaSummary
				union
(SELECT chargecodes.englishdescription                           AS
 ChargeType,
 invoicecharges.ccode                                     AS
 Ccode,
 billedinvoiceshipments.frt_taxapl                        AS
 FrtTaxapl,
 billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type,
 0                                                        VAT,
 CASE
   WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
        AND ( Row_number()
                OVER (
                  partition BY track_nr,
                billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd
                  ORDER BY invoicecharges.recid) = 2 )
        AND ( invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y'
               OR ( billedinvoiceshipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y'
                    AND invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = '' ) ) THEN
   0
   WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
        AND ( Row_number()
                OVER (
                  partition BY track_nr,
                billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd
                  ORDER BY invoicecharges.recid) != 2 )
        AND ( invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y'
               OR ( billedinvoiceshipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y'
                    AND invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = '' ) ) THEN
   0
   WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R008_01'
        AND ( invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y'
               OR ( billedinvoiceshipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y'
                    AND invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = '' ) ) THEN
   0
   ELSE invoicecharges.publ_amt
 END                                                      AS
 NonTaxableCharges,
 CASE
   WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
        AND ( Row_number()
                OVER (
                  partition BY track_nr,
                billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd
                  ORDER BY invoicecharges.recid) = 2 )
        AND ( invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y'
               OR ( billedinvoiceshipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y'
                    AND invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = '' ) ) THEN
   -1 *
   invoicecharges.publ_amt
   WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
        AND ( Row_number()
                OVER (
                  partition BY track_nr,
                billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd
                  ORDER BY invoicecharges.recid) != 2 )
        AND ( invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y'
               OR ( billedinvoiceshipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y'
                    AND invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = '' ) ) THEN
   invoicecharges.publ_amt
   WHEN invoicetransactions.china != 'R008_01'
        AND ( invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y'
               OR ( billedinvoiceshipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y'
                    AND invoicecharges.chg_taxapl = '' ) ) THEN
   invoicecharges.publ_amt
   ELSE 0
 END                                                      AS
 TaxableCharges,
 CASE
   WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
        AND ( Row_number()
                OVER (
                  partition BY track_nr,
                billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd
                  ORDER BY invoicecharges.recid) = 2 ) THEN -1 *
   invoicecharges.inct_amt
   ELSE invoicecharges.inct_amt
 END                                                      AS
 Discount,
 CASE
   WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
        AND ( Row_number()
                OVER (
                  partition BY track_nr,
                billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd
                  ORDER BY invoicecharges.recid) = 2 ) THEN -1 *
   invoicecharges.camount
   ELSE invoicecharges.camount
 END                                                      AS
 NetCharges,
 CASE
   WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
        AND ( Row_number()
                OVER (
                  partition BY track_nr,
                billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd
                  ORDER BY invoicecharges.recid) != 2 ) THEN
   invoicecharges.camount
   WHEN invoicetransactions.china = 'R008_01'
        AND ( Row_number()
                OVER (
                  partition BY track_nr,
                billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd
                  ORDER BY invoicecharges.recid) = 2 ) THEN
   -1 * ( -1 * invoicecharges.camount )
   ELSE 0
 END                                                      AS
 taxable2NetChargesTotal,
 0                                                        AS
 transportationTaxable2Total,
 0                                                        AS
 transportationDiscountTaxable2Total,
 invoicecharges.recid                                     AS
 RecId,
 billedinvoiceshipments.recids                            AS
 RecIds,
 invoicecharges.track_nr,
 CASE
   WHEN billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT
        transactiontype
                                                FROM
        invoicetransactions with(nolock)
                                                WHERE
        invoicetype =
        billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd) THEN 'R003_01'
   ELSE invoicetransactions.china
 END                                                      AS
 InvoiceTransactions,
 billedinvoiceinfos.billed_inv_no                         AS
 InvoiceNumber,
 billedinvoiceinfos.bill_amt - billedinvoiceinfos.tax_var AS
 Amount
FROM   billedinvoiceinfos WITH(nolock)
 JOIN billedinvoiceshipments WITH(nolock)
   ON billedinvoiceinfos.billed_inv_no =
      billedinvoiceshipments.inv_no
      AND billedinvoiceinfos.cny_cd =
          billedinvoiceshipments.cny_cd
 JOIN invoicecharges WITH(nolock)
   ON billedinvoiceshipments.recid = invoicecharges.recid
      AND billedinvoiceshipments.cny_cd = invoicecharges.cny_cd
 JOIN chargecodes WITH(nolock)
   ON invoicecharges.ccode = chargecodes.chargecodes
      AND invoicecharges.cny_cd = chargecodes.countrycode
 LEFT JOIN invoicetransactions WITH(nolock)
        ON billedinvoiceinfos.type_cd =
           invoicetransactions.invoicetype
           AND billedinvoiceshipments.inv_type =
               invoicetransactions.transactiontype
WHERE  billedinvoiceinfos.cny_cd = 'CN'
 AND chargecodes.countrycode = 'CN'
 AND billedinvoiceinfos.inv_no = @inv_no)) as Ter
 SELECT Row_number()
                 OVER (
                   partition BY invoicetransactions, invoiceno
                   ORDER BY recid) AS RowNo,
               recid,
               chargetype,
               invoiceno,
               invoicetransactions,
               CASE
                 WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                      AND ( ccode = 'VAT'
                            AND ( chargetype IS NOT NULL
                                  AND ( chargetype != 'Address Correction'
                                         OR chargetype != 'BLANK'
                                         OR chargetype != 'Transportation' ) )
                            AND netcharges >= 0
                            AND taxablecharges >= 0
                            AND nontaxablecharges >= 0
                            AND discount >= 0 ) THEN netcharges
                 WHEN invoicetransactions IN ( 'R004_01', 'R005_01' )
                      AND ( ccode = 'VAT'
                            AND ( chargetype IS NOT NULL
                                  AND ( chargetype != 'Address Correction'
                                         OR chargetype != 'BLANK'
                                         OR chargetype != 'Transportation' ) )
                            AND netcharges >= 0
                            AND nontaxablecharges >= 0 ) THEN netcharges
                 ELSE vat
               END                 AS VAT,
               CASE
                 WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R005_01'
                      AND ( chargetype IS NOT NULL
                            AND ( chargetype != 'Address Correction'
                                   OR chargetype != 'BLANK'
                                   OR chargetype != 'Transportation' ) ) THEN
                 netcharges
                 ELSE 0
               END                 AS adjustments,
               CASE
                 WHEN invoicetransactions IN ( 'R003_01', 'R004_01' )
                      AND ( chargetype = 'Address Correction'
                             OR chargetype = 'BLANK'
                             OR chargetype = 'Transportation' ) THEN (
                 taxablecharges )
                 WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                      AND ( chargetype = 'Address Correction'
                             OR chargetype = 'BLANK'
                             OR chargetype = 'Transportation' ) THEN
                 transportationtaxable2total
                 ELSE 0
               END                 AS WorldwideShipments,
               CASE
                 WHEN invoicetransactions IN ( 'R003_01', 'R004_01' )
                      AND ( chargetype = 'Address Correction'
                             OR chargetype = 'BLANK'
                             OR chargetype = 'Transportation' ) THEN
                 ( discount )
                 WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R008_01'
                      AND ( chargetype = 'Address Correction'
                             OR chargetype = 'BLANK'
                             OR chargetype = 'Transportation' ) THEN
                 transportationdiscounttaxable2total
                 ELSE 0
               END                 AS Discount,
               CASE
                 WHEN invoicetransactions = 'R003_01'
                       OR invoicetransactions = 'R004_01' THEN netcharges
                 ELSE 0
               END                 AS OtherCharges,
               taxable2netchargestotal,
               transportationtaxable2total,
               transportationdiscounttaxable2total,
               amount
			   into #ChinaSummary2
        FROM   #ChinaSummary1
		SELECT invoicetransactions                               AS
       InvoiceTransactionsRule,
       invoiceno,
       Sum(adjustments)                                  AS Adjustments,
       Sum(worldwideshipments)                           AS WorldwideShipments,
       Sum(discount)                                     AS Discounts,
       Sum(othercharges)
       + Sum(taxable2netchargestotal) + ( Sum(discount) ) - ( Sum(
       worldwideshipments) )
                                                         AS OtherCharges,
       ( Sum(worldwideshipments) ) + (
       Sum(othercharges)
       + Sum(taxable2netchargestotal) +
       ( Sum(discount) ) - Sum(worldwideshipments) ) +
       Sum(adjustments) - Sum(discount)                  AS totalCharges,
       Sum(vat)                                          AS VAT,
       ( ( Sum(worldwideshipments) ) + (
         Sum(othercharges)
         + Sum(taxable2netchargestotal) +
           ( Sum(discount) ) - Sum(worldwideshipments)
                                       )
         + Sum(adjustments) - Sum(discount) ) + Sum(vat) AS totalAmountDue,
       CASE
         WHEN ( ( ( Sum(worldwideshipments) ) + (
                             Sum(othercharges)
                                                  + Sum(taxable2netchargestotal)
                             +
                                                             ( Sum(discount) ) -
                             Sum(worldwideshipments) ) + Sum(adjustments) - Sum(
                  discount) ) +
                       Sum(vat) ) != ( Sum(amount) / Count(amount) ) THEN 1
         ELSE 0
       END                                               AS IsMissCharges
FROM   #ChinaSummary2
GROUP  BY invoiceno,
          invoicetransactions
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CUSTOMER_PROFILES] ( @NoOfMonths INT )
AS
BEGIN
declare @time nvarchar(100)
	SET @time='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
	INSERT INTO [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message],[Level],[TimeStamp],[Exception],[LogEvent])
	VALUES ('Archival Customer Profiles Data ' + @time,'Information',GETUTCDATE(),null,@time)
BEGIN TRANSACTION
BEGIN TRY

	END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[DeleteInvoiceTableTempsCN]				
   @CountryCode VARCHAR(2)
AS	
BEGIN
        EXEC ALIPSdevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN'
		EXEC ALIPSdevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN' 
		EXEC ALIPSdevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN'
		EXEC ALIPSdevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN'
		BEGIN
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN1
		END
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 pc.[creationTime],
		 pc.recid	
		,pc.ccode	
		,pc.camount	
		,pc.publ_amt	
		,pc.inct_amt	
		,pc.chg_taxapl	
		,pc.acc_tier	
		,pc.acc_tax	
		,pc.exp_msg	
		,pc.info_ir	
		,pc.track_nr	
		,pc.duty_amt	
		,pc.cny_cd	
		,pc.slic_num INTO #PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN1 
		FROM [ALIPS].[Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN pc with(nolock)	
		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceCharges] c with(nolock) ON pc.recid = c.recid AND pc.ccode = c.ccode AND pc.info_ir = c.info_ir AND pc.track_nr = c.track_nr	
	    WHERE pc.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)	
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN1
		END	
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 p.CreationTime	
		,p.recid
		,p.bill_wt
		,p.wt_unt
		,p.s_length
		,p.s_width
		,p.s_height
		,p.unit_meas
		,p.ref_track
		,p.k_length
		,p.k_width
		,p.k_height
		,p.uom_cd
		,p.cny_cd
		,p.slic_num 
		INTO #PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN1 
		FROM [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN p with(nolock)
		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] d with(nolock) ON p.[recid] = d.[recid] AND p.cny_cd = d.cny_cd and p.ref_track = d.ref_track		
		WHERE d.recid is null AND d.ref_track is null AND p.Cny_cd = @CountryCode
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN1
		END	
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 s.CreationTime
		,s.recid
		,s.inv_no
		,s.inv_type
		,s.exp_dt
		,s.exp_date
		,s.gcc_num
		,s.track_no
		,s.track_11
		,s.csort
		,s.product
		,s.service
		,s.bill_term
		,s.zone
		,s.mvm_drc
		,s.package
		,s.actual_wt
		,s.bill_wt
		,s.wt_msg
		,s.ref_1
		,s.ref_2
		,s.shp_cont
		,s.ship_name
		,s.ship_city
		,s.ship_state
		,s.ship_postl
		,s.ship_ctry
		,s.cons_cont
		,s.cons_name
		,s.cons_city
		,s.cons_state
		,s.cons_postl
		,s.cons_ctry
		,s.dest_ctry
		,s.freight
		,s.discount
		,s.acc_201
		,s.acc_205
		,s.frt_rate
		,s.acc_adc
		,s.acc_chg
		,s.orig_inv
		,s.inv_total
		,s.desc_goods
		,s.decl_value
		,s.sur_ups
		,s.sur_brok
		,s.exchg_rate
		,s.exrate_cd
		,s.custom_no
		,s.reason_cd
		,s.acct_no
		,s.rqst_name
		,s.rqst_comp
		,s.rqst_phone
		,s.rqst_email
		,s.trd_cont
		,s.trd_name
		,s.trd_city
		,s.trd_state
		,s.trd_postal
		,s.trd_ctry
		,s.comments
		,s.trans_cd
		,s.chg_msg
		,s.tier_inct
		,s.minshp_prc
		,s.svc_supp
		,s.min_pkgwt
		,s.frt_taxapl
		,s.vartaxamt
		,s.ship_flr
		,s.ship_sui
		,s.ship_sta
		,s.cons_flr
		,s.cons_sui
		,s.cons_sta
		,s.ccard_cd
		,s.ccard_4dgt
		,s.payor
		,s.cny_cd
		,s.custom_clr
		,s.CUSTOM_EXCHG_RATE
		,s.[IA_CRD_TYP_CD]
		,s.[IA_CRD_NR]
		,s.[IA_CRD_ATH_NR]
		,s.[IA_CRD_EXP_DT]  
		,s.[slic_num] INTO #PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN1
		from [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN s with(nolock) 
		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceShipments] shipment with(nolock) on s.recid = shipment.recid AND s.cny_cd = shipment.cny_cd and s.inv_no = shipment.inv_no
		WHERE shipment.recid IS NULL AND shipment.cny_cd IS NULL AND shipment.inv_no is null AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN1
		END		
		SELECT DISTINCT
			 p.[creationTime]	
			,p.ac_no
			,p.inv_no
			,p.inv_dt
			,p.inv_date
			,p.due_date
			,p.period_dt
			,p.type_cd
			,p.bill_cont
			,p.bill_name
			,p.bill_state
			,p.bill_city
			,p.bill_postl
			,p.bill_ctry
			,p.pmt_term
			,p.ust_id
			,p.cop_coll
			,p.taxable
			,p.nontax
			,p.tax_amt
			,p.bill_amt
			,p.inv_curr
			,p.[filename]
			,p.cuser
			,p.cdate
			,p.last_print
			,p.no_printed
			,p.giro_prt
			,p.no_giro
			,p.out_queue
			,p.sort_fld
			,p.way_sort
			,p.disp_level
			,p.slic_num
			,p.cps_pln_nr
			,p.cps_pln_dt
			,p.cps_pln_na
			,p.taxtier
			,p.tax_pct
			,p.bill_flr
			,p.bill_sui
			,p.bill_sta
			,p.[processed]
			,p.[orig_type_cd]
			,p.os_amt
			,p.cny_cd
			,p.tax_var
			,p.batch_no
			,p.batch_dt
			,p.void
			,p.crdt_ind
			,p.cd_ind
			,p.BAFlag
			,p.close_dt
			,p.last_print_date
			,p.last_print_by
			,p.org_inv_date
			,p.TAX_ID
			,p.EXEMPTION_ID
			,p.[PROC_TM] INTO #PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN1
			FROM [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN p with(nolock)
			LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceInfos] i with(nolock) ON p.inv_no = i.inv_no AND p.cny_cd = i.cny_cd 
			WHERE i.inv_no IS NULL AND  p.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode	
		BEGIN
			EXEC ALIPS.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN'
			EXEC ALIPS.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN' 
			EXEC ALIPS.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN'
			EXEC ALIPS.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN'
		END
		BEGIN
		INSERT INTO [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN
		 (
			CreationTime,
			recid,
			ccode,
			camount,
			publ_amt,
			inct_amt,
			chg_taxapl,
			acc_tier,
			acc_tax,
			exp_msg,
			info_ir,
			track_nr,
			duty_amt,
			cny_cd,
			slic_num
		)
		SELECT
			[creationTime],
			recid,
			ccode,
			camount,
			publ_amt,
			inct_amt,
			chg_taxapl,
			acc_tier,
			acc_tax,
			exp_msg,
			info_ir,
			track_nr,
			duty_amt,
			cny_cd,
			slic_num
		 FROM #PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN1
		INSERT INTO [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN(
		CreationTime
		,recid
		,bill_wt
		,wt_unt
		,s_length
		,s_width
		,s_height
		,unit_meas
		,ref_track
		,k_length
		,k_width
		,k_height
		,uom_cd
		,cny_cd
		,slic_num 
		)
		SELECT DISTINCT 
			 CreationTime	
			,recid
			,bill_wt
			,wt_unt
			,s_length
			,s_width
			,s_height
			,unit_meas
			,ref_track
			,k_length
			,k_width
			,k_height
			,uom_cd
			,cny_cd
			,slic_num 
		 FROM #PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN1
		INSERT INTO [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN (
			 CreationTime
			,recid
			,inv_no
			,inv_type
			,exp_dt
			,exp_date
			,gcc_num
			,track_no
			,track_11
			,csort
			,product
			,service
			,bill_term
			,zone
			,mvm_drc
			,package
			,actual_wt
			,bill_wt
			,wt_msg
			,ref_1
			,ref_2
			,shp_cont
			,ship_name
			,ship_city
			,ship_state

			,cons_cont

			,cons_city

			,IA_CRD_TYP_CD
			,IA_CRD_NR
			,IA_CRD_ATH_NR
			,IA_CRD_EXP_DT	
			,slic_num
			)
			SELECT DISTINCT 
				 CreationTime

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[DeleteInvoiceTableTempsJP]				
   @CountryCode VARCHAR(2)
AS	
BEGIN
            EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceChargeTempsJP'
			EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsJP' 
			EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentTempsJP'
			EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceInfoTempsJP'
		BEGIN
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceChargeTempsJP1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceChargeTempsJP1
		END
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 pc.[creationTime],
		 pc.recid	
		,pc.ccode	
		,pc.camount	
		,pc.publ_amt	
		,pc.inct_amt	
		,pc.chg_taxapl	
		,pc.acc_tier	
		,pc.acc_tax	
		,pc.exp_msg	
		,pc.info_ir	
		,pc.track_nr	
		,pc.duty_amt	
		,pc.cny_cd	
		,pc.slic_num INTO #PullInvoiceChargeTempsJP1 
		FROM [ALIPS].[Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsJP pc with(nolock)	
		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceCharges] c with(nolock) ON pc.recid = c.recid AND pc.ccode = c.ccode AND pc.info_ir = c.info_ir AND pc.track_nr = c.track_nr	
	    WHERE pc.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)	
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsJP1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsJP1
		END	
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 p.CreationTime	
		,p.recid
		,p.bill_wt
		,p.wt_unt
		,p.s_length
		,p.s_width
		,p.s_height
		,p.unit_meas
		,p.ref_track
		,p.k_length
		,p.k_width
		,p.k_height
		,p.uom_cd
		,p.cny_cd
		,p.slic_num 
		INTO #PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsJP1 
		FROM [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsJP p with(nolock)
		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] d with(nolock) ON p.[recid] = d.[recid] AND p.cny_cd = d.cny_cd and p.ref_track = d.ref_track		
		WHERE d.recid is null AND d.ref_track is null AND p.Cny_cd = @CountryCode
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceShipmentTempsJP1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceShipmentTempsJP1
		END	
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 s.CreationTime
		,s.recid
		,s.inv_no
		,s.inv_type
		,s.exp_dt
		,s.exp_date
		,s.gcc_num
		,s.track_no
		,s.track_11
		,s.csort
		,s.product
		,s.service
		,s.bill_term
		,s.zone
		,s.mvm_drc

		,s.actual_wt

		,s.payor
		,s.cny_cd
		,s.custom_clr
		,s.CUSTOM_EXCHG_RATE
		,s.[IA_CRD_TYP_CD]
		,s.[IA_CRD_NR]
		,s.[IA_CRD_ATH_NR]
		,s.[IA_CRD_EXP_DT]  
		,s.[slic_num] INTO #PullInvoiceShipmentTempsJP1
		from [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsJP s with(nolock) 
		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceShipments] shipment with(nolock) on s.recid = shipment.recid AND s.cny_cd = shipment.cny_cd and s.inv_no = shipment.inv_no
		WHERE shipment.recid IS NULL AND shipment.cny_cd IS NULL AND shipment.inv_no is null AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceInfoTempsJP1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceInfoTempsJP1
		END		
		SELECT DISTINCT
			 p.[creationTime]	
			,p.ac_no

			,p.EXEMPTION_ID
			,p.[PROC_TM] INTO #PullInvoiceInfoTempsJP1
			FROM [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceInfoTempsJP p with(nolock)
			LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceInfos] i with(nolock) ON p.inv_no = i.inv_no AND p.cny_cd = i.cny_cd 
			WHERE i.inv_no IS NULL AND  p.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode	
		BEGIN
			EXEC ALIPS.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceChargeTempsJP'

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[DeleteInvoiceTableTempsKR]				
   @CountryCode VARCHAR(2)
AS	
BEGIN
        EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceChargeTempsKR'
		EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsKR' 
		EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentTempsKR'
		EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceInfoTempsKR'
		BEGIN
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceChargeTempsKR1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceChargeTempsKR1
		END
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 pc.[creationTime],
		 pc.recid	
		,pc.ccode	
		,pc.camount	
		,pc.publ_amt	
		,pc.inct_amt	
		,pc.chg_taxapl	
		,pc.acc_tier	
		,pc.acc_tax	
		,pc.exp_msg	
		,pc.info_ir	
		,pc.track_nr	
		,pc.duty_amt	
		,pc.cny_cd	
		,pc.slic_num INTO #PullInvoiceChargeTempsKR1 
		FROM [ALIPS].[Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsKR pc with(nolock)	
		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceCharges] c with(nolock) ON pc.recid = c.recid AND pc.ccode = c.ccode AND pc.info_ir = c.info_ir AND pc.track_nr = c.track_nr	
	    WHERE pc.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)	
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsKR1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsKR1
		END	
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 p.CreationTime	
		,p.recid
		,p.bill_wt
		,p.wt_unt
		,p.s_length
		,p.s_width
		,p.s_height
		,p.unit_meas
		,p.ref_track
		,p.k_length
		,p.k_width
		,p.k_height
		,p.uom_cd
		,p.cny_cd
		,p.slic_num 
		INTO #PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsKR1 
		FROM [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsKR p with(nolock)
		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] d with(nolock) ON p.[recid] = d.[recid] AND p.cny_cd = d.cny_cd and p.ref_track = d.ref_track		
		WHERE d.recid is null AND d.ref_track is null AND p.Cny_cd = @CountryCode
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceShipmentTempsKR1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceShipmentTempsKR1
		END	
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 s.CreationTime
		,s.recid
		,s.inv_no
		,s.inv_type
		,s.exp_dt
		,s.exp_date
		,s.gcc_num
		,s.track_no
		,s.track_11
		,s.csort
		,s.product
		,s.service
		,s.bill_term
		,s.zone
		,s.mvm_drc
		,s.package
		,s.actual_wt
		,s.bill_wt
		,s.wt_msg
		,s.ref_1
		,s.ref_2
		,s.shp_cont
		,s.ship_name
		,s.ship_city
		,s.ship_state
		,s.ship_postl
		,s.ship_ctry
		,s.cons_cont
		,s.cons_name
		,s.cons_city
		,s.cons_state
		,s.cons_postl
		,s.cons_ctry
		,s.dest_ctry
		,s.freight
		,s.discount
		,s.acc_201
		,s.acc_205
		,s.frt_rate
		,s.acc_adc
		,s.acc_chg
		,s.orig_inv
		,s.inv_total

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[DeleteInvoiceTableTempsTW]				
   @CountryCode VARCHAR(2)
AS	
BEGIN
       EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceChargeTempsTW'
	   EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsTW' 
	   EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentTempsTW'
	   EXEC ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceInfoTempsTW'
		BEGIN
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceChargeTempsTW1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceChargeTempsTW1
		END
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 pc.[creationTime],
		 pc.recid	
		,pc.ccode	
		,pc.camount	
		,pc.publ_amt	
		,pc.inct_amt	
		,pc.chg_taxapl	
		,pc.acc_tier	
		,pc.acc_tax	
		,pc.exp_msg	
		,pc.info_ir	
		,pc.track_nr	
		,pc.duty_amt	
		,pc.cny_cd	
		,pc.slic_num INTO #PullInvoiceChargeTempsTW1 
		FROM [ALIPS].[Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsTW pc with(nolock)	
		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceCharges] c with(nolock) ON pc.recid = c.recid AND pc.ccode = c.ccode AND pc.info_ir = c.info_ir AND pc.track_nr = c.track_nr	
	    WHERE pc.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)	
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsTW1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsTW1
		END	
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 p.CreationTime	
		,p.recid
		,p.bill_wt
		,p.wt_unt
		,p.s_length
		,p.s_width
		,p.s_height
		,p.unit_meas
		,p.ref_track
		,p.k_length
		,p.k_width
		,p.k_height
		,p.uom_cd
		,p.cny_cd
		,p.slic_num 
		INTO #PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsTW1 
		FROM [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsTW p with(nolock)
		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] d with(nolock) ON p.[recid] = d.[recid] AND p.cny_cd = d.cny_cd and p.ref_track = d.ref_track		
		WHERE d.recid is null AND d.ref_track is null AND p.Cny_cd = @CountryCode
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceShipmentTempsTW1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceShipmentTempsTW1
		END	
		SELECT DISTINCT 
		 s.CreationTime
		,s.recid
		,s.inv_no
		,s.inv_type
		,s.exp_dt
		,s.exp_date
		,s.gcc_num
		,s.track_no
		,s.track_11
		,s.csort
		,s.product
		,s.service
		,s.bill_term
		,s.zone
		,s.mvm_drc
		,s.package
		,s.actual_wt
		,s.bill_wt
		,s.wt_msg
		,s.ref_1
		,s.ref_2
		,s.shp_cont
		,s.ship_name
		,s.ship_city
		,s.ship_state
		,s.ship_postl
		,s.ship_ctry
		,s.cons_cont
		,s.cons_name
		,s.cons_city
		,s.cons_state
		,s.cons_postl
		,s.cons_ctry
		,s.dest_ctry
		,s.freight
		,s.discount
		,s.acc_201
		,s.acc_205
		,s.frt_rate
		,s.acc_adc
		,s.acc_chg
		,s.orig_inv
		,s.inv_total
		,s.desc_goods
		,s.decl_value
		,s.sur_ups
		,s.sur_brok
		,s.exchg_rate
		,s.exrate_cd
		,s.custom_no
		,s.reason_cd
		,s.acct_no
		,s.rqst_name
		,s.rqst_comp
		,s.rqst_phone
		,s.rqst_email
		,s.trd_cont
		,s.trd_name
		,s.trd_city
		,s.trd_state
		,s.trd_postal
		,s.trd_ctry
		,s.comments
		,s.trans_cd
		,s.chg_msg
		,s.tier_inct
		,s.minshp_prc
		,s.svc_supp
		,s.min_pkgwt
		,s.frt_taxapl
		,s.vartaxamt

		,s.cons_flr

		,s.[IA_CRD_TYP_CD]
		,s.[IA_CRD_NR]

		,s.[slic_num] INTO #PullInvoiceShipmentTempsTW1

		LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceShipments] shipment with(nolock) on s.recid = shipment.recid AND s.cny_cd = shipment.cny_cd and s.inv_no = shipment.inv_no
		WHERE shipment.recid IS NULL AND shipment.cny_cd IS NULL AND shipment.inv_no is null AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode
		IF (OBJECT_ID(N'TEMPDB..#PullInvoiceInfoTempsTW1',N'U') IS NOT NULL)

			DROP TABLE #PullInvoiceInfoTempsTW1
		END

			 p.[creationTime]

			,p.bill_flr
			,p.bill_sui
			,p.bill_sta
			,p.[processed]
			,p.[orig_type_cd]
			,p.os_amt
			,p.cny_cd
			,p.tax_var
			,p.batch_no
			,p.batch_dt
			,p.void
			,p.crdt_ind
			,p.cd_ind
			,p.BAFlag
			,p.close_dt
			,p.last_print_date
			,p.last_print_by
			,p.org_inv_date
			,p.TAX_ID
			,p.EXEMPTION_ID
			,p.[PROC_TM] INTO #PullInvoiceInfoTempsTW1
			FROM [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceInfoTempsTW p with(nolock)
			LEFT JOIN [ALIPS].[dbo].[InvoiceInfos] i with(nolock) ON p.inv_no = i.inv_no AND p.cny_cd = i.cny_cd 
			WHERE i.inv_no IS NULL AND  p.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode	
		BEGIN
			EXEC ALIPS.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceChargeTempsTW'
			EXEC ALIPS.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsTW' 
			EXEC ALIPS.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceShipmentTempsTW'
			EXEC ALIPS.[dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table] 'DBO','PullInvoiceInfoTempsTW'
		END
		BEGIN
		INSERT INTO [ALIPS].[dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsTW
		 (
			CreationTime,
			recid,
			ccode,
			camount,
			publ_amt,
			inct_amt,
			chg_taxapl,
			acc_tier,
			acc_tax,
			exp_msg,
			info_ir,
			track_nr,
			duty_amt,
			cny_cd,
			slic_num
		)
		SELECT
			[creationTime],
			recid,
			ccode,
			camount,
			publ_amt,
			inct_amt,
			chg_taxapl,
			acc_tier,
			acc_tax,
