CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetAllTaxInvoiceBySearching]
(
	@ac_no varchar(10),
	@inv_no varchar(12),
	@inv_dt_from datetime2(7),
	@inv_dt_to datetime2(7),
	@exclude_product ExcludedProduct readonly
)
AS
BEGIN
	CREATE TABLE #Invoices(
		Id int,
		inv_no varchar(12),
		ac_no varchar(10),
		inv_dt datetime2(7),
		bill_amt decimal(18,2),
		nontax decimal(18,2),
		taxable decimal(18,2),
		type_cd varchar(2)
	);
	CREATE NONCLUSTERED INDEX IX_Invoices_AccountNumber
	ON #Invoices ([ac_no])
	INCLUDE ([Id],[inv_no],[inv_dt],[nontax],[taxable],[type_cd])
	INSERT INTO #Invoices
	SELECT
	bi.Id,
	bi.inv_no,
	bi.ac_no,
	bi.inv_dt,
	bi.bill_amt,
	bi.nontax,
	bi.taxable,
	bi.type_cd
	FROM BilledInvoiceInfos bi with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles cp with(nolock)
		ON bi.ac_no = cp.AccountNumber
	JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments bs with(nolock)
		ON bi.inv_no = bs.inv_no
	WHERE (bi.IsMissCharges is null or bi.IsMissCharges = 0)
		  and (bi.type_cd <> '8' OR bs.sur_brok > 0) 
		  and bi.cny_cd = 'KR' 
		  and bi.type_cd not in ('4', '5', '9', '16', '24')
		  and (cp.IsOptOutVATInvoice is null or cp.IsOptOutVATInvoice = 0 or bs.product not in (select product from @exclude_product))
	GROUP BY 
	bi.Id,
	bi.inv_no,
	bi.ac_no,
	bi.inv_dt,
	bi.bill_amt,
	bi.nontax,
	bi.taxable,
	bi.type_cd
	SELECT
	ISNULL(ti.Id, bi.Id) AS Id,
	bi.inv_no AS InvoiceNumber,
	bi.ac_no AS AccountNumber,
	bi.nontax AS TotalNonTaxableCharges,
	bi.taxable AS TotalTaxableCharges,
	bi.type_cd AS TypeCode,
	ti.modification_code AS ModificationCode,
	ti.Status AS Status,
	ti.IsBlockRevised AS IsBlockRevised
	FROM #Invoices bi with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles c with(nolock)
		ON bi.ac_no = c.AccountNumber
	LEFT JOIN TaxInvoices ti with(nolock)
		ON bi.inv_no = ti.inv_no
	WHERE c.CustomerCountryCode = 'KR'
	  AND (@ac_no is null OR @ac_no = '' OR bi.ac_no LIKE '%' + @ac_no + '%')
	  AND (@inv_no is null OR @inv_no = '' OR bi.inv_no LIKE '%' + @inv_no + '%')
	  AND (@inv_dt_from is null OR bi.inv_dt >= @inv_dt_from)
	  AND (@inv_dt_to is null OR bi.inv_dt <= @inv_dt_to)
	DROP TABLE #Invoices
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetAllTaxInvoiceByUploading]
(
	@invoice_numbers InvoiceNumbers readonly,
	@exclude_product ExcludedProduct readonly
)
AS
BEGIN
	CREATE TABLE #Invoices(
		Id int,
		inv_no varchar(12),
		ac_no varchar(10),
		inv_dt datetime2(7),
		bill_amt decimal(18,2),
		nontax decimal(18,2),
		taxable decimal(18,2),
		type_cd varchar(2)
	);
	CREATE NONCLUSTERED INDEX IX_Invoices_AccountNumber
	ON #Invoices ([ac_no])
	INCLUDE ([Id],[inv_no],[inv_dt],[nontax],[taxable],[type_cd])
	INSERT INTO #Invoices
	SELECT
	bi.Id,
	bi.inv_no,
	bi.ac_no,
	bi.inv_dt,
	bi.bill_amt,
	bi.nontax,
	bi.taxable,
	bi.type_cd
	FROM BilledInvoiceInfos bi with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles cp with(nolock)
		ON bi.ac_no = cp.AccountNumber
	JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments bs with(nolock)
		ON bi.inv_no = bs.inv_no
	WHERE (bi.IsMissCharges is null or bi.IsMissCharges = 0)
		  and (bi.type_cd <> '8' OR bs.sur_brok > 0) 
		  and bi.cny_cd = 'KR' 
		  and bi.type_cd not in ('4', '5', '9', '16', '24')
		  and (cp.IsOptOutVATInvoice is null or cp.IsOptOutVATInvoice = 0 or bs.product not in (select product from @exclude_product))
		  and (bi.inv_no IN (SELECT invoiceNumber FROM @invoice_numbers))
	GROUP BY 
	bi.Id,
	bi.inv_no,
	bi.ac_no,
	bi.inv_dt,
	bi.bill_amt,
	bi.nontax,
	bi.taxable,
	bi.type_cd
	SELECT
	ISNULL(ti.Id, bi.Id) AS Id,
	bi.inv_no AS InvoiceNumber,
	bi.ac_no AS AccountNumber,
	bi.nontax AS TotalNonTaxableCharges,
	bi.taxable AS TotalTaxableCharges,
	bi.type_cd AS TypeCode,
	ti.modification_code AS ModificationCode,
	ISNULL(ti.Status, '') AS Status,
	ISNULL(ti.IsBlockRevised, 0) AS IsBlockRevised
	FROM #Invoices bi with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles c with(nolock)
		ON bi.ac_no = c.AccountNumber
	LEFT JOIN TaxInvoices ti with(nolock)
		ON bi.inv_no = ti.inv_no
	WHERE c.CustomerCountryCode = 'KR'
	DROP TABLE #Invoices
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetDateTimeUpdateSettings_v2]
	@countryCode VARCHAR(2),
	@LasteCdateTime DateTime,
	@LasteCdateTimeNew DateTime
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	IF(@countryCode='CN')
	BEGIN
	Select IsNULL((
	select top 1 cdate from (
	SELECT i.cdate
	FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles c with(nolock) on i.ac_no = c.accountNumber and i.cny_cd = c.customercountrycode AND CustomerCountryCode = @countryCode and  i.slic_num in (select Slic from UPSInformations where CountryCode = @countryCode)
	LEFT JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos b with(nolock) on b.inv_no = i.inv_no and b.cny_cd = i.cny_cd AND b.cny_cd = @countryCode
	JOIN (SELECT inv_no, cny_cd,SUM(inv_total)inv_total FROM InvoiceShipments with(nolock) WHERE cny_cd = @countryCode GROUP BY inv_no, cny_cd) s on s.inv_no = i.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
	WHERE b.inv_no is null and i.cny_cd = @countryCode AND i.cdate >= (Select Value from Settings WITH(NOLOCK) where Type = CONCAT('LatestTransformTime',@countryCode)) AND (i.bill_amt - (i.cop_coll + i.tax_var)) = inv_total
	UNION
	SELECT i.cdate
	FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
	LEFT JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos b with(nolock) on b.inv_no = i.inv_no and b.cny_cd = i.cny_cd AND b.cny_cd = @countryCode
	JOIN (SELECT inv_no, cny_cd,SUM(inv_total)inv_total FROM InvoiceShipments with(nolock) WHERE cny_cd = @countryCode GROUP BY inv_no, cny_cd) s on s.inv_no = i.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
	WHERE b.inv_no is null and i.cny_cd = @countryCode and i.ac_no NOT IN (SELECT AccountNumber FROM  CustomerProfiles WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE CustomerCountryCode=@countryCode)
	and ((i.ac_no like '9%' or i.ac_no like  '8%') OR i.type_cd in ('4','24','31')) AND i.cdate >= (Select Value from Settings WITH(NOLOCK) where Type = CONCAT('LatestTransformTime',@countryCode))
	AND (i.bill_amt - (i.cop_coll + i.tax_var)) = inv_total
	) as t order by cdate
	),@LasteCdateTimeNew)
	END
	ELSE IF(@countryCode='TW')
	BEGIN
	Select IsNULL((
	select top 1 cdate from (
	SELECT i.cdate
	FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles c with(nolock) on i.ac_no = c.accountNumber and i.cny_cd = c.customercountrycode AND CustomerCountryCode = @countryCode
	LEFT JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos b with(nolock) on b.inv_no = i.inv_no and b.cny_cd = i.cny_cd AND b.cny_cd = @countryCode
	JOIN (SELECT inv_no, cny_cd,SUM(inv_total) inv_total FROM InvoiceShipments with(nolock) WHERE cny_cd = @countryCode GROUP BY inv_no, cny_cd) s on s.inv_no = i.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
	WHERE b.inv_no is null and i.cny_cd = @countryCode AND i.cdate >= (Select Value from Settings WITH(NOLOCK) where Type = CONCAT('LatestTransformTime',@countryCode)) AND (i.bill_amt - (i.cop_coll + i.tax_var)) = inv_total
	AND ((I.tax_amt > 0  and i.type_cd not in (SELECT VALUE FROM STRING_SPLIT((select VALUE_UNIT from SysConfig WITH(NOLOCK) where [KEY]='Inv_Trnsfrm_TW'), ',')) AND ((i.GUI_NO IS NOT NULL AND I.GUI_NO !='')  OR (I.RCV_NO IS NOT NULL AND I.RCV_NO !=''))) OR (sign(I.tax_amt) = 0 or sign(I.tax_amt) = -1 )  OR i.type_cd in (SELECT VALUE FROM STRING_SPLIT((select VALUE_UNIT from SysConfig where [KEY]='Inv_Trnsfrm_TW'), ',')) OR (i.ac_no like '9%' or i.ac_no like  '8%')
	OR c.GUI_TYPE='Y')
	UNION
	SELECT i.cdate
	FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
	LEFT JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos b with(nolock) on b.inv_no = i.inv_no and b.cny_cd = i.cny_cd AND b.cny_cd = @countryCode
	JOIN (SELECT inv_no, cny_cd,SUM(inv_total) inv_total FROM InvoiceShipments with(nolock) WHERE cny_cd = @countryCode GROUP BY inv_no, cny_cd) s on s.inv_no = i.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
	WHERE b.inv_no is null and i.cny_cd = @countryCode
	and (i.ac_no like '9%' or i.ac_no like  '8%' OR i.type_cd in (SELECT VALUE FROM STRING_SPLIT((select VALUE_UNIT from SysConfig WITH(NOLOCK) where [KEY]='Inv_Trnsfrm_TW'), ','))) AND i.cdate >= (Select Value from Settings WITH(NOLOCK) where Type = CONCAT('LatestTransformTime',@countryCode))
	AND (i.bill_amt - (i.cop_coll + i.tax_var)) = inv_total
	) as t order by cdate
	),@LasteCdateTimeNew)
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
	Select IsNULL((
	select top 1 cdate from (
	SELECT i.cdate
	FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles c with(nolock) on i.ac_no = c.accountNumber and i.cny_cd = c.customercountrycode AND CustomerCountryCode = @countryCode
	LEFT JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos b with(nolock) on b.inv_no = i.inv_no and b.cny_cd = i.cny_cd AND b.cny_cd = @countryCode
	JOIN (SELECT inv_no, cny_cd,SUM(inv_total)inv_total FROM InvoiceShipments with(nolock) WHERE cny_cd = @countryCode GROUP BY inv_no, cny_cd) s on s.inv_no = i.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
	WHERE b.inv_no is null and i.cny_cd = @countryCode AND i.cdate >= (Select Value from Settings where Type = CONCAT('LatestTransformTime',@countryCode)) AND (i.bill_amt - (i.cop_coll + i.tax_var)) = inv_total
	UNION
	SELECT i.cdate
	FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
	LEFT JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos b with(nolock) on b.inv_no = i.inv_no and b.cny_cd = i.cny_cd AND b.cny_cd = @countryCode
	JOIN (SELECT inv_no, cny_cd,SUM(inv_total)inv_total FROM InvoiceShipments with(nolock) WHERE cny_cd = @countryCode GROUP BY inv_no, cny_cd) s on s.inv_no = i.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
	WHERE b.inv_no is null and i.cny_cd = @countryCode and i.ac_no NOT IN (SELECT AccountNumber FROM  CustomerProfiles WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE CustomerCountryCode=@countryCode)
	and ((i.ac_no like '9%' or i.ac_no like  '8%') OR i.type_cd in ('4','24','31')) AND i.cdate >= (Select Value from Settings WITH(NOLOCK) where Type = CONCAT('LatestTransformTime',@countryCode))
	AND (i.bill_amt - (i.cop_coll + i.tax_var)) = inv_total
	) as t order by cdate
	),@LasteCdateTimeNew)
	END
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetGeneralInformationForCover]
		@invoiceNo varchar(max),
		@countryCode varchar(2),
		@LanguagePreference Varchar(10)='Local'
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	DECLARE @TypeCode varchar(2)='99'
	SELECT @TypeCode=type_cd FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
	  JOIN MarketingMessages WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = MarketingMessages.TypeCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = MarketingMessages.Slic
	  WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = convert(varchar(12),@invoiceNo) AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = convert(varchar(2),@countryCode)
	IF(@TypeCode='99')
	BEGIN
		SELECT @TypeCode=type_cd FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		JOIN MarketingMessages WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = MarketingMessages.TypeCode AND CONCAT(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd,'ALL') = MarketingMessages.Slic
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = convert(varchar(12),@invoiceNo) AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = convert(varchar(2),@countryCode)
	END
	IF(@LANGUAGEPREFERENCE='ENGLISH')
	BEGIN
		SELECT
		BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt as InvoiceDate,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.ac_no as AccountNo,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no as InvoiceNo,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_var as TaxVar,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.due_date ='99991231' THEN null ELSE CONVERT(datetime, BilledInvoiceInfos.due_date, 120) END as PaymentDue,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1
		THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_name
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.AccountName END as AccountName,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1
		THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_city
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.Address1 END as Address1,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1
		THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_state
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.Address2 END as Address2,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1
		THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_cont
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.ExportedContactPerson END as ContactPerson,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1
		THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_Postl
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.PostalCode END as PostalCode,
		ISNULL(InvoiceTypeDescriptions.English, N'Invoice') as EnglishInvoiceTypeDescription,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.TollFreeNo ELSE UPSInformations.TollFreeNo END as TollFreeNo,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.Fax  ELSE UPSInformations.Fax END as Fax,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BankName ELSE UPSInformations.BankName END as BankName,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BankAccountNo ELSE UPSInformations.BankAccountNo END AS BankAccountNo,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.RemittanceCompanyName ELSE UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyName END as RemittanceCompanyName,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC = NULL THEN U.RemittanceCompanyAddress ELSE UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyAddress END as RemittanceCompanyAddress,
		CASE
		WHEN MarketingMessages.MarketingMessages IS NOT NULL THEN MarketingMessages.MarketingMessages
		WHEN Msgs.MarketingMessages IS NOT NULL THEN Msgs.MarketingMessages
		ELSE  CONCAT(LOWER(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd),CONCAT('ALL','/99'))
		END as MarketingMessages,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_curr as InvoiceCurrency,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt as Amount,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_amt		
		FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		LEFT JOIN CustomerProfiles WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceInfos.ac_no = CustomerProfiles.AccountNumber AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = CustomerProfiles.CustomerCountryCode
		LEFT JOIN InvoiceTypeDescriptions WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTypeDescriptions.InvoiceType
		LEFT JOIN UPSInformations WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = UPSInformations.CountryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = UPSInformations.Slic
		LEFT JOIN UPSInformations as U WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = U.CountryCode AND CONCAT(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd, 'ALL') =U.Slic
		LEFT JOIN MarketingMessages WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = MarketingMessages.CountryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = MarketingMessages.Slic AND MarketingMessages.TypeCode = @typeCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt BETWEEN MarketingMessages.EffectiveFrom AND MarketingMessages.EffectiveTo
		LEFT JOIN MarketingMessages as Msgs WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = Msgs.CountryCode AND CONCAT(LOWER(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd),'ALL') = Msgs.Slic
		AND Msgs.TypeCode = @typeCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt BETWEEN Msgs.EffectiveFrom AND Msgs.EffectiveTo
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = @invoiceNo AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
	IF(@countryCode='TW')
	BEGIN
		SELECT
		BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt as InvoiceDate,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.ac_no as AccountNo,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no as InvoiceNo,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_var as TaxVar,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.due_date ='99991231' THEN null ELSE CONVERT(datetime, BilledInvoiceInfos.due_date, 120) END as PaymentDue,
		CustomerProfiles.CustomerTaxId as CustomerTaxId,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.AccountName is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_name
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.AccountName END as AccountName,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_city
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.Address1 END as Address1,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_state
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.Address2 END as Address2,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_cont
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.ExportedContactPerson END as ContactPerson,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_Postl
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.PostalCode END as PostalCode,
		ISNULL(InvoiceTypeDescriptions.ChineseTraditional, N'帳單') as LocalInvoiceTypeDescription,
		ISNULL(InvoiceTypeDescriptions.English, N'Invoice') as EnglishInvoiceTypeDescription,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.TollFreeNo  else UPSInformations.TollFreeNo end as TollFreeNo,
		RIGHT(CustomerProfiles.AEPhoneNumber, 4) as Extension,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.Fax else UPSInformations.Fax end as Fax,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.ShipmentPickupTel else UPSInformations.ShipmentPickupTel end as ShipmentPickupTel,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.RemittanceCompanyName else UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyName end as RemittanceCompanyName,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BankName else UPSInformations.BankName end as BankName,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount IS NULL THEN '01810914482'
		WHEN SUBSTRING(CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount,1,5)='21000' THEN '66'+VirtualBankAccount
		WHEN SUBSTRING(CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount,1,5) !='21000' THEN '01810914482' ELSE CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount
		END AS ATMVirtualBankAccount,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount IS NULL THEN '01810914482'
		WHEN SUBSTRING(CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount,1,5) !='21000' THEN '01810914482' ELSE CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount
		END AS VirtualBankAccount,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BankAccountNo else UPSInformations.BankAccountNo end AS BankAccountNo,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BankCode else UPSInformations.BankCode end as BankCode,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.RemittanceCompanyEmail else UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyEmail end as RemittanceCompanyEmail,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.RemittanceCompanyAddress else UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyAddress end as RemittanceCompanyAddress,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.RemittanceCompanyPostal else UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyPostal end as RemittanceCompanyPostal,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BranchCode else UPSInformations.BranchCode end as BranchCode,
		CASE WHEN MarketingMessages.MarketingMessages IS NOT NULL THEN MarketingMessages.MarketingMessages
		WHEN Msgs.MarketingMessages IS NOT NULL THEN Msgs.MarketingMessages
		ELSE  CONCAT(LOWER(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd),CONCAT('ALL','/99')) END as MarketingMessages,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_curr as InvoiceCurrency,
		CASE WHEN isnull(BilledInvoiceInfos.GUI_NO,'')!='' THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.GUI_NO
		WHEN isnull(BilledInvoiceInfos.RCV_NO,'')!='' THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.RCV_NO
		ELSE CONCAT(GUINumberReferences.B2B, GUINumberReferences.B2C) END as GUINumber,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt as Amount,
		CustomerProfiles.CollectorName as CollectorName,
		CustomerProfiles.CollectorEmail as CollectorEmail,
		CustomerProfiles.CollectorPhoneNumber as CollectorPhoneNumber,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN N'帳單總額 Invoice Total'
		ELSE  N'本期費用總額 Total Amount Due' END as totalAmountText,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN N'帳單金额 Total Amount Due'
		ELSE '' END as amountDueText,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN ChargeCodeDescription.ChineseTraditional
		ELSE '' END as chargeCodeDesc,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_curr
		ELSE ''  END as ChgInvoiceCurrency,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 then (BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt-ISNULL(BilledInvoiceInfos.os_amt,0))
		ELSE 0 END as ChgAmt		
		FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		LEFT JOIN CustomerProfiles WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.ac_no = CustomerProfiles.AccountNumber AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = CustomerProfiles.CustomerCountryCode
		LEFT JOIN GUINumberReferences WITH(NOLOCK) ON GUINumberReferences.BilledInvoiceNumber = BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no AND GUINumberReferences.CountryCode = BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd
		LEFT JOIN InvoiceTypeDescriptions WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTypeDescriptions.InvoiceType
		LEFT JOIN ChargeCodeDescription WITH(NOLOCK) ON ChargeCodeDescription.CardCode = BilledInvoiceInfos.ccard_cd
		LEFT JOIN UPSInformations WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = UPSInformations.CountryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = UPSInformations.Slic
		LEFT JOIN UPSInformations as U WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = U.CountryCode AND CONCAT(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd, 'ALL') =U.Slic
		LEFT JOIN MarketingMessages WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = MarketingMessages.CountryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = MarketingMessages.Slic AND MarketingMessages.TypeCode = @typeCode 
		AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt BETWEEN MarketingMessages.EffectiveFrom AND MarketingMessages.EffectiveTo
		LEFT JOIN MarketingMessages as Msgs WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = Msgs.CountryCode AND CONCAT(LOWER(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd),'ALL') = Msgs.Slic
		AND Msgs.TypeCode = @typeCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt BETWEEN Msgs.EffectiveFrom AND Msgs.EffectiveTo
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = @invoiceNo AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode
	END
	ELSE IF(@countryCode='CN')
	BEGIN
		SELECT
		BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt as InvoiceDate,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.ac_no as AccountNo,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no as InvoiceNo,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_var as TaxVar,
		CustomerProfiles.AeName as AEName,
		CONVERT(datetime, BilledInvoiceInfos.period_dt, 120) as ShippingPeriod,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.due_date ='99991231' THEN null ELSE CONVERT(datetime, BilledInvoiceInfos.due_date, 120) END as PaymentDue,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.AccountName is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_name
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.AccountName END as AccountName,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_city
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.Address1 END as Address1,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_state
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.Address2 END as Address2,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_cont
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.ExportedContactPerson END as ContactPerson,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_Postl
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.PostalCode END as PostalCode,
		ISNULL(InvoiceTypeDescriptions.ChineseSimplified, N'帐单') as LocalInvoiceTypeDescription,
		ISNULL(InvoiceTypeDescriptions.English, N'Invoice') as EnglishInvoiceTypeDescription,
		UPSInformations.TollFreeNo as TollFreeNo,
		UPSInformations.InvoiceInquiryTel as Extension,
		UPSInformations.Fax as Fax,
		UPSInformations.ShipmentPickupTel as ShipmentPickupTel,
		UPSInformations.ChequeHeader as ChequeHeader,
		UPSInformations.BankName as BankName,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount!='' THEN CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount ELSE UPSInformations.BankAccountNo END AS BankAccountNo,
		UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyName as RemittanceCompanyName,
		UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyAddress as RemittanceCompanyAddress,
		UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyPostal as RemittanceCompanyPostal,
		MarketingMessages.MarketingMessages as MarketingMessages,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN ''
		ELSE BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_curr  END as InvoiceCurrency,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt as Amount,
		CustomerProfiles.CollectorName as CollectorName,
		CustomerProfiles.CollectorEmail as CollectorEmail,
		CustomerProfiles.CollectorPhoneNumber as CollectorPhoneNumber,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN N'帐单总额 Invoice Total' ELSE  N'本期费用总额 Total Amount Due' END as totalAmountText,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN N'帐单金额 Total Amount Due'
		ELSE '' END as amountDueText,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN 'Card Desc Text'
		ELSE '' END as chargeCodeDesc,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_curr
		ELSE ''  END as ChgInvoiceCurrency,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN (BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt-BilledInvoiceInfos.os_amt)
		ELSE 0 END as ChgAmt,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd as TypeCD,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num as SLIC
		FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		LEFT JOIN CustomerProfiles WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.ac_no = CustomerProfiles.AccountNumber AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = CustomerProfiles.CustomerCountryCode
		LEFT JOIN InvoiceTypeDescriptions WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTypeDescriptions.InvoiceType
		LEFT JOIN UPSInformations WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = UPSInformations.CountryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = UPSInformations.Slic
		LEFT JOIN MarketingMessages WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = MarketingMessages.CountryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = MarketingMessages.Slic AND MarketingMessages.TypeCode = @typeCode 
		AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt >= MarketingMessages.EffectiveFrom AND  MarketingMessages.EffectiveTo>=BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = @invoiceNo AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode
	END
	ELSE IF(@countryCode='JP')
	BEGIN
		SELECT
		CAST(period_dt as datetime2) as PeriodDate,
		CAST(inv_dt as datetime2) as InvoiceDate,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.ac_no as AccountNo,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no as InvoiceNo,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_var as TaxVar,
		case WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.due_date ='99991231' THEN null ELSE CONVERT(datetime, BilledInvoiceInfos.due_date, 120) END as PaymentDue,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.AccountName is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_name
		ELSE CASE WHEN ( LEN(CustomerProfiles.AccountName) !=0 AND LEN(REPLACE(CustomerProfiles.ExportedContactPerson,' ','')) =0 ) THEN CONCAT(CustomerProfiles.AccountName, ' ','  ',  N'  様')
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.AccountName END END as AccountName,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_city
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.Address1 END as Address1,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_state
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.Address2 END as Address2,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_cont
		WHEN lEN(REPLACE(CustomerProfiles.ExportedContactPerson,' ','')) !=0 THEN CONCAT(CustomerProfiles.ExportedContactPerson, ' ','  ',  N'  様')
		ELSE   CustomerProfiles.ExportedContactPerson END as ContactPerson,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_Postl
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.PostalCode END as PostalCode,
		ISNULL(InvoiceTypeDescriptions.Japanese, N'請求書') as LocalInvoiceTypeDescription,
		ISNULL(InvoiceTypeDescriptions.English, N'Invoice') as EnglishInvoiceTypeDescription,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.InvoiceInquiryTel else UPSInformations.InvoiceInquiryTel end as InvoiceInquiryTel,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.ShipmentPickupTel else UPSInformations.ShipmentPickupTel end as ShipmentPickupTel,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.ChequeHeader else UPSInformations.ChequeHeader end as ChequeHeader,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BankName else UPSInformations.BankName end as BankName,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BankCode else UPSInformations.BankCode end as BankCode,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BranchCode else UPSInformations.BranchCode end as BranchCode,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BranchName else UPSInformations.BranchName end as BranchName,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BankAccountNo else UPSInformations.BankAccountNo end AS BankAccountNo,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.RemittanceCompanyName else UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyName end as RemittanceCompanyName,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.RemittanceCompanyAddress else UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyAddress end as RemittanceCompanyAddress,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.RemittanceCompanyPostal else UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyPostal end as RemittanceCompanyPostal,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.RemittanceCompanyEmail else UPSInformations.RemittanceCompanyEmail end as RemittanceCompanyEmail,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.UPSTaxID else UPSInformations.UPSTaxID end as UPSTaxID,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.Fax else UPSInformations.Fax end as Fax,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BusinessType else UPSInformations.BusinessType end as BusinessType,
		CASE WHEN MarketingMessages.MarketingMessages IS NOT NULL THEN MarketingMessages.MarketingMessages
		WHEN Msgs.MarketingMessages IS NOT NULL THEN Msgs.MarketingMessages
		ELSE  CONCAT(LOWER(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd),CONCAT('ALL','/99')) END as MarketingMessages,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_curr as InvoiceCurrency,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt as Amount,
		CustomerProfiles.aeCode as AECode,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.UPSBillingContactPerson else UPSInformations.UPSBillingContactPerson end as UPSBillingContactPerson,
		case when (CustomerProfiles.ExportedSpecialInstruction is not null and len(CustomerProfiles.ExportedSpecialInstruction) !=0) then CONCAT(N'E - ',CustomerProfiles.ExportedSpecialInstruction) else '' end SplInsExport,
		case when (CustomerProfiles.ImportedSpecialInstruction is not null and len(CustomerProfiles.ImportedSpecialInstruction) !=0) then CONCAT(N'I - ',CustomerProfiles.ImportedSpecialInstruction) else '' end  SplIns,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd as TypeCD,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.taxable as Taxable,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.Nontax as Nontax		
		FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		LEFT JOIN CustomerProfiles WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.ac_no = CustomerProfiles.AccountNumber AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = CustomerProfiles.CustomerCountryCode
		LEFT JOIN InvoiceTypeDescriptions WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTypeDescriptions.InvoiceType
		LEFT JOIN UPSInformations WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = UPSInformations.CountryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = UPSInformations.Slic
		LEFT JOIN UPSInformations as U WITH(NOLOCK)ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = U.CountryCode AND CONCAT(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd, 'ALL') =U.Slic
		LEFT JOIN MarketingMessages WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = MarketingMessages.CountryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = MarketingMessages.Slic AND MarketingMessages.TypeCode = @typeCode 
		AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt BETWEEN MarketingMessages.EffectiveFrom AND MarketingMessages.EffectiveTo
		LEFT JOIN MarketingMessages as Msgs WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = Msgs.CountryCode AND CONCAT(LOWER(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd),'ALL') = Msgs.Slic
		AND Msgs.TypeCode = @typeCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt BETWEEN Msgs.EffectiveFrom AND Msgs.EffectiveTo
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = @invoiceNo AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode
	END
	ELSE IF(@countryCode='KR')
	BEGIN
		SELECT
		BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt as InvoiceDate,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.ac_no as AccountNo,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no as InvoiceNo,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_var as TaxVar,
		CONVERT(datetime, BilledInvoiceInfos.period_dt, 120) as ShippingPeriod,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.due_date ='99991231' THEN null ELSE CONVERT(datetime, BilledInvoiceInfos.due_date, 120) END as PaymentDue,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.AccountName is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_name
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.AccountName END as AccountName,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_city
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.Address1 END as Address1,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_state
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.Address2 END as Address2,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_cont
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.ExportedContactPerson END as ContactPerson,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.Address1 is null or CustomerProfiles.IsExempted=1 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.Bill_Postl
		ELSE CustomerProfiles.PostalCode END as PostalCode,
		ISNULL(InvoiceTypeDescriptions.Korean, N'청구서') as LocalInvoiceTypeDescription,
		ISNULL(InvoiceTypeDescriptions.English, N'Invoice') as EnglishInvoiceTypeDescription,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.InvoiceInquiryTel else UPSInformations.InvoiceInquiryTel end AS InvoiceInquiryTel,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.Fax else UPSInformations.Fax end as Fax,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BankName else CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BankName else UPSInformations.BankName end end as BankName,
		CASE WHEN CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount!='' THEN CustomerProfiles.VirtualBankAccount ELSE UPSInformations.BankAccountNo END AS BankAccountNo,
		CASE WHEN UPSInformations.SLIC IS NULL THEN U.BranchCode else UPSInformations.BranchCode end as BranchCode,
		CASE WHEN MarketingMessages.MarketingMessages IS NOT NULL THEN MarketingMessages.MarketingMessages
		WHEN Msgs.MarketingMessages IS NOT NULL THEN Msgs.MarketingMessages
		ELSE  CONCAT(LOWER(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd),CONCAT('ALL','/99')) END as MarketingMessages,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN ''
		ELSE BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_curr  END as InvoiceCurrency,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt as Amount,
		(BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_amt + BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_var) TaxAmount,
		CustomerProfiles.CollectorName as CollectorName,
		CustomerProfiles.CollectorEmail as CollectorEmail,
		CustomerProfiles.CollectorPhoneNumber as CollectorPhoneNumber,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN N'Invoice Total'
		ELSE  N'Total Amount Due' END as totalAmountText,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN N'Total Amount Due'
		ELSE '' END as amountDueText,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN 'Card Desc Text'
		ELSE '' END as chargeCodeDesc,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_curr
		ELSE ''  END as ChgInvoiceCurrency,
		CASE WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd=31 THEN (BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt-BilledInvoiceInfos.os_amt)
		ELSE 0 END as ChgAmt
		FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		LEFT JOIN CustomerProfiles WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.ac_no = CustomerProfiles.AccountNumber AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = CustomerProfiles.CustomerCountryCode
		LEFT JOIN InvoiceTypeDescriptions WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTypeDescriptions.InvoiceType
		LEFT JOIN UPSInformations WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = UPSInformations.CountryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = UPSInformations.Slic
		LEFT JOIN UPSInformations as U WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = U.CountryCode AND CONCAT(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd, 'ALL') = U.Slic
		LEFT JOIN MarketingMessages WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = MarketingMessages.CountryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.slic_num = MarketingMessages.Slic AND MarketingMessages.TypeCode = @typeCode 
		AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt BETWEEN MarketingMessages.EffectiveFrom AND MarketingMessages.EffectiveTo
		LEFT JOIN MarketingMessages as Msgs WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = Msgs.CountryCode AND CONCAT(LOWER(BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd),'ALL') = Msgs.Slic
		AND Msgs.TypeCode = @typeCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt BETWEEN Msgs.EffectiveFrom AND Msgs.EffectiveTo
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = @invoiceNo AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode
	END
	END
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetInvoiceCharges]
	@countryCode varchar(2),
	@SQLID Varchar(250),
	@inv_no Varchar(max)
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	Declare @LanguagePreference Varchar(20)
	select @LanguagePreference=R.DISPLAYTEXT from BilledInvoiceInfos BII WITH(NOLOCK)
	INNER JOIN CustomerProfiles CP WITH(NOLOCK) ON CP.ACCOUNTNUMBER=BII.AC_NO AND INV_NO=@inv_no
	INNER JOIN [REFERENCES] R WITH(NOLOCK) ON R.CODE=CP.INVOICELANGUAGEPREFERENCE AND [TYPE]=80
	IF(@SQLID='Details_Charges_ALL_Not_Rule')
		SELECT
		CASE WHEN @LanguagePreference='ENGLISH' then ISNULL(ChargeCodes.EnglishDescription,InvoiceCharges.CCODE)
		else ISNULL(ChargeCodes.LocalDescription,InvoiceCharges.CCODE) end as ChargeType,
		InvoiceCharges.publ_amt as TaxableCharges,
		InvoiceCharges.inct_amt as Discount,
		InvoiceCharges.camount as NetCharges,
		InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl as ChargeTaxapl,
		InvoiceCharges.recid as RecId,
		InvoiceCharges.ccode as ChargeCodes,
		CASE WHEN (BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type='EFD' or BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type='IFD') THEN 'Non'
		ELSE ChargeCodesVat.vat END as VAT,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt as VarTaxAmt
		FROM InvoiceCharges WITH(NOLOCK)
		INNER JOIN ChargeCodes WITH(NOLOCK) ON ChargeCodes.CountryCode = InvoiceCharges.cny_cd  AND InvoiceCharges.ccode = ChargeCodes.ChargeCodes
		LEFT JOIN ChargeCodesVat WITH(NOLOCK) ON ChargeCodesVat.ChargeCode = ChargeCodes.ChargeCodes and ChargeCodesVat.CountryCode = ChargeCodes.CountryCode
		JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments WITH(NOLOCK) on BilledInvoiceShipments.recid =  InvoiceCharges.recid and BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd = InvoiceCharges.cny_cd
		WHERE InvoiceCharges.cny_cd = @countryCode AND BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_no in (select value from string_split(@inv_no,','))
		AND BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_total<>0
	ELSE IF(@SQLID='Details_Charges_ALL_JP')
		SELECT
		CASE WHEN @LanguagePreference='ENGLISH' then ISNULL(ChargeCodes.EnglishDescription,InvoiceCharges.CCODE)
		else ISNULL(ChargeCodes.LocalDescription,InvoiceCharges.CCODE) end as ChargeType,
		CASE WHEN InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '') THEN 0 ELSE InvoiceCharges.publ_amt END as NonTaxableCharges,
		CASE WHEN InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '') THEN InvoiceCharges.publ_amt ELSE 0 END as TaxableCharges,
		CASE WHEN InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '') THEN 0 ELSE InvoiceCharges.inct_amt END as Discount,
		CASE WHEN InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '') THEN InvoiceCharges.inct_amt ELSE 0 END as TaxDiscount,
		InvoiceCharges.camount as NetCharges,
		InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl as ChargeTaxapl,
		InvoiceCharges.recid as RecId,
		BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no as InvoiceNumber,
		InvoiceCharges.ccode as ChargeCodes
		FROM InvoiceCharges WITH(NOLOCK)
		LEFT JOIN ChargeCodes WITH(NOLOCK) ON InvoiceCharges.ccode = ChargeCodes.ChargeCodes AND InvoiceCharges.cny_cd = ChargeCodes.CountryCode
		JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceShipments.recid = InvoiceCharges.recid and BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd = InvoiceCharges.cny_cd
		JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_no AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd
		LEFT JOIN InvoiceTransactions WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTransactions.InvoiceType AND BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type = InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode  AND billedinvoiceinfos.inv_no in (select value from string_split(@inv_no,','))
		AND BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_total<>0
	ELSE IF(@SQLID='Details_Charges_ALL')
	  SELECT
	  CASE WHEN @LanguagePreference='ENGLISH' then ISNULL(ChargeCodes.EnglishDescription,InvoiceCharges.CCODE)else ISNULL(ChargeCodes.LocalDescription,InvoiceCharges.CCODE) end as ChargeType,
	  CASE WHEN InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '') THEN 0 ELSE InvoiceCharges.publ_amt END as NonTaxableCharges,
	  CASE WHEN InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '') THEN InvoiceCharges.publ_amt ELSE 0 END as TaxableCharges,
	  InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl as ChargeTaxapl,
	  InvoiceCharges.inct_amt as Discount,
	  InvoiceCharges.camount as NetCharges,
	  InvoiceCharges.recid as RecId,
	  BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no as InvoiceNumber,
	  InvoiceCharges.ccode as ChargeCodes
	  FROM InvoiceCharges WITH(NOLOCK)
	  LEFT JOIN ChargeCodes WITH(NOLOCK) ON InvoiceCharges.ccode = ChargeCodes.ChargeCodes AND InvoiceCharges.cny_cd = ChargeCodes.CountryCode
	  JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceShipments.recid = InvoiceCharges.recid and BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd = InvoiceCharges.cny_cd
	  JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_no AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd
	  LEFT JOIN InvoiceTransactions WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTransactions.InvoiceType AND BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type = InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType
	  WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode AND billedinvoiceinfos.inv_no in (select value from string_split(@inv_no,','))
	  AND BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_total<>0
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetInvoiceData]
AS
BEGIN
    SELECT MAX(cdate) AS PLatestdate, cny_cd AS PCountry  
    FROM InvoiceInfos 
    GROUP BY cny_cd
    SELECT MAX(cdate) AS TLatestdate, cny_cd AS TCountry  
    FROM BilledInvoiceInfos 
    GROUP BY cny_cd
    SELECT MAX(cdate) AS TLatestdate, cny_cd AS TCountry  
    FROM BilledInvoiceInfos 
    WHERE IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 1 
    GROUP BY cny_cd
    SELECT TOP 10 inv_no, status 
    FROM TaxInvoices 
    ORDER BY id DESC
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetInvoiceInfosForTransform]
@CountryCode Varchar(2),
@CDATE DateTime=NULL,
@Type Varchar(20)=NULL
AS
BEGIN
      SET NOCOUNT ON;
	  IF(ISNULL(@Type,'')='Consolidated')
	  BEGIN
		SELECT
			InvoiceInfos.CreationTime AS CreationTime,
            InvoiceInfos.CreationUser AS CreationUser,
            InvoiceInfos.LastModificationTime AS LastModificationTime,
            InvoiceInfos.LastModificationUser AS LastModificationUser,
            InvoiceInfos.DeletionTime AS DeletionTime,
            InvoiceInfos.IsDeleted AS IsDeleted,
            InvoiceInfos.DeletionUser AS DeletionUser,
            InvoiceInfos.ac_no AS AccountNumber,
            InvoiceInfos.inv_no AS InvoiceNumber,
            InvoiceInfos.inv_dt AS InvoiceDate,
            InvoiceInfos.inv_date AS InvoiceDateString,
            InvoiceInfos.due_date AS InvoiceDueDate,
            InvoiceInfos.period_dt AS PeriodEndingDate,

            InvoiceInfos.bill_postl AS BillPostal,
            InvoiceInfos.bill_ctry AS BillCountry,
            InvoiceInfos.pmt_term AS PaymentTerms,
            InvoiceInfos.ust_id AS CustomerTaxID,
            InvoiceInfos.cop_coll AS AmountCollected,
            InvoiceInfos.taxable AS TotalTaxableCharges,
            InvoiceInfos.nontax AS TotalNonTaxableCharges,
            InvoiceInfos.tax_amt AS TotalTaxAmount,
            InvoiceInfos.bill_amt AS BillAmount,
            InvoiceInfos.inv_curr AS InvoiceCurrencyCode,
            InvoiceInfos.filename AS Filename,
            InvoiceInfos.cuser AS UploadUser,
            InvoiceInfos.cdate AS UploadDate,
            InvoiceInfos.last_print AS LastPrint,
            InvoiceInfos.no_printed AS NoPrinted,
            InvoiceInfos.giro_prt AS GiroPrint,
            InvoiceInfos.no_giro AS NoGiroPrint,
            InvoiceInfos.out_queue AS OutputQueue,
            InvoiceInfos.sort_fld AS SortFieldValue,
            InvoiceInfos.way_sort AS WaybillSorting,
            InvoiceInfos.disp_level AS DispLevel,
            InvoiceInfos.slic_num AS SlicNumber,
            InvoiceInfos.cps_pln_nr AS ConsolidatedPlanNumber,
            InvoiceInfos.cps_pln_dt AS ConsolidatedPlanDate,
            InvoiceInfos.cps_pln_na AS ConsolidatedPlanBillName,
            InvoiceInfos.taxtier AS TierTaxAmount,
            InvoiceInfos.tax_pct AS TaxPct,
            InvoiceInfos.bill_flr AS BillAddressFloor,
            InvoiceInfos.bill_sui AS BillAddressSuite,
            InvoiceInfos.bill_sta AS BillStateCode,
            InvoiceInfos.processed AS Processed,
            InvoiceInfos.orig_type_cd AS OriginalTypeCode,
            InvoiceInfos.os_amt AS OSAmount,
            InvoiceInfos.cny_cd AS CountryCode,
            InvoiceInfos.tax_var AS TaxVariance,
            InvoiceInfos.batch_no AS BatchNumber,
            InvoiceInfos.batch_dt AS BatchDate,
            InvoiceInfos.void AS InvoiceVoid,
            InvoiceInfos.crdt_ind AS Crdt_ind,
            InvoiceInfos.cd_ind AS Cd_ind,
            InvoiceInfos.BAFlag AS BAFlag,
            InvoiceInfos.close_dt AS Close_dt,
            InvoiceInfos.last_print_date AS LastPrintDate,
            InvoiceInfos.last_print_by AS LastPrintBy,
            InvoiceInfos.org_inv_date AS OriginalInvoiceDate,
            InvoiceInfos.TAX_ID AS TAX_ID,
            InvoiceInfos.EXEMPTION_ID AS EXEMPTION_ID,
            InvoiceInfos.PROC_TM AS PROC_TM,
            InvoiceInfos.IsBilled AS IsBilled,
            InvoiceInfos.ccard_cd as ccard_cd,
            InvoiceInfos.GUI_NO AS GUI_NO,
            InvoiceInfos.RCV_NO AS RCV_NO
		FROM InvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		JOIN CustomerProfiles WITH(NOLOCK) ON InvoiceInfos.ac_no = CustomerProfiles.AccountNumber AND  InvoiceInfos.cny_cd = CustomerProfiles.customercountrycode
		WHERE InvoiceInfos.cdate = cast(@cdate as datetime) AND CustomerProfiles.CustomerCountryCode = @countryCode
		AND (CustomerProfiles.HasConsolidatedInvoice = 1 and CustomerProfiles.ConsolidatedRule in ('20,40','30,40'))
		AND InvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode 
		AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM BilledInvoiceInfos BII WHERE BII.INV_NO=INVOICEINFOS.INV_NO AND InvoiceInfos.CNY_CD=BII.CNY_CD)
		AND InvoiceInfos.inv_dt BETWEEN CustomerProfiles.CombineDateFrom AND CustomerProfiles.CombineDateTo AND InvoiceInfos.type_cd NOT IN ('2', '17')
	  END
	  ELSE
	  BEGIN
	  SET @CDATE =(SELECT Value FROM SETTINGS WHERE TYPE='LATESTTRANSFORMTIME'+@CountryCode)
		SELECT I.* INTO #transforminvoices FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
		JOIN InvoiceShipmentGroupingData s WITH(NOLOCK) on s.inv_no = i.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
		WHERE i.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND i.cdate >= cast(@cdate as Datetime) AND (i.bill_amt - (i.cop_coll + i.tax_var)) = inv_total
		AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM BilledInvoiceInfos BII WHERE BII.INV_NO=I.INV_NO AND I.CNY_CD=BII.CNY_CD)
      IF(@CountryCode='CN')
      BEGIN
			SELECT TOP 6000 * FROM (

            i.CreationTime AS CreationTime,

            i.LastModificationUser AS LastModificationUser,

            i.bill_postl AS BillPostal,

            i.pmt_term AS PaymentTerms,
            i.ust_id AS CustomerTaxID,
            i.cop_coll AS AmountCollected,
            i.taxable AS TotalTaxableCharges,

            i.tax_amt AS TotalTaxAmount,
            i.bill_amt AS BillAmount,
            i.inv_curr AS InvoiceCurrencyCode,
            i.filename AS Filename,
            i.cuser AS UploadUser,
            i.cdate AS UploadDate,

            i.no_printed AS NoPrinted,
            i.giro_prt AS GiroPrint,
            i.no_giro AS NoGiroPrint,
            i.out_queue AS OutputQueue,
            i.sort_fld AS SortFieldValue,
            i.way_sort AS WaybillSorting,
            i.disp_level AS DispLevel,
            i.slic_num AS SlicNumber,
            i.cps_pln_nr AS ConsolidatedPlanNumber,
            i.cps_pln_dt AS ConsolidatedPlanDate,
            i.cps_pln_na AS ConsolidatedPlanBillName,
            i.taxtier AS TierTaxAmount,
            i.tax_pct AS TaxPct,
            i.bill_flr AS BillAddressFloor,
            i.bill_sui AS BillAddressSuite,
            i.bill_sta AS BillStateCode,
            i.processed AS Processed,
            i.orig_type_cd AS OriginalTypeCode,
            i.os_amt AS OSAmount,
            i.cny_cd AS CountryCode,
            i.tax_var AS TaxVariance,
            i.batch_no AS BatchNumber,
            i.batch_dt AS BatchDate,
            i.void AS InvoiceVoid,
            i.crdt_ind AS Crdt_ind,
            i.cd_ind AS Cd_ind,
            i.BAFlag AS BAFlag,
            i.close_dt AS Close_dt,
            i.last_print_date AS LastPrintDate,
            i.last_print_by AS LastPrintBy,
            i.org_inv_date AS OriginalInvoiceDate,
            i.TAX_ID AS TAX_ID,
            i.EXEMPTION_ID AS EXEMPTION_ID,
            i.PROC_TM AS PROC_TM,
            i.IsBilled AS IsBilled,
            i.ccard_cd as ccard_cd,
            i.GUI_NO AS GUI_NO,
            I.RCV_NO AS RCV_NO
            FROM #transforminvoices i with(nolock)
            JOIN CustomerProfiles c with(nolock) on i.ac_no = c.accountNumber and i.cny_cd = c.customercountrycode
			and  i.slic_num in (select Slic from UPSInformations WITH(NOLOCK) where CountryCode = @CountryCode)
            UNION
            SELECT
            i.CreationTime AS CreationTime,
            i.CreationUser AS CreationUser,
            i.LastModificationTime AS LastModificationTime,
            i.LastModificationUser AS LastModificationUser,
            i.DeletionTime AS DeletionTime,
            i.IsDeleted AS IsDeleted,
            i.DeletionUser AS DeletionUser,
            i.ac_no AS AccountNumber,
            i.inv_no AS InvoiceNumber,
            i.inv_dt AS InvoiceDate,
            i.inv_date AS InvoiceDateString,
            i.due_date AS InvoiceDueDate,
            i.period_dt AS PeriodEndingDate,
            i.type_cd AS TypeCode,
            i.bill_cont AS BillContactName,
            i.bill_name AS BillName,
            i.bill_state AS BillState,
            i.bill_city AS BillCity,
            i.bill_postl AS BillPostal,
            i.bill_ctry AS BillCountry,
            i.pmt_term AS PaymentTerms,
            i.ust_id AS CustomerTaxID,
            i.cop_coll AS AmountCollected,
            i.taxable AS TotalTaxableCharges,
            i.nontax AS TotalNonTaxableCharges,
            i.tax_amt AS TotalTaxAmount,
            i.bill_amt AS BillAmount,
            i.inv_curr AS InvoiceCurrencyCode,
            i.filename AS Filename,
            i.cuser AS UploadUser,
            i.cdate AS UploadDate,
            i.last_print AS LastPrint,
            i.no_printed AS NoPrinted,
            i.giro_prt AS GiroPrint,
            i.no_giro AS NoGiroPrint,
            i.out_queue AS OutputQueue,
            i.sort_fld AS SortFieldValue,
            i.way_sort AS WaybillSorting,
            i.disp_level AS DispLevel,
            i.slic_num AS SlicNumber,
            i.cps_pln_nr AS ConsolidatedPlanNumber,
            i.cps_pln_dt AS ConsolidatedPlanDate,
            i.cps_pln_na AS ConsolidatedPlanBillName,
            i.taxtier AS TierTaxAmount,
            i.tax_pct AS TaxPct,
            i.bill_flr AS BillAddressFloor,
            i.bill_sui AS BillAddressSuite,
            i.bill_sta AS BillStateCode,
            i.processed AS Processed,
            i.orig_type_cd AS OriginalTypeCode,
            i.os_amt AS OSAmount,
            i.cny_cd AS CountryCode,
            i.tax_var AS TaxVariance,
            i.batch_no AS BatchNumber,
            i.batch_dt AS BatchDate,
            i.void AS InvoiceVoid,
            i.crdt_ind AS Crdt_ind,
            i.cd_ind AS Cd_ind,
            i.BAFlag AS BAFlag,
            i.close_dt AS Close_dt,
            i.last_print_date AS LastPrintDate,
            i.last_print_by AS LastPrintBy,
            i.org_inv_date AS OriginalInvoiceDate,
            i.TAX_ID AS TAX_ID,
            i.EXEMPTION_ID AS EXEMPTION_ID,
            i.PROC_TM AS PROC_TM,
            i.IsBilled AS IsBilled,
            i.ccard_cd as ccard_cd,
            i.GUI_NO AS GUI_NO,
            I.RCV_NO AS RCV_NO
            FROM #transforminvoices I
			WHERE i.ac_no NOT IN (SELECT AccountNumber FROM  CustomerProfiles WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE CustomerCountryCode=@CountryCode)
            and ((i.ac_no like '9%' or i.ac_no like  '8%') OR i.type_cd in ('4','24','31'))) A ORDER BY UploadDate
      END
      ELSE        
      IF(@CountryCode='TW')
      BEGIN
			SELECT TOP 6000 * FROM (
            SELECT
            i.CreationTime AS CreationTime,
            i.CreationUser AS CreationUser,
            i.LastModificationTime AS LastModificationTime,
            i.LastModificationUser AS LastModificationUser,

            AND ((I.tax_amt > 0 AND ((i.GUI_NO IS NOT NULL AND I.GUI_NO !='')  OR (I.RCV_NO IS NOT NULL AND I.RCV_NO !=''))) OR (sign(I.tax_amt) = 0 or sign(I.tax_amt) = -1 )

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetInvoiceInfosForTransformCN]
AS
BEGIN
      SET NOCOUNT ON;
      DECLARE @CDATE DATETIME=(SELECT Value FROM SETTINGS WHERE TYPE='LATESTTRANSFORMTIMECN')
      ;WITH CTE AS (
		SELECT RANK() OVER(ORDER BY I.CDATE ASC) AS RNO,
      i.CreationTime AS CreationTime,
      i.CreationUser AS CreationUser,
      i.LastModificationTime AS LastModificationTime,
      i.LastModificationUser AS LastModificationUser,
      i.DeletionTime AS DeletionTime,
      i.IsDeleted AS IsDeleted,
      i.DeletionUser AS DeletionUser,
      i.Id AS Id,
      i.ac_no AS AccountNumber,
      i.inv_no AS InvoiceNumber,
      i.inv_dt AS InvoiceDate,
      i.inv_date AS InvoiceDateString,
      i.due_date AS InvoiceDueDate,
      i.period_dt AS PeriodEndingDate,
      i.type_cd AS TypeCode,
      i.bill_cont AS BillContactName,
      i.bill_name AS BillName,
      i.bill_state AS BillState,
      i.bill_city AS BillCity,
      i.bill_postl AS BillPostal,
      i.bill_ctry AS BillCountry,
      i.pmt_term AS PaymentTerms,
      i.ust_id AS CustomerTaxID,
      i.cop_coll AS AmountCollected,
      i.taxable AS TotalTaxableCharges,
      i.nontax AS TotalNonTaxableCharges,
      i.tax_amt AS TotalTaxAmount,
      i.bill_amt AS BillAmount,
      i.inv_curr AS InvoiceCurrencyCode,
      i.filename AS Filename,
      i.cuser AS UploadUser,
      i.cdate AS UploadDate,
      i.last_print AS LastPrint,
      i.no_printed AS NoPrinted,
      i.giro_prt AS GiroPrint,
      i.no_giro AS NoGiroPrint,
      i.out_queue AS OutputQueue,
      i.sort_fld AS SortFieldValue,
      i.way_sort AS WaybillSorting,
      i.disp_level AS DispLevel,
      i.slic_num AS SlicNumber,
      i.cps_pln_nr AS ConsolidatedPlanNumber,
      i.cps_pln_dt AS ConsolidatedPlanDate,
      i.cps_pln_na AS ConsolidatedPlanBillName,
      i.taxtier AS TierTaxAmount,
      i.tax_pct AS TaxPct,
      i.bill_flr AS BillAddressFloor,
      i.bill_sui AS BillAddressSuite,
      i.bill_sta AS BillStateCode,
      i.processed AS Processed,
      i.orig_type_cd AS OriginalTypeCode,
      i.os_amt AS OSAmount,
      i.cny_cd AS CountryCode,
      i.tax_var AS TaxVariance,
      i.batch_no AS BatchNumber,
      i.batch_dt AS BatchDate,
      i.void AS InvoiceVoid,
      i.crdt_ind AS Crdt_ind,
      i.cd_ind AS Cd_ind,
      i.BAFlag AS BAFlag,
      i.close_dt AS Close_dt,
      i.last_print_date AS LastPrintDate,
      i.last_print_by AS LastPrintBy,
      i.org_inv_date AS OriginalInvoiceDate,
      i.TAX_ID AS TAX_ID,
      i.EXEMPTION_ID AS EXEMPTION_ID,
      i.PROC_TM AS PROC_TM,
      i.IsBilled AS IsBilled,
      i.ccard_cd as ccard_cd,
      i.GUI_NO AS GUI_NO,
      I.RCV_NO AS RCV_NO
      FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
      JOIN CustomerProfiles c with(nolock) on i.ac_no = c.accountNumber and i.cny_cd = c.customercountrycode AND CustomerCountryCode = 'CN' and  i.slic_num in (select Slic from UPSInformations where CountryCode = 'CN')
      LEFT JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos b with(nolock) on b.inv_no = i.inv_no and b.cny_cd = i.cny_cd AND b.cny_cd = 'CN'
      JOIN (SELECT inv_no, cny_cd,SUM(inv_total)inv_total FROM InvoiceShipments with(nolock) WHERE cny_cd = 'CN' GROUP BY inv_no, cny_cd) s on s.inv_no = i.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
      WHERE b.inv_no is null and i.cny_cd = 'CN' AND i.cdate >= cast(@cdate as Datetime) AND (i.bill_amt - (i.cop_coll + i.tax_var)) = inv_total
      UNION
      SELECT RANK() OVER(ORDER BY I.CDATE) AS RNO,
      i.CreationTime AS CreationTime,
      i.CreationUser AS CreationUser,
      i.LastModificationTime AS LastModificationTime,
      i.LastModificationUser AS LastModificationUser,
      i.DeletionTime AS DeletionTime,
      i.IsDeleted AS IsDeleted,
      i.DeletionUser AS DeletionUser,
      i.Id AS Id,
      i.ac_no AS AccountNumber,
      i.inv_no AS InvoiceNumber,
      i.inv_dt AS InvoiceDate,
      i.inv_date AS InvoiceDateString,
      i.due_date AS InvoiceDueDate,
      i.period_dt AS PeriodEndingDate,
      i.type_cd AS TypeCode,
      i.bill_cont AS BillContactName,
      i.bill_name AS BillName,
      i.bill_state AS BillState,
      i.bill_city AS BillCity,
      i.bill_postl AS BillPostal,
      i.bill_ctry AS BillCountry,
      i.pmt_term AS PaymentTerms,
      i.ust_id AS CustomerTaxID,
      i.cop_coll AS AmountCollected,
      i.taxable AS TotalTaxableCharges,
      i.nontax AS TotalNonTaxableCharges,
      i.tax_amt AS TotalTaxAmount,
      i.bill_amt AS BillAmount,
      i.inv_curr AS InvoiceCurrencyCode,
      i.filename AS Filename,
      i.cuser AS UploadUser,
      i.cdate AS UploadDate,
      i.last_print AS LastPrint,
      i.no_printed AS NoPrinted,
      i.giro_prt AS GiroPrint,
      i.no_giro AS NoGiroPrint,
      i.out_queue AS OutputQueue,
      i.sort_fld AS SortFieldValue,
      i.way_sort AS WaybillSorting,
      i.disp_level AS DispLevel,
      i.slic_num AS SlicNumber,
      i.cps_pln_nr AS ConsolidatedPlanNumber,
      i.cps_pln_dt AS ConsolidatedPlanDate,
      i.cps_pln_na AS ConsolidatedPlanBillName,
      i.taxtier AS TierTaxAmount,
      i.tax_pct AS TaxPct,
      i.bill_flr AS BillAddressFloor,
      i.bill_sui AS BillAddressSuite,
      i.bill_sta AS BillStateCode,
      i.processed AS Processed,
      i.orig_type_cd AS OriginalTypeCode,
      i.os_amt AS OSAmount,
      i.cny_cd AS CountryCode,
      i.tax_var AS TaxVariance,
      i.batch_no AS BatchNumber,
      i.batch_dt AS BatchDate,
      i.void AS InvoiceVoid,
      i.crdt_ind AS Crdt_ind,
      i.cd_ind AS Cd_ind,
      i.BAFlag AS BAFlag,
      i.close_dt AS Close_dt,
      i.last_print_date AS LastPrintDate,
      i.last_print_by AS LastPrintBy,
      i.org_inv_date AS OriginalInvoiceDate,
      i.TAX_ID AS TAX_ID,
      i.EXEMPTION_ID AS EXEMPTION_ID,
      i.PROC_TM AS PROC_TM,
      i.IsBilled AS IsBilled,
      i.ccard_cd as ccard_cd,
      i.GUI_NO AS GUI_NO,
      I.RCV_NO AS RCV_NO
      FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
      LEFT JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos b with(nolock) on b.inv_no = i.inv_no and b.cny_cd = i.cny_cd AND b.cny_cd = 'CN'
      JOIN (SELECT inv_no, cny_cd,SUM(inv_total)inv_total FROM InvoiceShipments with(nolock) WHERE cny_cd = 'CN' GROUP BY inv_no, cny_cd) s on s.inv_no = i.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
      WHERE b.inv_no is null and i.cny_cd = 'CN' and i.ac_no NOT IN (SELECT AccountNumber FROM  CustomerProfiles WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE CustomerCountryCode='CN')
      and ((i.ac_no like '9%' or i.ac_no like  '8%') OR i.type_cd in ('4','24','31')) AND i.cdate >= cast(@cdate as Datetime)
      AND (i.bill_amt - (i.cop_coll + i.tax_var)) = inv_total)
SELECT TOP 6000 * FROM CTE WHERE RNO=1 ORDER BY ID 
END
