CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetInvoiceLanguageByCountry]
	@countryCode varchar(2),
	@InvoiceNo Varchar(12)=null
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	IF(@countryCode='JP' AND ISNULL(@InvoiceNo,'')<>'')
		select il.CountryCode
		,il.InvoiceDate
		,il.AccountNo
		,il.InvoiceNo
		,il.Page
		,il.Date
		,il.DiscountLevel
		,il.Reference
		,il.TrackingNo
		,il.SenderOrConsignee
		,il.Country
		,il.Zone
		,il.Service
		,il.Product
		,il.Package
		,il.Weight
		,il.PeriodTotal
		,il.ChargeType
		,il.TaxableCharges
		,il.Discount
		,il.NetCharges
		,il.OriginalInvoiceNo
		,il.Description
		,il.NonTaxable
		,il.Vat
		,il.TotalAdjustment
		,il.ConsumptionTax
		,il.TotalTierDiscount
		,il.TotalCharge
		,il.LessAndCOD
		,il.VatPaid
		,il.senderText
		,il.TotalWorldwideWaybillCharges
		,ups.BusinessType,
		CASE WHEN il.CountryCode = 'CN' THEN N'チャージ' ELSE NULL END  as ChargeType_Tier1
		,il.InvoiceAmount
		,il.Total
		from BilledInvoiceInfos bi
		inner join InvoiceLanguages il on il.CountryCode = bi.cny_cd
		LEFT join UPSInformations ups on ups.CountryCode = bi.cny_cd and ups.Slic = bi.slic_num
		WHERE bi.cny_cd = @countryCode and bi.inv_no = @invoiceNo
	ELSE
		select CountryCode
		,InvoiceDate
		,AccountNo
		,InvoiceNo
		,Page
		,Date
		,DiscountLevel
		,Reference
		,TrackingNo
		,SenderOrConsignee
		,Country
		,Zone
		,Service
		,Product
		,Package
		,Weight
		,PeriodTotal
		,ChargeType
		,TaxableCharges
		,Discount
		,NetCharges
		,OriginalInvoiceNo
		,Description
		,NonTaxable
		,Vat
		,TotalAdjustment
		,ConsumptionTax
		,TotalTierDiscount
		,TotalCharge
		,LessAndCOD
		,VatPaid
		,senderText
		,TotalWorldwideWaybillCharges,
		CASE WHEN CountryCode = 'JP' THEN N'チャージ' ELSE NULL END  as ChargeType_Tier1
		,InvoiceAmount
		,Total
		from InvoiceLanguages  WHERE CountryCode = @countryCode
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetInvoicesforUpload]
@CountryCode varchar(2),
@numberCheckBulky INT,
@IsBulky bit
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
	UPDATE BILLEDINVOICEINFOS SET ISRUNUPLOAD=0,DELETIONTIME=NULL WHERE CNY_CD=@CountryCode and IsRunUpload=1 and DeletionTime<=DATEADD(HOUR,-3,GETDATE())
	IF(@IsBulky='1')
	BEGIN
	SELECT
    BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no AS InvoiceNo,
    BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd as CountryCode,
    BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt as Amount,
    CASE
    WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt != 0 and type_cd !='4' THEN '1'
    WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt = 0  and type_cd !='4' THEN '2'
    WHEN type_cd ='4' THEN 3 ELSE NULL END AS [Rule],
    CASE
    WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.LastModificationTime IS NOT NULL AND BilledInvoiceInfos.LastModificationUser = 'SYSTEM_GeneratedFileName' THEN 1
    ELSE 0 END as IsGeneratedInvoiceName,
    CASE
    WHEN ISNULL(BilledInvoiceInfos.IsRunUpload, 0) != 1 THEN 0
    ELSE 1 END as IsRunUpload
    FROM BilledInvoiceInfos
    LEFT JOIN InvoiceShipmentGroupingData as BilledInvoiceShipments on BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_no and BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd
    WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 0 AND ISNULL(BilledInvoiceInfos.[IsMissCharges],0) = 0
    AND ISNULL(BilledInvoiceShipments.TotalShipment,0) > @numberCheckBulky
    AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode
	AND IsRunUpload<>1
    AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt = ( SELECT TOP 1 inv_dt FROM BilledInvoiceInfos
	JOIN InvoiceShipmentGroupingData as BilledInvoiceShipments on BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_no and BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd
	WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 0 AND ISNULL(BilledInvoiceInfos.[IsMissCharges],0) = 0
	AND ISNULL(BilledInvoiceShipments.TotalShipment,0) > @numberCheckBulky
	AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode
	AND IsRunUpload<>1
	ORDER By (CASE
	WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt != 0 THEN '1'
	WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt = 0 THEN '2'
	ELSE NULL END) ASC, BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt ASC)
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
	SELECT
    BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no AS InvoiceNo,
    BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd as CountryCode,
    BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt as Amount,
    CASE
    WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt != 0 and type_cd !='4' THEN '1'
    WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt = 0  and type_cd !='4' THEN '2'
    WHEN type_cd ='4' THEN 3 ELSE NULL END AS [Rule],
    CASE
    WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.LastModificationTime IS NOT NULL AND BilledInvoiceInfos.LastModificationUser = 'SYSTEM_GeneratedFileName' THEN 1
    ELSE 0 END as IsGeneratedInvoiceName,
    CASE
    WHEN ISNULL(BilledInvoiceInfos.IsRunUpload, 0) != 1 THEN 0
    ELSE 1 END as IsRunUpload
    FROM BilledInvoiceInfos
    JOIN InvoiceShipmentGroupingData as BilledInvoiceShipments on BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_no and BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd
    WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 0 AND ISNULL(BilledInvoiceInfos.[IsMissCharges],0) = 0
	AND IsRunUpload<>1
    AND ISNULL(BilledInvoiceShipments.TotalShipment,0) <= @numberCheckBulky
    AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode
    AND BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt = ( SELECT TOP 1 inv_dt FROM BilledInvoiceInfos
	JOIN InvoiceShipmentGroupingData as BilledInvoiceShipments on BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_no and BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd
	WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 0  AND ISNULL(BilledInvoiceInfos.[IsMissCharges],0) = 0
	AND ISNULL(BilledInvoiceShipments.TotalShipment,0) <= @numberCheckBulky 
	AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode
	AND IsRunUpload<>1
	ORDER By (CASE
	WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt != 0 THEN '1'
	WHEN BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt = 0 THEN '2'
	ELSE NULL END) ASC, BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_dt ASC)
	END
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetInvoiceShipmentsorChargesForTransform]
@CountryCode Varchar(2),
@Type Varchar(10),
@InvoiceNumbers Varchar(max)
AS
BEGIN
		SET NOCOUNT ON;
		DECLARE @SearchValues as Table(SearchValue varchar(100))
		INSERT INTO @SearchValues
		select value from string_split(@InvoiceNumbers,',')
		IF(@TYPE='SHIPMENTS')
		BEGIN
			select 
			s.CreationTime AS CreationTime,
			s.CreationUser AS CreationUser,
			s.LastModificationTime AS LastModificationTime,
			s.LastModificationUser AS LastModificationUser,
			s.DeletionTime AS DeletionTime,
			s.IsDeleted AS IsDeleted,
			s.DeletionUser AS DeletionUser,
			s.Id AS Id,
			s.recid AS RecId,
			s.inv_no AS InvoiceNumber,
			s.inv_type AS InvoiceType,
			s.exp_dt AS ExportDate,
			s.exp_date AS ExportDateString,
			s.gcc_num AS WorldEaseNumber,
			s.track_no AS TrackingNumber,
			s.track_11 AS Tracking11,
			s.csort AS SortDuplicateTrackingNumber,
			s.product AS Product,
			s.service AS Service,
			s.bill_term AS BillTerms,
			s.zone AS Zone,
			ISNULL(s.mvm_drc,'') AS MovementDirection,
			s.package AS Package,
			s.actual_wt AS ActualWeight,
			s.bill_wt AS BillingWeight,
			s.wt_msg AS BillWeightMessage,
			s.ref_1 AS Ref_1,
			s.ref_2 AS Ref_2,
			s.shp_cont AS ShipperContactName,
			s.ship_name AS ShipperName,
			s.ship_city AS ShipperCity,
			s.ship_state AS ShipperState,
			s.ship_postl AS ShipperPostal,
			s.ship_ctry AS ShipperCountry,
			s.cons_cont AS ConsigneeContactName,
			s.cons_name AS ConsigneeName,
			s.cons_city AS ConsigneeCity,
			s.cons_state AS ConsigneeState,
			s.cons_postl AS ConsigneePostal,
			s.cons_ctry AS ConsigneeCountry,
			s.dest_ctry AS DestinationExportCountry,
			s.freight AS Freight,
			s.discount AS Discount,
			s.acc_201 AS AccessorialDutyAmount,
			s.acc_205 AS AccessorialVatGst,
			s.frt_rate AS FreightExchangeRate,
			s.acc_adc AS AccessorialAddrCorr,
			s.acc_chg AS Charges,
			s.orig_inv AS OriginalInvoice,
			s.inv_total AS TotalCharges,
			s.desc_goods AS DescGoods,
			s.decl_value AS DeclareValue,
			s.sur_ups AS Surcharges,
			s.sur_brok AS SurchargesBrok,
			s.exchg_rate AS ExchangeRate,
			s.exrate_cd AS ExchangeRateCurrency,
			s.custom_no AS CustomsNumber,
			s.reason_cd AS MessageReason,
			s.acct_no AS OriginalBill,
			s.rqst_name AS RequesterName,
			s.rqst_comp AS RequesterCompanyName,
			s.rqst_phone AS RequesterPhoneNumber,
			s.rqst_email AS RequesterEmail,
			s.trd_cont AS RequesterContactInformation,
			s.trd_name AS RTNRequesterName,
			s.trd_city AS RTNRequesterCity,
			s.trd_state AS RTNRequesterState,
			s.trd_postal AS RTNRequesterPostal,
			s.trd_ctry AS RTNRequesterCountryCode,
			s.comments AS Comments,
			s.trans_cd AS TransactionCode,
			isnull(s.chg_msg,'') AS ChgMsg,
			s.tier_inct AS TierIncentiveAmount,
			s.minshp_prc AS MinshpPrc,
			s.svc_supp AS ServiceSupplmentCode,
			s.min_pkgwt AS MinPkgwt,
			s.frt_taxapl AS FreightAmount,
			s.vartaxamt AS Vartaxamt,
			s.ship_flr AS ShipperAddressFloor,
			s.ship_sui AS ShipperAddressSuite,
			s.ship_sta AS ShipperAddressStateCode,
			s.cons_flr AS ConsigneeAddressFloor,
			s.cons_sui AS ConsigneeAddressSuite,
			s.cons_sta AS ConsigneeAddressStateCode,
			s.ccard_cd AS CreditCardCode,
			s.ccard_4dgt AS Ccard4dgt,
			s.payor AS Payor,
			s.cny_cd AS CountryCode,
			s.slic_num AS SlicNumber,
			s.custom_clr AS CustomClearanceNumber,
			s.CUSTOM_EXCHG_RATE AS CUSTOM_EXCHG_RATE,
			s.IA_CRD_TYP_CD AS IA_CRD_TYP_CD,
			s.IA_CRD_NR AS IA_CRD_NR,
			s.IA_CRD_ATH_NR AS IA_CRD_ATH_NR,
			s.IA_CRD_EXP_DT AS IA_CRD_EXP_DT,
			s.IsBilled AS IsBilled,
			s.RodeName AS RodeName,
			s.SequenceNumber AS SequenceNumber,
			s.BatchNumber AS BatchNumber
			FROM InvoiceShipments S WITH(NOLOCK) 
			inner join @SearchValues ss on  SS.SearchValue=S.inv_no
			WHERE cny_cd=@CountryCode
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
		SELECT * FROM InvoiceCharges S(nolock)
		inner join @SearchValues ss on  SS.SearchValue=S.recid
		WHERE cny_cd=@CountryCode
		END
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetInvoiceTransaction]
@invoiceNo varchar(12),
@CountryCode Varchar(2),
@IsSuppressShipment BIT=0
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	IF(@IsSuppressShipment=1)
		SELECT DISTINCT
		BIS.inv_type as TransactionType,
		CASE
		WHEN @countryCode = 'CN' THEN InvoiceTransactions.China
		WHEN @countryCode = 'JP' THEN InvoiceTransactions.Japan
		WHEN @countryCode = 'KR' THEN InvoiceTransactions.Korea
		WHEN @countryCode = 'TW' THEN InvoiceTransactions.Taiwan
		END as SqlQueryId
		FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments BIS WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = BIS.billed_inv_no AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BIS.cny_cd
		LEFT JOIN InvoiceCharges IC WITH(NOLOCK) ON IC.recid=BIS.recid AND IC.inv_no=BIS.inv_no
		AND (camount!=0 or publ_amt!=0 or inct_amt!=0)
		LEFT JOIN InvoiceTransactions WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BIS.inv_type = InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType AND InvoiceTransactions.InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = @invoiceNo
		AND BIS.inv_total<>0
	ELSE
		SELECT DISTINCT
		BIS.inv_type as TransactionType,
		CASE
		WHEN @countryCode = 'CN' THEN InvoiceTransactions.China
		WHEN @countryCode = 'JP' THEN InvoiceTransactions.Japan
		WHEN @countryCode = 'KR' THEN InvoiceTransactions.Korea
		WHEN @countryCode = 'TW' THEN InvoiceTransactions.Taiwan
		END as SqlQueryId
		FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments BIS WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = BIS.billed_inv_no AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BIS.cny_cd
		LEFT JOIN InvoiceTransactions WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BIS.inv_type = InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType AND InvoiceTransactions.InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = @invoiceNo
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetShipmentDataforCoverTemplate]
	@invoiceNo VARCHAR(MAX),
	@countryCode VARCHAR(2),
	@invoiceTransaction varchar(30)
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	IF(@invoiceTransaction='OtherTransactionType_CV')
	BEGIN
		SELECT
		BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201 as Acc201,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205 as Acc205,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt as VarTaxAmt,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.freight as Freight,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc as AccAdc,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg as AccChg,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.discount as Discount,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct as TierInct,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.recid as RecId,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no as TrackingNo,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type as InvoiceType,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl as FrtTaxapl,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.recids as RecIds,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.dest_ctry as DestCtry
		FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.billed_inv_no AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd
		LEFT OUTER JOIN InvoiceTransactions WITH(NOLOCK) ON BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type = InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countryCode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = @invoiceNo AND
		InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType is NULL
		ORDER By RecId
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
	SELECT DISTINCT
		BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201 as Acc201,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205 as Acc205,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt as VarTaxAmt,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.freight as Freight,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc as AccAdc,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg as AccChg,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.discount as Discount,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl as FrtTaxapl,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct as TierInct,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.recid as RecId,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no as TrackingNo,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type as InvoiceType,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.recids as RecIds,
		BilledInvoiceShipments.dest_ctry as DestCtry
		FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK)
		JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.billed_inv_no AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd
		JOIN InvoiceTransactions WITH(NOLOCK)
		ON BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type = InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType
		WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = @countrycode AND BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = @invoiceNo AND @invoiceTransaction=
		CASE WHEN @COUNTRYCODE='TW' THEN INVOICETRANSACTIONS.TAIWAN
		WHEN @COUNTRYCODE='CN' THEN INVOICETRANSACTIONS.CHINA
		WHEN @COUNTRYCODE='KR' THEN INVOICETRANSACTIONS.KOREA
		WHEN @COUNTRYCODE='JP' THEN INVOICETRANSACTIONS.JAPAN END
		ORDER By RecId
	END
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetTaxInvoiceBySearching]
(
	@ac_no varchar(10),
	@inv_no varchar(12),
	@inv_dt_from datetime2(7),
	@inv_dt_to datetime2(7),
	@page_number int,
	@page_size int,
	@order_by varchar(100),
	@exclude_product ExcludedProduct readonly,
	@total int output
)
AS
BEGIN
	CREATE TABLE #Invoices(
		Id int,
		inv_no varchar(12),
		ac_no varchar(10),
		inv_dt datetime2(7),
		bill_amt decimal(18,2),
		nontax decimal(18,2),
		taxable decimal(18,2),
		type_cd varchar(2),
		tax_amt decimal(18,2)
	);
	CREATE NONCLUSTERED INDEX IX_Invoices_AccountNumber
	ON #Invoices ([ac_no])
	INCLUDE ([Id],[inv_no],[inv_dt],[nontax],[taxable],[type_cd])
	INSERT INTO #Invoices
	SELECT
	bi.Id,
	bi.inv_no,
	bi.ac_no,
	bi.inv_dt,
	bi.bill_amt,
	bi.nontax,
	bi.taxable,
	bi.type_cd,
	bi.tax_amt
	FROM BilledInvoiceInfos bi with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles cp with(nolock)
		ON bi.ac_no = cp.AccountNumber
	JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments bs with(nolock)
		ON bi.inv_no = bs.inv_no
	WHERE (bi.IsMissCharges is null or bi.IsMissCharges = 0)
		  and (bi.type_cd <> '8' OR bs.sur_brok > 0) 
		  and bi.cny_cd = 'KR' 
		  and bi.type_cd not in ('4', '5', '9', '16', '24')
		  and (cp.IsOptOutVATInvoice is null or cp.IsOptOutVATInvoice = 0 or bs.product not in (select product from @exclude_product))
	GROUP BY 
	bi.Id,
	bi.inv_no,
	bi.ac_no,
	bi.inv_dt,
	bi.bill_amt,
	bi.nontax,
	bi.taxable,
	bi.type_cd,
	bi.tax_amt
	SELECT
	c.Id AS CustomerId,
	ISNULL(ti.Id, bi.Id) AS Id,
	c.CompanyNameOnTaxInvoice AS CompanyName,
	c.AddressKoreaOnTaxInvoice AS CustomerAddress,
	c.RecipientNameOnTaxInvoice1 AS RecipientOfTaxInvoice,
	c.RepresentativeNameOnTaxInvoice AS RepresentativeName,
	c.CustomerTaxId AS TaxId,
	c.BusinessType AS BusinessType,
	c.BusinessItem AS BusinessItem,
	c.RecipientPhoneOnTaxInvoice1 AS ReferenceNumber,
	c.RecipientEmailOnTaxInvoice1 AS EmailAddress,
	c.TaxIdentificationId AS TaxIdentificationId,
	bi.inv_no AS InvoiceNumber,
	bi.ac_no AS AccountNumber,
	bi.inv_dt AS InvoiceDate,
	bi.bill_amt AS BillAmount,
	ISNULL(ti.Status, '') AS Status,
	ti.SubmitDate AS Date,
	bi.nontax AS TotalNonTaxableCharges,
	bi.taxable AS TotalTaxableCharges,
	bi.type_cd AS TypeCode,
    ISNULL(ti.Message, '') AS Message,
	ti.IsNegativeAmount AS IsNegativeAmount,
	ti.modification_code AS ModificationCode,
	ti.IsBlockRevised AS IsBlockRevised,
	ti.ntsConfirmNum AS NtsConfirmNum,
	ti.Suffix AS Suffix,
	bi.tax_amt AS TotalTaxAmount
	INTO #Temp
	FROM #Invoices bi with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles c with(nolock)
		ON bi.ac_no = c.AccountNumber
	LEFT JOIN TaxInvoices ti with(nolock)
		ON bi.inv_no = ti.inv_no
	WHERE c.CustomerCountryCode = 'KR'
	  AND (@ac_no is null OR @ac_no = '' OR bi.ac_no LIKE '%' + @ac_no + '%')
	  AND (@inv_no is null OR @inv_no = '' OR bi.inv_no LIKE '%' + @inv_no + '%')
	  AND (@inv_dt_from is null OR bi.inv_dt >= @inv_dt_from)
	  AND (@inv_dt_to is null OR bi.inv_dt <= @inv_dt_to)
	;WITH Paging AS
	(
		SELECT * FROM #Temp Result
		ORDER BY
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceNumber ASCENDING' THEN  InvoiceNumber END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceNumber DESCENDING' THEN  InvoiceNumber END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'accountNumber ASCENDING' THEN  AccountNumber END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'accountNumber DESCENDING' THEN  AccountNumber END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceDate ASCENDING' THEN  InvoiceDate END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceDate DESCENDING' THEN  InvoiceDate END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'typeCode ASCENDING' THEN  TypeCode END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'typeCode DESCENDING' THEN  TypeCode END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'billAmount ASCENDING' THEN  BillAmount END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'billAmount DESCENDING' THEN  BillAmount END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'status ASCENDING' THEN  Status END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'status DESCENDING' THEN  Status END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'date ASCENDING' THEN  Date END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'date DESCENDING' THEN  Date END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'message ASCENDING' THEN  Message END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'message DESCENDING' THEN  Message END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'Id ASCENDING' THEN  Id END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'Id DESCENDING' THEN  Id END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'ntsConfirmNum ASCENDING' THEN  NtsConfirmNum END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'ntsConfirmNum DESCENDING' THEN  NtsConfirmNum END) DESC
			OFFSET @page_number  ROWS
			FETCH NEXT @page_size ROWS ONLY
	)
	SELECT * FROM Paging
	ORDER BY
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceNumber ASCENDING' THEN  InvoiceNumber END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceNumber DESCENDING' THEN  InvoiceNumber END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'accountNumber ASCENDING' THEN  AccountNumber END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'accountNumber DESCENDING' THEN  AccountNumber END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceDate ASCENDING' THEN  InvoiceDate END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceDate DESCENDING' THEN  InvoiceDate END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'typeCode ASCENDING' THEN  TypeCode END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'typeCode DESCENDING' THEN  TypeCode END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'billAmount ASCENDING' THEN  BillAmount END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'billAmount DESCENDING' THEN  BillAmount END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'status ASCENDING' THEN  Status END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'status DESCENDING' THEN  Status END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'date ASCENDING' THEN  Date END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'date DESCENDING' THEN  Date END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'message ASCENDING' THEN  Message END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'message DESCENDING' THEN  Message END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'Id ASCENDING' THEN  Id END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'Id DESCENDING' THEN  Id END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'ntsConfirmNum ASCENDING' THEN  NtsConfirmNum END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'ntsConfirmNum DESCENDING' THEN  NtsConfirmNum END) DESC
	OPTION (RECOMPILE);
	SET @total = (SELECT COUNT(1) FROM #Temp)
	DROP TABLE #Invoices
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetTaxInvoiceByUploading]
(
	@invoice_numbers InvoiceNumbers readonly,
	@page_number int,
	@page_size int,
	@order_by varchar(100),
	@exclude_product ExcludedProduct readonly,
	@total int output
)
AS
BEGIN
	CREATE TABLE #Invoices(
		Id int,
		inv_no varchar(12),
		ac_no varchar(10),
		inv_dt datetime2(7),
		bill_amt decimal(18,2),
		nontax decimal(18,2),
		taxable decimal(18,2),
		type_cd varchar(2),
		tax_amt decimal(18,2)
	);
	CREATE NONCLUSTERED INDEX IX_Invoices_AccountNumber
	ON #Invoices ([ac_no])
	INCLUDE ([Id],[inv_no],[inv_dt],[nontax],[taxable],[type_cd])
	INSERT INTO #Invoices
	SELECT
	bi.Id,
	bi.inv_no,
	bi.ac_no,
	bi.inv_dt,
	bi.bill_amt,
	bi.nontax,
	bi.taxable,
	bi.type_cd,
	bi.tax_amt
	FROM BilledInvoiceInfos bi with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles cp with(nolock)
		ON bi.ac_no = cp.AccountNumber
	JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments bs with(nolock)
		ON bi.inv_no = bs.inv_no
	WHERE (bi.IsMissCharges is null or bi.IsMissCharges = 0)
		and (bi.type_cd <> '8' OR bs.sur_brok > 0) 
		and bi.cny_cd = 'KR' 
		and bi.type_cd not in ('4', '5', '9', '16', '24')
		and (cp.IsOptOutVATInvoice is null or cp.IsOptOutVATInvoice = 0 or bs.product not in (select product from @exclude_product))
		and (bi.inv_no IN (SELECT invoiceNumber FROM @invoice_numbers))
	GROUP BY 
	bi.Id,
	bi.inv_no,
	bi.ac_no,
	bi.inv_dt,
	bi.bill_amt,
	bi.nontax,
	bi.taxable,
	bi.type_cd,
	bi.tax_amt
	SELECT
	c.Id AS CustomerId,
	ISNULL(ti.Id, bi.Id) AS Id,
	c.CompanyNameOnTaxInvoice AS CompanyName,
	c.AddressKoreaOnTaxInvoice AS CustomerAddress,
	c.RecipientNameOnTaxInvoice1 AS RecipientOfTaxInvoice,
	c.RepresentativeNameOnTaxInvoice AS RepresentativeName,
	c.CustomerTaxId AS TaxId,
	c.BusinessType AS BusinessType,
	c.BusinessItem AS BusinessItem,
	c.RecipientPhoneOnTaxInvoice1 AS ReferenceNumber,
	c.RecipientEmailOnTaxInvoice1 AS EmailAddress,
	c.TaxIdentificationId AS TaxIdentificationId,
	bi.inv_no AS InvoiceNumber,
	bi.ac_no AS AccountNumber,
	bi.inv_dt AS InvoiceDate,
	bi.bill_amt AS BillAmount,
	ISNULL(ti.Status, '') AS Status,
	ti.SubmitDate AS Date,
	bi.nontax AS TotalNonTaxableCharges,
	bi.taxable AS TotalTaxableCharges,
	bi.type_cd AS TypeCode,
    ISNULL(ti.Message, '') AS Message,
	ti.IsNegativeAmount AS IsNegativeAmount,
	ti.modification_code AS ModificationCode,
	ti.IsBlockRevised AS IsBlockRevised,
	ti.ntsConfirmNum AS NtsConfirmNum,
	ti.Suffix AS Suffix,
	bi.tax_amt AS TotalTaxAmount
	INTO #Temp
	FROM #Invoices bi with(nolock)
	JOIN CustomerProfiles c with(nolock)
		ON bi.ac_no = c.AccountNumber
	LEFT JOIN TaxInvoices ti with(nolock)
		ON bi.inv_no = ti.inv_no
	WHERE c.CustomerCountryCode = 'KR'
	;WITH Paging AS
	(
		SELECT * FROM #Temp Result
		ORDER BY
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceNumber ASCENDING' THEN  InvoiceNumber END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceNumber DESCENDING' THEN  InvoiceNumber END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'accountNumber ASCENDING' THEN  AccountNumber END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'accountNumber DESCENDING' THEN  AccountNumber END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceDate ASCENDING' THEN  InvoiceDate END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceDate DESCENDING' THEN  InvoiceDate END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'typeCode ASCENDING' THEN  TypeCode END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'typeCode DESCENDING' THEN  TypeCode END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'billAmount ASCENDING' THEN  BillAmount END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'billAmount DESCENDING' THEN  BillAmount END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'status ASCENDING' THEN  Status END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'status DESCENDING' THEN  Status END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'date ASCENDING' THEN  Date END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'date DESCENDING' THEN  Date END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'message ASCENDING' THEN  Message END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'message DESCENDING' THEN  Message END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'Id ASCENDING' THEN  Id END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'Id DESCENDING' THEN  Id END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'ntsConfirmNum ASCENDING' THEN  NtsConfirmNum END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'ntsConfirmNum DESCENDING' THEN  NtsConfirmNum END) DESC
			OFFSET @page_number  ROWS
			FETCH NEXT @page_size ROWS ONLY
	)
	SELECT * FROM Paging
	ORDER BY
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceNumber ASCENDING' THEN  InvoiceNumber END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceNumber DESCENDING' THEN  InvoiceNumber END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'accountNumber ASCENDING' THEN  AccountNumber END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'accountNumber DESCENDING' THEN  AccountNumber END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceDate ASCENDING' THEN  InvoiceDate END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'invoiceDate DESCENDING' THEN  InvoiceDate END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'typeCode ASCENDING' THEN  TypeCode END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'typeCode DESCENDING' THEN  TypeCode END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'billAmount ASCENDING' THEN  BillAmount END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'billAmount DESCENDING' THEN  BillAmount END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'status ASCENDING' THEN  Status END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'status DESCENDING' THEN  Status END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'date ASCENDING' THEN  Date END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'date DESCENDING' THEN  Date END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'message ASCENDING' THEN  Message END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'message DESCENDING' THEN  Message END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'Id ASCENDING' THEN  Id END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'Id DESCENDING' THEN  Id END) DESC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'ntsConfirmNum ASCENDING' THEN  NtsConfirmNum END) ASC,
			(CASE WHEN @order_by = 'ntsConfirmNum DESCENDING' THEN  NtsConfirmNum END) DESC
	OPTION (RECOMPILE);
	SET @total = (SELECT COUNT(1) FROM #Temp)
	DROP TABLE #Invoices
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[InsertDataIntoArchival]			
  AS
  declare @noofdays int 
  declare @noofarcdays int 
  select @noofdays = [Value] from [dbo].sysConfig where [KEY]='ArchiveJobHistory'
  select @noofarcdays = ([VALUE]+VALUE_ARC) from [dbo].sysConfig where [KEY]='ArchiveJobHistory'
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_AUDIT_LOGS] @noofdays
	END
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_BILLEDINVOICE_INFOS] @noofdays
	END
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_BILLEDINVOICE_SHIPMENTS] @noofdays
	END
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_CUSTOMER_PROFILES] @noofdays
	END
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_INVOICE_CHARGES] @noofdays
	END
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_INVOICE_INFOS] @noofdays
	END
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_INVOICE_SHIPMENTS] @noofdays
	END
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_INVOICESHIPMENT_DETAILS] @noofdays
	END
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_CustomerCountries]  @noofdays
	END
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_REFRENCES] @noofdays
	END
	BEGIN
		EXEC [dbo].[Archive_Delete_Data] @noofarcdays
	END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[InsertInvoiceandShipmentsTableTemps]
	@CountryCode Varchar(2),
	@recid varchar(max),
	@Type Varchar(100)
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	IF(@CountryCode='JP')
	IF(@Type='Invoices')
	INSERT INTO BilledInvoiceInfoTempsJP(CreationTime,CreationUser,ac_no,inv_no,inv_dt,inv_date,due_date,period_dt,type_cd,bill_cont,bill_name,bill_state,bill_city
	,bill_postl,bill_ctry,pmt_term,ust_id,cop_coll,taxable,nontax,tax_amt,bill_amt,inv_curr,filename,cuser,cdate,last_print,no_printed,giro_prt,no_giro,out_queue,sort_fld,way_sort,disp_level,slic_num,cps_pln_nr,cps_pln_dt,cps_pln_na,taxtier,tax_pct,bill_flr
	,bill_sui,bill_sta,processed,orig_type_cd,os_amt,cny_cd,tax_var,batch_no,batch_dt,inv_void,crdt_ind,cd_ind,BAFlag,close_dt,last_print_date,last_print_by,org_inv_date,TAX_ID,EXEMPTION_ID,PROC_TM,OldPresentationRule,HasPrinted,CustomerRuleSumary,FileSequenceNumber
	,IsGeneratedAfterTransform,IsGeneratedAfterDate,ccard_cd,GUI_NO,RCV_NO,IsDeleted,billed_inv_no)
	select getdate(),'System',ac_no,inv_no,inv_dt,inv_date,due_date,period_dt,type_cd,bill_cont,bill_name,bill_state,bill_city
	,bill_postl,bill_ctry,pmt_term,ust_id,cop_coll,taxable,nontax,tax_amt,bill_amt,inv_curr,filename,cuser,cdate,last_print,no_printed,giro_prt,no_giro,out_queue,sort_fld,way_sort,disp_level,slic_num,cps_pln_nr,cps_pln_dt,cps_pln_na,taxtier,tax_pct,bill_flr
	,bill_sui,bill_sta,processed,orig_type_cd,os_amt,cny_cd,tax_var,batch_no,batch_dt,null,crdt_ind,cd_ind,BAFlag,close_dt,last_print_date,last_print_by,org_inv_date,TAX_ID,EXEMPTION_ID,PROC_TM,null,0,null,0
	,0,0,ccard_cd,GUI_NO,RCV_NO,0,inv_no from InvoiceInfos  where cny_cd = 'JP' AND inv_no in (select value from string_split(@recid,','))
	ELSE
	INSERT INTO BilledInvoiceShipmentTempsJP (CreationTime, CreationUser, IsDeleted, recid, inv_no, billed_inv_no, inv_type, exp_dt, exp_date, gcc_num, track_no, track_11, csort, product, service
		, bill_term, zone, mvm_drc, package, actual_wt, bill_wt, wt_msg, ref_1, ref_2, shp_cont, ship_name, ship_city, ship_state, ship_postl, ship_ctry, cons_cont, cons_name, cons_city, cons_state
		, cons_postl, cons_ctry, dest_ctry, freight, discount, acc_201, acc_205, frt_rate, acc_adc, acc_chg, orig_inv, inv_total, desc_goods, decl_value, sur_ups, sur_brok, exchg_rate, exrate_cd
		, custom_no, reason_cd, acct_no, rqst_name, rqst_comp, rqst_phone, rqst_email, trd_cont, trd_name, trd_city, trd_state, trd_postal, trd_ctry, comments, trans_cd, chg_msg, tier_inct, minshp_prc
		, svc_supp, min_pkgwt, frt_taxapl, vartaxamt, ship_flr, ship_sui, ship_sta, cons_flr, cons_sui, cons_sta, ccard_cd, ccard_4dgt, payor, cny_cd, custom_clr, CUSTOM_EXCHG_RATE, IA_CRD_TYP_CD, IA_CRD_NR
		, IA_CRD_ATH_NR, IA_CRD_EXP_DT, recids, slic_num)
    SELECT
    GETDATE() CreationTime, 'SYSTEM' as CreationUser, 0 as IsDeleted, recid, inv_no, inv_no as billed_inv_no, inv_type, exp_dt, exp_date, gcc_num, track_no, track_11, csort, product, service

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[InsertInvoiceChinaToAlips]
(
@InvoiceCdate DATETIME,
@MsCdate int,
@CountryCode VarChar(2)
)
AS
BEGIN
DECLARE @LatestInvoiceCdate DATETIME;
set @LatestInvoiceCdate  = (SELECT top 1 cdate FROM [Lips].[dbo].[tblinv_info] WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE [cdate] > CAST(DATEADD (ms, 
@MsCdate ,@InvoiceCdate ) as DATETIME) and cny_cd = @CountryCode ORDER by cdate)
BEGIN TRY
	BEGIN TRANSACTION 
Insert Into [ALIPSDevelopment].dbo.PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN
    (
	CreationTime
	,ac_no
	,inv_no
	,inv_dt
	,inv_date
	,due_date
	,period_dt
	,type_cd
	,bill_cont
	,bill_name
	,bill_state
	,bill_city
	,bill_postl
	,bill_ctry
	,pmt_term
	,ust_id
	,cop_coll
	,taxable
	,nontax
	,tax_amt
	,bill_amt
	,inv_curr
	,[filename]
	,cuser
	,cdate
	,last_print
	,no_printed
	,giro_prt
	,no_giro
	,out_queue
	,sort_fld
	,way_sort
	,disp_level
	,slic_num
	,cps_pln_nr
	,cps_pln_dt
	,cps_pln_na
	,taxtier
	,tax_pct
	,bill_flr
	,bill_sui
	,bill_sta
	,processed
	,orig_type_cd
	,os_amt
	,cny_cd
	,tax_var
	,batch_no
	,batch_dt
	,void
	,crdt_ind
	,cd_ind
	,BAFlag
	,close_dt
	,last_print_date
	,last_print_by
	,org_inv_date
	,TAX_ID
	,EXEMPTION_ID
	,PROC_TM
	) 
SELECT  DISTINCT
    DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE()) AS [CreationTime]
      ,i.[ac_no]
      ,i.[inv_no]
      ,i.[inv_dt]
      ,i.[inv_date]
      ,i.[due_date]
      ,i.[period_dt]
      ,i.[type_cd]
      ,i.[bill_cont]
      ,i.[bill_name]
      ,i.[bill_state]
      ,i.[bill_city]
      ,i.[bill_postl]
      ,i.[bill_ctry]
      ,i.[pmt_term]
      ,i.[ust_id]
      ,i.[cop_coll]
      ,i.[taxable]
      ,i.[nontax]
      ,i.[tax_amt]
      ,i.[bill_amt]
      ,i.[inv_curr]
      ,i.[filename]
      ,i.[cuser]
      ,i.[cdate]
      ,i.[last_print]
      ,i.[no_printed]
      ,i.[giro_prt]
      ,i.[no_giro]
      ,i.[out_queue]
      ,i.[sort_fld]
      ,i.[way_sort]
      ,i.[disp_level]
      ,i.[slic_num]
      ,i.[cps_pln_nr]
      ,i.[cps_pln_dt]
      ,i.[cps_pln_na]
      ,i.[taxtier]
      ,i.[tax_pct]
      ,i.[bill_flr]
      ,i.[bill_sui]
      ,i.[bill_sta]
      ,'' AS [processed]
      ,'' AS [orig_type_cd]
      ,i.[os_amt]
      ,i.[cny_cd]
      ,i.[tax_var]
      ,i.[batch_no]
      ,i.[batch_dt]
      ,i.[void]
      ,i.[crdt_ind]
      ,i.[cd_ind]
      ,i.[BAFlag]
      ,i.[close_dt]
      ,i.[last_print_date]
      ,i.[last_print_by]
      ,i.[org_inv_date]
      ,i.[TAX_ID]
      ,i.[EXEMPTION_ID]
      ,'' AS [PROC_TM]
FROM [Lips].[dbo].[tblinv_info] i with(nolock)
LEFT JOIN [ALIPSDevelopment].dbo.PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN [pi] with(nolock) on i.inv_no = [pi].inv_no and i.cny_cd = [pi].cny_cd and i.cny_cd = @CountryCode
WHERE i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate  AND i.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode AND [pi].inv_no IS NULL 
INSERT INTO [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN (
	 CreationTime
	,recid
	,inv_no
	,inv_type
	,exp_dt
	,exp_date
	,gcc_num
	,track_no
	,track_11
	,csort
	,product
	,service
	,bill_term
	,zone
	,mvm_drc
	,package
	,actual_wt
	,bill_wt
	,wt_msg
	,ref_1
	,ref_2
	,shp_cont
	,ship_name
	,ship_city
	,ship_state
	,ship_postl
	,ship_ctry
	,cons_cont
	,cons_name
	,cons_city
	,cons_state
	,cons_postl
	,cons_ctry
	,dest_ctry
	,freight
	,discount
	,acc_201
	,acc_205
	,frt_rate
	,acc_adc
	,acc_chg
	,orig_inv
	,inv_total
	,desc_goods
	,decl_value
	,sur_ups
	,sur_brok
	,exchg_rate
	,exrate_cd
	,custom_no
	,reason_cd
	,acct_no
	,rqst_name
	,rqst_comp
	,rqst_phone
	,rqst_email
	,trd_cont
	,trd_name
	,trd_city
	,trd_state
	,trd_postal
	,trd_ctry
	,comments
	,trans_cd
	,chg_msg
	,tier_inct
	,minshp_prc
	,svc_supp
	,min_pkgwt
	,frt_taxapl
	,vartaxamt
	,ship_flr
	,ship_sui
	,ship_sta
	,cons_flr
	,cons_sui
	,cons_sta
	,ccard_cd
	,ccard_4dgt
	,payor
	,cny_cd
	,custom_clr
	,CUSTOM_EXCHG_RATE
	,IA_CRD_TYP_CD
	,IA_CRD_NR
	,IA_CRD_ATH_NR
	,IA_CRD_EXP_DT	
	)
SELECT DISTINCT 
	 DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE())	as CreationTime
	,s.recid
	,s.inv_no
	,s.inv_type
	,s.exp_dt
	,s.exp_date
	,s.gcc_num
	,s.track_no
	,s.track_11
	,s.csort
	,s.product
	,s.service
	,s.bill_term
	,s.zone
	,s.mvm_drc
	,s.package
	,s.actual_wt
	,s.bill_wt
	,s.wt_msg
	,s.ref_1
	,s.ref_2
	,s.shp_cont
	,s.ship_name
	,s.ship_city
	,s.ship_state
	,s.ship_postl
	,s.ship_ctry
	,s.cons_cont
	,s.cons_name
	,s.cons_city
	,s.cons_state
	,s.cons_postl
	,s.cons_ctry
	,s.dest_ctry
	,s.freight
	,s.discount
	,s.acc_201
	,s.acc_205
	,s.frt_rate
	,s.acc_adc
	,s.acc_chg
	,s.orig_inv
	,s.inv_total
	,s.desc_goods
	,s.decl_value
	,s.sur_ups
	,s.sur_brok
	,s.exchg_rate
	,s.exrate_cd
	,s.custom_no
	,s.reason_cd
	,s.acct_no
	,s.rqst_name
	,s.rqst_comp
	,s.rqst_phone
	,s.rqst_email
	,s.trd_cont
	,s.trd_name
	,s.trd_city
	,s.trd_state
	,s.trd_postal
	,s.trd_ctry
	,s.comments
	,s.trans_cd
	,s.chg_msg
	,s.tier_inct
	,s.minshp_prc
	,s.svc_supp
	,s.min_pkgwt
	,s.frt_taxapl
	,s.vartaxamt
	,s.ship_flr
	,s.ship_sui
	,s.ship_sta
	,s.cons_flr
	,s.cons_sui
	,s.cons_sta
	,s.ccard_cd
	,s.ccard_4dgt
	,s.payor
	,s.cny_cd
	,s.custom_clr
	,s.CUSTOM_EXCHG_RATE
	,s.[IA_CRD_TYP_CD]
    ,s.[IA_CRD_NR]
    ,s.[IA_CRD_ATH_NR]
    ,s.[IA_CRD_EXP_DT]  
	FROM [Lips].[dbo].[tblinv_ship] s with(nolock) 
	JOIN [Lips].[dbo].[tblinv_info] i with(nolock) on i.inv_no = s.inv_no and i.cny_cd = s.cny_cd 
	LEFT JOIN [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN shipment with(nolock) on s.recid = shipment.recid AND s.cny_cd = shipment.cny_cd and s.inv_no = shipment.inv_no
	WHERE shipment.recid IS NULL AND shipment.cny_cd IS NULL AND shipment.inv_no is null AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate
INSERT INTO [ALIPSDevelopment].dbo.PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN (
	CreationTime
	,recid
	,bill_wt
	,wt_unt
	,s_length
	,s_width
	,s_height
	,unit_meas
	,ref_track
	,k_length
	,k_width
	,k_height
	,uom_cd
	,cny_cd
	,slic_num 
 )
 SELECT 
	DISTINCT 
	 DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE())	
	  ,d.[recid]
      ,d.[bill_wt]
      ,d.[wt_unt]
      ,d.[s_length]
      ,d.[s_width]
      ,d.[s_height]
      ,d.[unit_meas]
      ,d.[ref_track]
      ,d.[k_length]
      ,d.[k_width]
      ,d.[k_height]
      ,d.[uom_cd]
      ,s.[cny_cd]
      ,i.[slic_num]
	FROM [LIPS].[dbo].[tblinv_ship_det] d with(nolock)
	JOIN [Lips].[dbo].[tblinv_ship] s with(nolock) ON d.[recid] = s.[recID] and (d.ref_track = s.track_no or d.ref_track != s.track_no )
	JOIN [Lips].[dbo].[tblinv_info] i with(nolock) ON s.[inv_no] = i.[inv_no] and i.cny_cd = s.cny_cd
	LEFT JOIN [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN pd with(nolock) ON pd.[recid] = d.[recid] AND pd.cny_cd = d.cny_cd and pd.ref_track = d.ref_track AND pd.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode	
	WHERE pd.recid is null AND pd.ref_track is null AND  i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate AND i.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode AND s.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode
Insert Into	[ALIPSDevelopment].[Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN (
		 CreationTime		
		,recid	
		,ccode	
		,camount	
		,publ_amt	
		,inct_amt	
		,chg_taxapl	
		,acc_tier	
		,acc_tax	
		,exp_msg	
		,info_ir	
		,track_nr	
		,duty_amt	
		,cny_cd	
		,slic_num
		 )
	SELECT DISTINCT 
		 DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE())	
		,pc.recid	
		,pc.ccode	
		,pc.camount	
		,pc.publ_amt	
		,pc.inct_amt	
		,pc.chg_taxapl	
		,pc.acc_tier	
		,pc.acc_tax	
		,pc.exp_msg	
		,pc.info_ir	
		,pc.track_nr	
		,pc.duty_amt	
		,s.cny_cd	
		,i.slic_num 	
	FROM [Lips].[dbo].[tblinv_chgs] pc with(nolock)	
	JOIN [Lips].[dbo].[tblinv_ship] s with(nolock) ON pc.recid = s.recid and s.cny_cd = s.cny_cd
	JOIN [Lips].[dbo].[tblinv_info] i with(nolock) on i.inv_no = s.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd	
    LEFT JOIN [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN c with(nolock) ON pc.recid = c.recid AND pc.ccode = c.ccode AND pc.info_ir = c.info_ir AND pc.track_nr = c.track_nr	
	WHERE i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND i.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)
	COMMIT TRANSACTION 
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
 IF ((XACT_STATE()) = -1 OR ERROR_NUMBER() Is not NULL )
        BEGIN  
            PRINT  N'The transaction is in an uncommittable state.' +  
                    'Rolling back transaction.'  
            ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;  
        END;  
        IF ((XACT_STATE()) = 1 AND  ERROR_NUMBER() Is NULL)
        BEGIN  
            PRINT N'The transaction is committable.' +  
                'Committing transaction.'  
            COMMIT TRANSACTION;     
        END
END CATCH
END

	JOIN [dbLips].[dbo].[tblinv_info] i with(nolock) on i.inv_no = s.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd

    ,s.[IA_CRD_ATH_NR]
    ,s.[IA_CRD_EXP_DT]  
	FROM [dbLips].[dbo].[tblinv_ship] s with(nolock) 
	JOIN [dbLips].[dbo].[tblinv_info] i with(nolock) on i.inv_no = s.inv_no and i.cny_cd = s.cny_cd 
	LEFT JOIN [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsKR shipment with(nolock) on s.recid = shipment.recid AND s.cny_cd = shipment.cny_cd and s.inv_no = shipment.inv_no
	WHERE shipment.recid IS NULL AND shipment.cny_cd IS NULL AND shipment.inv_no is null AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate
INSERT INTO [ALIPSDevelopment].dbo.PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsKR (
	CreationTime
	,recid
	,bill_wt
	,wt_unt
	,s_length

	,s_height

	,ref_track
	,k_length
	,k_width
	,k_height
	,uom_cd
	,cny_cd
	,slic_num 
 )
 SELECT 
	DISTINCT 
	 DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE())	
	  ,d.[recid]
      ,d.[bill_wt]
      ,d.[wt_unt]
      ,d.[s_length]
      ,d.[s_width]
      ,d.[s_height]
      ,d.[unit_meas]
      ,d.[ref_track]
      ,d.[k_length]
      ,d.[k_width]
      ,d.[k_height]
      ,d.[uom_cd]
      ,s.[cny_cd]
      ,i.[slic_num]
	FROM [dbLips].[dbo].[tblinv_ship_det] d with(nolock)
	JOIN [dbLips].[dbo].[tblinv_ship] s with(nolock) ON d.[recid] = s.[recID] and (d.ref_track = s.track_no or d.ref_track != s.track_no )
	JOIN [dbLips].[dbo].[tblinv_info] i with(nolock) ON s.[inv_no] = i.[inv_no] and i.cny_cd = s.cny_cd
	LEFT JOIN [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsKR pd with(nolock) ON pd.[recid] = d.[recid] AND pd.cny_cd = d.cny_cd and pd.ref_track = d.ref_track AND pd.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode	
	WHERE pd.recid is null AND pd.ref_track is null AND  i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate AND i.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode AND s.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode
Insert Into	[ALIPSDevelopment].[Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsKR (
		 CreationTime		
		,recid	
		,ccode	
		,camount	
		,publ_amt	
		,inct_amt	
		,chg_taxapl	
		,acc_tier	
		,acc_tax	
		,exp_msg	
		,info_ir	
		,track_nr	
		,duty_amt	
		,cny_cd	
		,slic_num
		 )
	SELECT DISTINCT 
		 DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE())	
		,pc.recid	
		,pc.ccode	
		,pc.camount	
		,pc.publ_amt	
		,pc.inct_amt	
		,pc.chg_taxapl	
		,pc.acc_tier	
		,pc.acc_tax	
		,pc.exp_msg	
		,pc.info_ir	
		,pc.track_nr	
		,pc.duty_amt	
		,s.cny_cd	
		,i.slic_num 	
	FROM [dbLips].[dbo].[tblinv_chgs] pc with(nolock)	
	JOIN [dbLips].[dbo].[tblinv_ship] s with(nolock) ON pc.recid = s.recid and s.cny_cd = s.cny_cd
	JOIN [dbLips].[dbo].[tblinv_info] i with(nolock) on i.inv_no = s.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd	
    LEFT JOIN [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsKR c with(nolock) ON pc.recid = c.recid AND pc.ccode = c.ccode AND pc.info_ir = c.info_ir AND pc.track_nr = c.track_nr	
	WHERE i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND i.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)
	COMMIT TRANSACTION 
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
 IF ((XACT_STATE()) = -1 OR ERROR_NUMBER() Is not NULL )
        BEGIN  
            PRINT  N'The transaction is in an uncommittable state.' +  
                    'Rolling back transaction.'  
            ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;  
        END;  
        IF ((XACT_STATE()) = 1 AND  ERROR_NUMBER() Is NULL)
        BEGIN  
            PRINT N'The transaction is committable.' +  
                'Committing transaction.'  
            COMMIT TRANSACTION;     
        END
END CATCH
END

	JOIN [dbLips].[dbo].[tblinv_info] i with(nolock) on i.inv_no = s.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd	
    LEFT JOIN [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsTW c with(nolock) ON pc.recid = c.recid AND pc.ccode = c.ccode AND pc.info_ir = c.info_ir AND pc.track_nr = c.track_nr	
	WHERE i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND i.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)

BEGIN CATCH
 IF ((XACT_STATE()) = -1 OR ERROR_NUMBER() Is not NULL )

        IF ((XACT_STATE()) = 1 AND  ERROR_NUMBER() Is NULL)
        BEGIN  
            PRINT N'The transaction is committable.' +  
                'Committing transaction.'

        END

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[InsertInvoiceTempToMainCN]				
   @CountryCode VARCHAR(2)
AS	
BEGIN
	INSERT INTO [dbo].INVOICEINFOS (
	CreationTime
	,CreationUser
	,IsDeleted
	,ac_no
	,inv_no
	,inv_dt
	,inv_date
	,due_date
	,period_dt
	,type_cd
	,bill_cont
	,bill_name
	,bill_state
	,bill_city
	,bill_postl
	,bill_ctry
	,pmt_term
	,ust_id
	,cop_coll
	,taxable
	,nontax
	,tax_amt
	,bill_amt
	,inv_curr
	,[filename]
	,cuser
	,cdate
	,last_print
	,no_printed
	,giro_prt
	,no_giro
	,out_queue
	,sort_fld
	,way_sort
	,disp_level
	,slic_num
	,cps_pln_nr
	,cps_pln_dt
	,cps_pln_na
	,taxtier
	,tax_pct
	,bill_flr
	,bill_sui
	,bill_sta
	,processed
	,orig_type_cd
	,os_amt
	,cny_cd
	,tax_var
	,batch_no
	,batch_dt
	,void
	,crdt_ind
	,cd_ind
	,BAFlag
	,close_dt
	,last_print_date
	,last_print_by
	,org_inv_date
	,TAX_ID
	,EXEMPTION_ID
	,PROC_TM
	,IsBilled
	,ccard_cd
	) 
SELECT DISTINCT
	 DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE())[creationTime]	
	,'SSIS' AS [CreationUser]
	,0 AS [IsDeleted]
	,p.ac_no
	,p.inv_no
	,p.inv_dt
	,p.inv_date
	,p.due_date
	,p.period_dt
	,p.type_cd
	,p.bill_cont
	,p.bill_name
	,p.bill_state
	,p.bill_city
	,p.bill_postl
	,p.bill_ctry
	,p.pmt_term
	,p.ust_id
	,p.cop_coll
	,p.taxable
	,p.nontax
	,p.tax_amt

	,s.tier_inct
	,s.minshp_prc
	,s.svc_supp
	,s.min_pkgwt
	,s.frt_taxapl
	,s.vartaxamt
	,s.ship_flr
	,s.ship_sui
	,s.ship_sta
	,s.cons_flr
	,s.cons_sui
	,s.cons_sta
	,s.ccard_cd
	,s.ccard_4dgt
	,s.payor
	,s.cny_cd
	,s.custom_clr
	,s.CUSTOM_EXCHG_RATE
	,s.[IA_CRD_TYP_CD]
    	,s.[IA_CRD_NR]
    	,s.[IA_CRD_ATH_NR]
    	,s.[IA_CRD_EXP_DT]  
	,0 AS [SequenceNumber]
	,0 AS [BatchNumber]
	,0 AS [IsBilled]
	,s.[slic_num]
	from [dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN s with(nolock) 
	LEFT JOIN [dbo].[InvoiceShipments] shipment with(nolock) on s.recid = shipment.recid AND s.cny_cd = shipment.cny_cd and s.inv_no = shipment.inv_no
	WHERE shipment.recid IS NULL AND shipment.cny_cd IS NULL AND shipment.inv_no is null AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[InvoiceShipmentGroupingData] (
        CreationTime,
        CreationUser,
        Inv_no,
        Cny_cd,
        inv_total,
		TotalShipment
    )
    SELECT DISTINCT
        DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE()) AS [CreationTime],
        'SSIS' AS [CreationUser],
        i.inv_no,
        i.cny_cd,
        SUM(i.inv_total),
		count(*)
    FROM [dbo].[PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN] i WITH (NOLOCK)
    LEFT JOIN [dbo].[InvoiceShipmentGroupingData] sg WITH (NOLOCK) 
        ON i.cny_cd = sg.cny_cd 
        AND i.inv_no = sg.inv_no
    WHERE sg.cny_cd IS NULL 
      AND sg.inv_no IS NULL 
      AND i.cny_cd = @CountryCode
    GROUP BY i.inv_no, i.cny_cd;
	INSERT INTO dbo.InvoiceShipmentDetails(
	CreationTime
	,CreationUser
	,IsDeleted
	,recid
	,bill_wt
	,wt_unt
	,s_length
	,s_width
	,s_height
	,unit_meas
	,ref_track
	,k_length
	,k_width
	,k_height
	,uom_cd
	,cny_cd
	,slic_num 
 )
SELECT 
	DISTINCT 
	 DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE())	
	,'SSIS' as CreationUser
	,0 as IsDeleted
	,p.recid
	,p.bill_wt
	,p.wt_unt
	,p.s_length
	,p.s_width
	,p.s_height
	,p.unit_meas
	,p.ref_track
	,p.k_length
	,p.k_width
	,p.k_height
	,p.uom_cd
	,p.cny_cd
	,p.slic_num 
	FROM [dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN p with(nolock)
	LEFT JOIN [dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] d with(nolock) ON p.[recid] = d.[recid] AND p.cny_cd = d.cny_cd and p.ref_track = d.ref_track		
	WHERE d.recid is null AND d.ref_track is null AND p.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode
	Insert Into	[Dbo].InvoiceCharges	(
		 CreationTime	
		,CreationUser	
		,IsDeleted	
		,recid	
		,ccode	
		,camount	
		,publ_amt	
		,inct_amt	
		,chg_taxapl	
		,acc_tier	
		,acc_tax	
		,exp_msg	
		,info_ir	
		,track_nr	
		,duty_amt	
		,cny_cd	
		,slic_num
		 )
	SELECT DISTINCT 
		 DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE())	
		,'SSIS' as CreationUser	
		,0 as IsDeleted	
		,pc.recid	
		,pc.ccode	
		,pc.camount	
		,pc.publ_amt	
		,pc.inct_amt	
		,pc.chg_taxapl	
		,pc.acc_tier	
		,pc.acc_tax	
		,pc.exp_msg	
		,pc.info_ir	
		,pc.track_nr	
		,pc.duty_amt	
		,pc.cny_cd	
		,pc.slic_num 	
	FROM [Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN pc	with(nolock)	
	LEFT JOIN [dbo].[InvoiceCharges] c with(nolock) ON pc.recid = c.recid AND pc.ccode = c.ccode AND pc.info_ir = c.info_ir AND pc.track_nr = c.track_nr	
	WHERE pc.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)
END

	LEFT JOIN [dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] d with(nolock) ON p.[recid] = d.[recid] AND p.cny_cd = d.cny_cd and p.ref_track = d.ref_track		
	WHERE d.recid is null AND d.ref_track is null AND p.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode

		 CreationTime	
		,CreationUser	
		,IsDeleted

		,pc.cny_cd	
		,pc.slic_num 	
	FROM [Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsJP pc	with(nolock)	
	LEFT JOIN [dbo].[InvoiceCharges] c with(nolock) ON pc.recid = c.recid AND pc.ccode = c.ccode AND pc.info_ir = c.info_ir AND pc.track_nr = c.track_nr	
	WHERE pc.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)

	,s.cny_cd
	,s.custom_clr
	,s.CUSTOM_EXCHG_RATE
	,s.[IA_CRD_TYP_CD]
    	,s.[IA_CRD_NR]
    	,s.[IA_CRD_ATH_NR]
    	,s.[IA_CRD_EXP_DT]  
	,0 AS [SequenceNumber]
	,0 AS [BatchNumber]
	,0 AS [IsBilled]
	,s.[slic_num]
	from [dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsKR s with(nolock) 
	LEFT JOIN [dbo].[InvoiceShipments] shipment with(nolock) on s.recid = shipment.recid AND s.cny_cd = shipment.cny_cd and s.inv_no = shipment.inv_no
	WHERE shipment.recid IS NULL AND shipment.cny_cd IS NULL AND shipment.inv_no is null AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode

        CreationTime,
        CreationUser,
        Inv_no,
        Cny_cd,
		inv_total
    )
    SELECT DISTINCT
        DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE()) AS [CreationTime],
        'SSIS' AS [CreationUser],
        i.inv_no,
        i.cny_cd,
        SUM(i.inv_total) AS [Sum_inv_total]
    FROM [dbo].[PullInvoiceShipmentTempsKR] i WITH (NOLOCK)
    LEFT JOIN [dbo].[InvoiceShipmentGroupingData] sg WITH (NOLOCK) 
        ON i.cny_cd = sg.cny_cd 
        AND i.inv_no = sg.inv_no
    WHERE sg.cny_cd IS NULL 
      AND sg.inv_no IS NULL 
      AND i.cny_cd = @CountryCode
    GROUP BY i.inv_no, i.cny_cd;
	INSERT INTO [dbo].InvoiceShipmentDetails(
	CreationTime
	,CreationUser
	,IsDeleted
	,recid

	,wt_unt

SELECT 
	DISTINCT 
	 DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE())	
	,'SSIS' as CreationUser
	,0 as IsDeleted
	,p.recid
	,p.bill_wt
	,p.wt_unt
	,p.s_length
	,p.s_width
	,p.s_height
	,p.unit_meas
	,p.ref_track
	,p.k_length
	,p.k_width
	,p.k_height
	,p.uom_cd
	,p.cny_cd
	,p.slic_num 
	FROM [dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsKR p with(nolock)
	LEFT JOIN [dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] d with(nolock) ON p.[recid] = d.[recid] AND p.cny_cd = d.cny_cd and p.ref_track = d.ref_track		
	WHERE d.recid is null AND d.ref_track is null AND p.[cny_cd] = @CountryCode
	Insert Into	[Dbo].InvoiceCharges	(
		 CreationTime	
		,CreationUser	
		,IsDeleted	
		,recid	
		,ccode	
		,camount	
		,publ_amt	
		,inct_amt	
		,chg_taxapl	
		,acc_tier	
		,acc_tax	
		,exp_msg	
		,info_ir	
		,track_nr	
		,duty_amt	
		,cny_cd	
		,slic_num
		 )
	SELECT DISTINCT 
		 DATEADD(hour, 8, GETUTCDATE())	
		,'SSIS' as CreationUser	
		,0 as IsDeleted	
		,pc.recid	
		,pc.ccode	
		,pc.camount	
		,pc.publ_amt	
		,pc.inct_amt	
		,pc.chg_taxapl	
		,pc.acc_tier	
		,pc.acc_tax	
		,pc.exp_msg	
		,pc.info_ir	
		,pc.track_nr	
		,pc.duty_amt	
		,pc.cny_cd	
		,pc.slic_num 	
	FROM [Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsKR pc	with(nolock)	
	LEFT JOIN [dbo].[InvoiceCharges] c with(nolock) ON pc.recid = c.recid AND pc.ccode = c.ccode AND pc.info_ir = c.info_ir AND pc.track_nr = c.track_nr	
	WHERE pc.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)
END

	WHERE pc.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND (c.recid is null AND c.[ccode] is null AND c.[info_ir]  is null AND c.[track_nr] is null)

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[InsertShipmentsToTableTemps]
	@CountryCode Varchar(2),
	@recid varchar(max)

	ELSE IF(@CountryCode='TW')

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[INVOICE_CHARGES] (@NoOfMonths INT )
AS
BEGIN
    declare @time nvarchar(100)
	SET @time='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
	INSERT INTO [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message],[Level],[TimeStamp],[Exception],[LogEvent])
	VALUES ('Archival InvoiceCharges Data ' + @time,'Information',GETUTCDATE(),null,@time)
BEGIN TRANSACTION
BEGIN TRY
SET IDENTITY_INSERT ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[InvoiceCharges] ON;
	INSERT INTO ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[InvoiceCharges]
	(
	CreationTime,
	CreationUser,
	LastModificationTime,
	LastModificationUser,
	DeletionTime,
	IsDeleted,
	DeletionUser,
	Id,
	recid,
	ccode,
	camount,
	publ_amt,
	inct_amt,
	chg_taxapl,
	acc_tier,
	acc_tax,
	exp_msg,
	info_ir,
	track_nr,
	duty_amt,
	inv_no,
	billed_inv_no,
	cny_cd,
	slic_num
	)
	SELECT 
	CreationTime,
	CreationUser,
	LastModificationTime,
	LastModificationUser,
	DeletionTime,
	IsDeleted,
	DeletionUser,
	Id,
	recid,
	ccode,
	camount,
	publ_amt,
	inct_amt,
	chg_taxapl,
	acc_tier,
	acc_tax,
	exp_msg,
	info_ir,
	track_nr,
	duty_amt,
	inv_no,
	billed_inv_no,
	cny_cd,
	slic_num
	FROM ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[InvoiceCharges]
	 WHERE CreationTime <= Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	SET IDENTITY_INSERT ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[InvoiceCharges] OFF;
	delete FROM ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[InvoiceCharges]	WHERE CreationTime <= Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
	COMMIT TRANSACTION
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION
		INSERT INTO ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message], [Level], [TimeStamp], [Exception], [LogEvent] )
		VALUES ('Archive Data InvoiceCharges Table, InvoiceCharges: Date=' + GETUTCDATE(), 'Error', GETUTCDATE(), null, ERROR_MESSAGE())
	END CATCH
	END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE  PROCEDURE [dbo].[INVOICE_INFOS] (@NoOfMonths INT )
AS
BEGIN 
declare @time nvarchar(100)
	SET @time='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
	INSERT INTO [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message],[Level],[TimeStamp],[Exception],[LogEvent])
	VALUES ('Archival Invoice Infos Data ' + @time,'Information',GETUTCDATE(),null,@time)
BEGIN TRANSACTION
BEGIN TRY
SET IDENTITY_INSERT ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[InvoiceInfos] ON;
INSERT INTO [ALIPSDevelopment_arc].dbo.[InvoiceInfos]
(
     CreationTime,
     CreationUser,
     LastModificationTime,
     LastModificationUser,
     DeletionTime,
     IsDeleted,
     DeletionUser,
     id,
     ac_no,
	 inv_no,
	 inv_dt,
	 inv_date,
	 due_date,
	 period_dt,

	 [filename],

	 cps_pln_nr,
	 cps_pln_dt,
	 cps_pln_na,
	 taxtier,
	 tax_pct,
	 bill_flr,
	 bill_sui,
	 bill_sta,
	 processed,
	 orig_type_cd,
	 os_amt,
	 cny_cd,
	 tax_var,
	 batch_no,
	 batch_dt,
	 void,
	 crdt_ind,
	 cd_ind,
	 BAFlag,
	 close_dt,
	 last_print_date,
	 last_print_by,
	 org_inv_date,
	 TAX_ID,
	 EXEMPTION_ID,
	 PROC_TM,
	 IsBilled
	 )
 SELECT 
     CreationTime,
     CreationUser,
     LastModificationTime,
     LastModificationUser,
     DeletionTime,
     IsDeleted,
     DeletionUser,
     id,
     ac_no,
	 inv_no,
	 inv_dt,
	 inv_date,
	 due_date,
	 period_dt,
	 type_cd,
	 bill_cont,
	 bill_name,
	 bill_state,
	 bill_city,
	 bill_postl,
	 bill_ctry,
	 pmt_term,
	 ust_id,
	 cop_coll,
	 taxable,
	 nontax,
	 tax_amt,
	 bill_amt,
	 inv_curr,
	 [filename],
	 cuser,
	 cdate,
	 last_print,
	 no_printed,
	 giro_prt,
	 no_giro,
	 out_queue,
	 sort_fld,
	 way_sort,
	 disp_level,
	 slic_num,
	 cps_pln_nr,
	 cps_pln_dt,
	 cps_pln_na,
	 taxtier,
	 tax_pct,
	 bill_flr,
	 bill_sui,
	 bill_sta,
	 processed,
	 orig_type_cd,
	 os_amt,
	 cny_cd,
	 tax_var,
	 batch_no,
	 batch_dt,
	 void,
	 crdt_ind,
	 cd_ind,
	 BAFlag,
	 close_dt,
	 last_print_date,
	 last_print_by,
	 org_inv_date,
	 TAX_ID,
	 EXEMPTION_ID,
	 PROC_TM,
	 IsBilled
  FROM  [ALIPSDevelopment].dbo.[InvoiceInfos] 
  WHERE CreationTime <= Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
  SET IDENTITY_INSERT ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[InvoiceInfos] OFF;
  delete FROM  [ALIPSDevelopment].dbo.[InvoiceInfos] WHERE CreationTime <= Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
COMMIT TRANSACTION
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION
		INSERT INTO ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message], [Level], [TimeStamp], [Exception], [LogEvent] )
		VALUES ('Archive Data InvoiceInfos Table, InvoiceInfos: Date=' + GETUTCDATE(), 'Error', GETUTCDATE(), null, ERROR_MESSAGE())
	END CATCH
	END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[INVOICESHIPMENT_DETAILS] (@NoOfMonths INT)
AS
BEGIN
  	declare @time nvarchar(100)
	SET @time='Start at ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), GETUTCDATE(), 120)
	INSERT INTO [ALIPSDevelopment].[dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message],[Level],[TimeStamp],[Exception],[LogEvent])
	VALUES ('Archival Invoice Shipment Details Data ' + @time,'Information',GETUTCDATE(),null,@time)
BEGIN TRANSACTION
BEGIN TRY
SET IDENTITY_INSERT ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] ON;
  INSERT INTO ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails]
  (
      CreationTime,
	  CreationUser,
	  LastModificationTime,
	  LastModificationUser,
	  DeletionTime,
	  IsDeleted,
	  DeletionUser,
	  Id,
	  recid,
	  bill_wt,
	  wt_unt,
	  s_length,
	  s_width,
	  s_height,
	  unit_meas,
	  ref_track,
	  k_length,
	  k_width,
	  k_height,
	  uom_cd,
	  cny_cd,
	  slic_num
	)
	SELECT 
	CreationTime,
	  CreationUser,
	  LastModificationTime,
	  LastModificationUser,
	  DeletionTime,
	  IsDeleted,
	  DeletionUser,
	  Id,
	  recid,
	  bill_wt,
	  wt_unt,
	  s_length,
	  s_width,
	  s_height,
	  unit_meas,
	  ref_track,
	  k_length,
	  k_width,
	  k_height,
	  uom_cd,
	  cny_cd,
	  slic_num
	  FROM ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails]
	  WHERE CreationTime <= Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
  SET IDENTITY_INSERT ALIPSDevelopment_arc.[dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] ON;
  delete FROM ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[InvoiceShipmentDetails] WHERE CreationTime <= Dateadd(month,-@NoOfMonths,GETDATE())
    COMMIT TRANSACTION
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION
		INSERT INTO ALIPSDevelopment.[dbo].[TBL_LOG]([Message], [Level], [TimeStamp], [Exception], [LogEvent] )
		VALUES ('Archive Data InvoiceShipmentDetails Table, InvoiceShipmentDetails: Date=' + GETUTCDATE(), 'Error', GETUTCDATE(), null, ERROR_MESSAGE())
	END CATCH
	END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[OnDemandExcelQuery]
	@countryCode varchar(2),
	@InvoiceNo Varchar(max)
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	IF(@COUNTRYCODE='CN')
		select  bi.ac_no as '客户账号', bi.inv_no as '账单号码', bi.inv_dt as '账单日期', bs.inv_type as '账单类型', FORMAT(cast(bs.exp_dt as datetime),'MM/dd/yyyy') as '出口日期',
		bs.track_no as '运单号（长', bs.track_11 as '运单号（短）', bs.product as '货物类型', bs.service as '服务等级', bs.bill_term as '付款方式', 
		bs.package as '包裹数量', bs.actual_wt as '实际重量', bs.bill_wt as '计费重量', bs.ref_1 as '参考编码 1', bs.ref_2 as '参考编码 2', 
		bs.shp_cont as '寄件人', bs.ship_name as '寄件公司', bs.ship_ctry as '寄件国家', bs.cons_cont as '收件人', bs.cons_name as '收件公司',
		bs.cons_ctry as '收件国家', bs.inv_total as '应收费用', bs.freight as '运费', bs.discount as '普通折扣', bs.sur_ups as '运费附加费', 
		bs.sur_brok as '税金附加费', bs.tier_inct as '阶梯折扣', ic.fuel_surcharges as '燃油附加费', ic.peak_surcharges as '旺季附加费', 
		(ic.address_correction + bs.acc_adc) as '更改地址',  ic.additional_handling as '附加手续费', ic.other_charges as '其他附加费' from billedinvoiceinfos bi 
		inner join billedinvoiceshipments bs on bs.inv_no = bi.inv_no 
		left join (select recid, sum(case when(ccode = 'FSC') then camount ELSE 0 END) as fuel_surcharges,  sum(case when((ccode = 'PSC' or ccode = 'PSR')) then camount ELSE 0 END) as peak_surcharges, 
		sum(case when(ccode = 'ADC') then(camount) ELSE 0 END) as address_correction, sum(case when(ccode = 'AHC') then camount ELSE 0 END) as additional_handling, 
		sum(case when((ccode != 'FSC' and ccode != 'PSC'  and ccode != 'PSR' and ccode != 'ADC' and ccode != 'AHC')) then camount ELSE 0 END) as other_charges 
		from invoicecharges  group by recid) ic on ic.recid = bs.recid where bi.cny_cd=@CountryCode and bi.inv_no = @InvoiceNo
	 ELSE 
		select  bi.ac_no as 'Account Number', bi.inv_no as 'Invoice Number', bi.inv_dt as 'Invoice Date', bs.inv_type as 'Invoice Type', FORMAT(cast(bs.exp_dt as datetime),'MM/dd/yyyy') as 'Transaction Date',
		bs.track_no as 'Tracking Number', bs.track_11 as 'Shipment Number', bs.product as 'Container Type', bs.service as 'Service Level', bs.bill_term as 'Bill Term',
		bs.package as 'Package Quantity', bs.actual_wt as 'Entered Weight', bs.bill_wt as 'Billable Weight', bs.ref_1 as 'Shipment Reference Number 1', bs.ref_2 as 'Shipment Reference Number 2',
		bs.shp_cont as 'Sender Name', bs.ship_name as 'Sender Company Name', bs.ship_ctry as 'Sender Country', bs.cons_cont as 'Receiver Name', bs.cons_name as 'Receiver Company Name',
		bs.cons_ctry as 'Receiver Country', bs.inv_total as 'Invoice Amount', bs.freight as 'Freight Charge Amount', bs.discount as 'Basic Incentive Amount', bs.sur_ups as 'Freight Accessorial Surcharge',
		bs.sur_brok as 'Duty/Tax and Brokerage Accessorial Surcharge', bs.tier_inct as 'Tier Incentive Amount', ic.fuel_surcharges as 'Fuel Surcharge', ic.peak_surcharges as 'Peak Surcharge',
		(ic.address_correction + bs.acc_adc) as 'Address Correction',  ic.additional_handling as 'Additional Handling', ic.other_charges as 'Other Surcharges' from billedinvoiceinfos bi
		inner join billedinvoiceshipments bs on bs.inv_no = bi.inv_no
		left join (select recid, sum(case when(ccode = 'FSC') then camount ELSE 0 END) as fuel_surcharges,  sum(case when((ccode = 'PSC' or ccode = 'PSR')) then camount ELSE 0 END) as peak_surcharges,
		sum(case when(ccode = 'ADC') then(camount) ELSE 0 END) as address_correction, sum(case when(ccode = 'AHC') then camount ELSE 0 END) as additional_handling,
		sum(case when((ccode != 'FSC' and ccode != 'PSC'  and ccode != 'PSR' and ccode != 'ADC' and ccode != 'AHC')) then camount ELSE 0 END) as other_charges
		from invoicecharges  group by recid) ic on ic.recid = bs.recid where bi.cny_cd=@CountryCode and bi.inv_no = @InvoiceNo
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PullingAuditlogsCN]			
   @countryCode varchar(2),
   @LatestInvoiceCdate DATETIME
  AS
	BEGIN
Declare @TotalInvoiceInfos Int = 0;
Declare @TotalInvoiceCharges Int = 0;
Declare @TotalInvoiceShipments Int = 0;
Declare @TotalInvoiceShipmentDetails Int = 0;
Set @TotalInvoiceInfos = (SELECT count(1) FROM PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN WHERE cny_cd = @CountryCode AND cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate)
Set @TotalInvoiceCharges = (
 SELECT count(1) FROM PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN c
 JOIN PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN s on c.recid = s.recid and c.cny_cd = s.cny_cd 
 JOIN PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN i on i.inv_no = s.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
WHERE i.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate
)
Set @TotalInvoiceShipmentDetails = ( 
SELECT count(1) FROM PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN c
 JOIN PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN s on c.recid = s.recid and c.cny_cd = s.cny_cd 
 JOIN PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN i on i.inv_no = s.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
WHERE i.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate
)
Set @TotalInvoiceShipments  = ( SELECT count(1) FROM PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN s
 JOIN PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN i on i.inv_no = s.inv_no and s.cny_cd = i.cny_cd
WHERE i.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND s.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND i.cdate = @LatestInvoiceCdate
)
Insert Into AuditLogs (CreationTime, CreationUser, ActivityCategory, ActivitySubCategory, Level, ActivityMessage, ActivityMessageDesc, Country)
Select GETDATE(), 'SSIS', N'EBSSynchronization', N'PullData', 'Information', Concat(N'Pull ', @TotalInvoiceInfos , ' rows invoice info in China successful'),Concat('Cdate Invoice: ', CONVERT(VARCHAR, @LatestInvoiceCdate ,121)), @CountryCode
UNION(
Select GETDATE(), 'SSIS', N'EBSSynchronization', N'PullData', 'Information', Concat(N'Pull ', @TotalInvoiceCharges , ' rows invoice charges in China successful'),Concat('Cdate Invoice: ', CONVERT(VARCHAR, @LatestInvoiceCdate ,121)), @CountryCode
)
UNION(
Select GETDATE(), 'SSIS', N'EBSSynchronization', N'PullData', 'Information', Concat(N'Pull ', @TotalInvoiceShipmentDetails , ' rows invoice shipment details in China successful'),Concat('Cdate Invoice: ', CONVERT(VARCHAR, @LatestInvoiceCdate ,121)), @CountryCode
)
UNION(
Select GETDATE(), 'SSIS', N'EBSSynchronization', N'PullData', 'Information', Concat(N'Pull ', @TotalInvoiceShipments , ' rows invoice shipments in China successful'),Concat('Cdate Invoice: ', CONVERT(VARCHAR, @LatestInvoiceCdate ,121)), @CountryCode
)
	END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[PullInvoiceChinaOnlineCN]			
   @countryCode varchar(2),
   @EBSLatestExecutionTime DATETIME,
   @InvoiceInfosCount INT,
   @ShipmentCount INT,
   @ShipmentDetailCount INT,
   @ChargesCount INT   
  AS
	BEGIN
		declare @infoscount int, @shipcount int, @shipdtlcount int, @invchargescount int, @invnoshipmentcount int, @latestcdate datetime,
		@failedatacnt int, @shipmentrecidcnt int, @chrgsrecidcnt int, @errtext varchar(200), @inv_nos nvarchar(MAX)
		set @infoscount = (select count(*) from [dbo].PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN where cny_cd=@countryCode)
		set @shipcount = (select count(*) from [dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN where cny_cd=@countryCode)
		set @shipdtlcount = (select count(*) from [dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsCN where cny_cd=@countryCode)
		set @invchargescount = (select count(*) from [Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN where cny_cd=@countryCode)
		set @invnoshipmentcount = (select count(distinct(inv_no)) from [dbo].[PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN] where inv_no in 
(select inv_no from [dbo].[PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN] where cny_cd=@countryCode))
		set @shipmentrecidcnt = (select count(distinct(recid)) from [dbo].[PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN]  where inv_type not in('TID', 'ADC')
		and sur_ups>0)
		set @chrgsrecidcnt = (select count(*) from (select pc.recid, sum(pc.camount) as camount, ps.sur_ups, ps.sur_brok, ps.acc_adc, ((ps.sur_ups + ps.sur_brok) - ps.acc_adc) as tot  from [dbo].[PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN] pc join (select recid,sum(sur_ups) as sur_ups, sum(case when sur_brok is not null  then sur_brok else 0 end ) as sur_brok, sum(case when acc_adc is not null  then acc_adc else 0 end ) as acc_adc 
from [dbo].[PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN] where inv_type not in('TID', 'ADC') and sur_ups>0 
group by recid) as ps  on ps.recid = pc.recid group by pc.recid,ps.sur_ups, ps.sur_brok, ps.acc_adc) as temp where camount=tot)
	set @inv_nos = ''
	if(@infoscount >0 AND @shipcount >0  AND @invnoshipmentcount = @infoscount 
		AND @shipmentrecidcnt = @chrgsrecidcnt)
		BEGIN	
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[InsertInvoiceTempToMainCN] @countryCode
			END
			BEGIN 
				EXEC [dbo].[DeleteInvoiceTableTempsCN] @countryCode 
			END
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[Settings]
				SET [Value] = ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 CASE WHEN [cdate] IS NULL THEN @EBSLatestExecutionTime 
				ELSE CAST([cdate] AS DATETIME) END AS LatestInvoiceCdate FROM [dbo].InvoiceInfos 
				WHERE  cny_cd = @countryCode ORDER BY cdate DESC),@EBSLatestExecutionTime)
				WHERE [TYPE] = CONCAT('EBSLatestExecutionTime',@countryCode)
				set @latestcdate = (SELECT TOP 1 CAST([cdate] AS DATETIME) AS LatestInvoiceCdate FROM [dbo].InvoiceInfos 
				WHERE  cny_cd = @countryCode ORDER BY cdate DESC)
				delete from [dbo].[EBSPullingFailedData] where CAST([Cdate] AS DATETIME)=@latestcdate and cny_cd=@countryCode 
			END
		END	
	ELSE
		BEGIN 
			set @latestcdate = (SELECT TOP 1 CASE WHEN [cdate] IS NULL THEN @EBSLatestExecutionTime 
				ELSE CAST([cdate] AS DATETIME) END AS LatestInvoiceCdate FROM [dbo].[PullInvoiceInfoTempsCN] 
				WHERE  cny_cd = @countryCode ORDER BY cdate DESC)
			set @failedatacnt = (select count(*) from [dbo].[EBSPullingFailedData] where CAST([Cdate] AS DATETIME)=@latestcdate and cny_cd=@countryCode)
			IF (@invnoshipmentcount != @infoscount )
				BEGIN
				set @errtext = 'Invoice infos count and invoice shipment count is not matching'
				END
			else IF(@shipmentrecidcnt != @chrgsrecidcnt)
				BEGIN
					select @inv_nos = @inv_nos + inv_no + ','  from (select  ps.inv_no, pc.recid, sum(pc.camount) as camount,
					ps.sur_ups, ps.sur_brok, ps.acc_adc, ((ps.sur_ups + ps.sur_brok) - ps.acc_adc) as tot 
					from [dbo].[PullInvoiceChargeTempsCN] pc join (select recid,sum(sur_ups) as sur_ups, sum(case when sur_brok is not null  then sur_brok else 0 end ) as sur_brok, 
					sum(case when acc_adc is not null  then acc_adc else 0 end ) as acc_adc, inv_no from [dbo].[PullInvoiceShipmentTempsCN] 
					where inv_type not in('TID', 'ADC') and sur_ups>0  group by recid,inv_no) as ps  on ps.recid = pc.recid
					group by pc.recid,ps.sur_ups, ps.sur_brok, ps.acc_adc,ps.inv_no) as temp where camount!=tot
					set @errtext = 'Invoice charges are not matching'
				END
			ELSE
				BEGIN
					set @errtext = 'Total tables rows count are not marching'
				END 
			IF(@failedatacnt>0)	
			 BEGIN 
				update [dbo].[EBSPullingFailedData] set isEmailSent=0, reason=@errtext,inv_nos=@inv_nos, LastUpdateTime=GETUTCDATE()
				where CAST([Cdate] as datetime)=@latestcdate and cny_cd=@countryCode   
			 END
			ELSE
			 BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[EBSPullingFailedData] (Cdate, cny_cd, reason,inv_nos, creationTime)
				VALUES (@latestcdate, @countryCode, @errtext,@inv_nos,GETUTCDATE());
			 END	
			EXEC [dbo].[DeleteInvoiceTableTempsCN] @countryCode
		END
	END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[PullInvoiceChinaOnlineJP]			
   @countryCode varchar(2),
   @EBSLatestExecutionTime DATETIME,
   @InvoiceInfosCount INT,
   @ShipmentCount INT,
   @ShipmentDetailCount INT,
   @ChargesCount INT
  AS
  BEGIN
		declare @infoscount int, @shipcount int, @shipdtlcount int, @invchargescount int,  @invnoshipmentcount int, @latestcdate datetime,
		@failedatacnt int, @shipmentrecidcnt int, @chrgsrecidcnt int, @errtext varchar(200), @inv_nos nvarchar(MAX)
		set @infoscount = (select count(*) from [dbo].PullInvoiceInfoTempsJP where cny_cd=@countryCode)
		set @shipcount = (select count(*) from [dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsJP where cny_cd=@countryCode)
		set @shipdtlcount = (select count(*) from [dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentDetailTempsJP where cny_cd=@countryCode)
		set @invchargescount = (select count(*) from [Dbo].PullInvoiceChargeTempsJP where cny_cd=@countryCode)
		set @invnoshipmentcount = (select count(distinct(inv_no)) from [dbo].PullInvoiceShipmentTempsJP where inv_no in 
(select inv_no from [dbo].PullInvoiceInfoTempsJP where cny_cd=@countryCode))
		set @shipmentrecidcnt = (select count(distinct(recid)) from [dbo].[PullInvoiceShipmentTempsJP]  where inv_type not in('TID', 'ADC') and sur_ups>0)
		set @chrgsrecidcnt = (select count(*) from (select pc.recid, sum(pc.camount) as camount, ps.sur_ups, ps.sur_brok, ps.acc_adc, ((ps.sur_ups + ps.sur_brok) - ps.acc_adc) as tot  from [dbo].[PullInvoiceChargeTempsJP] pc join (select recid,sum(sur_ups) as sur_ups, sum(case when sur_brok is not null  then sur_brok else 0 end ) as sur_brok, sum(case when acc_adc is not null  then acc_adc else 0 end ) as acc_adc 
from [dbo].[PullInvoiceShipmentTempsJP] where inv_type not in('TID', 'ADC') and sur_ups>0
group by recid) as ps  on ps.recid = pc.recid group by pc.recid,ps.sur_ups, ps.sur_brok, ps.acc_adc) as temp where camount=tot)
		set @inv_nos = ''
	if(@infoscount >0 AND @shipcount >0  AND @invnoshipmentcount = @infoscount 
	AND @shipmentrecidcnt = @chrgsrecidcnt)
	 BEGIN
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[InsertInvoiceTempToMainJP] @countryCode
		END
		BEGIN 
			EXEC [dbo].[DeleteInvoiceTableTempsJP] @countryCode 
		END
		BEGIN
			UPDATE [dbo].[Settings]
			SET [Value] = ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 CASE WHEN [cdate] IS NULL THEN @EBSLatestExecutionTime 
			ELSE CAST([cdate] AS DATETIME) END AS LatestInvoiceCdate FROM [dbo].InvoiceInfos 
			WHERE  cny_cd = @countryCode ORDER BY [cdate] DESC),@EBSLatestExecutionTime)
			WHERE [TYPE] = CONCAT('EBSLatestExecutionTime',@countryCode)
			set @latestcdate = (SELECT TOP 1 CAST([cdate] AS DATETIME) AS LatestInvoiceCdate FROM [dbo].InvoiceInfos 
				WHERE  cny_cd = @countryCode ORDER BY cdate DESC)
				delete from [dbo].[EBSPullingFailedData] where CAST([Cdate] AS DATETIME)=@latestcdate and cny_cd=@countryCode 
		END
	  END	
	ELSE
		BEGIN
			set @latestcdate = (SELECT TOP 1 CASE WHEN [cdate] IS NULL THEN @EBSLatestExecutionTime 
				ELSE CAST([cdate] AS DATETIME) END AS LatestInvoiceCdate FROM [dbo].[PullInvoiceInfoTempsJP] 
				WHERE  cny_cd = @countryCode ORDER BY cdate DESC)
			set @failedatacnt = (select count(*) from [dbo].[EBSPullingFailedData] where CAST([Cdate] AS DATETIME)=@latestcdate and cny_cd=@countryCode)
			IF (@invnoshipmentcount != @infoscount )
				BEGIN
				set @errtext = 'Invoice infos count and invoice shipment count is not matching'
				END
			else IF(@shipmentrecidcnt != @chrgsrecidcnt)
				BEGIN
					select @inv_nos = @inv_nos + inv_no + ','  from (select  ps.inv_no, pc.recid, sum(pc.camount) as camount,
					ps.sur_ups, ps.sur_brok, ps.acc_adc, ((ps.sur_ups + ps.sur_brok) - ps.acc_adc) as tot 
					from [dbo].[PullInvoiceChargeTempsJP] pc join (select recid,sum(sur_ups) as sur_ups, sum(case when sur_brok is not null  then sur_brok else 0 end ) as sur_brok, 
					sum(case when acc_adc is not null  then acc_adc else 0 end ) as acc_adc, inv_no from [dbo].[PullInvoiceShipmentTempsJP] 
					where inv_type not in('TID', 'ADC') and sur_ups>0  group by recid,inv_no) as ps  on ps.recid = pc.recid
					group by pc.recid,ps.sur_ups, ps.sur_brok, ps.acc_adc,ps.inv_no) as temp where camount!=tot
					set @errtext = 'Invoice charges are not matching'
				END
			ELSE
				BEGIN
					set @errtext = 'Total tables rows count are not marching'
				END
			IF(@failedatacnt>0)	
			 BEGIN 
				update [dbo].[EBSPullingFailedData] set isEmailSent=0, reason=@errtext,inv_nos=@inv_nos, LastUpdateTime=GETUTCDATE()
				where CAST([Cdate] as datetime)=@latestcdate and cny_cd=@countryCode  
			 END
			ELSE
			 BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[EBSPullingFailedData] (Cdate, cny_cd, reason,inv_nos, creationTime)
				VALUES (@latestcdate, @countryCode, @errtext,@inv_nos,GETUTCDATE());
			 END
			EXEC [dbo].[DeleteInvoiceTableTempsJP] @countryCode 
		END
	END	
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
