CREATE procedure [dbo].[SP_checklistreport]
As
declare @CNpullingcdate datetime, @KRPullingcdate datetime, @TWPullingcdate datetime, @JPPullingcdate datetime,
@CNTrnasformcdate datetime, @KRTransformcdate datetime, @TWTransformcdate datetime, @JPTransformCdate datetime,
@Taxinvdate date,
@cndate date,@krdate date,@twdate date,@jpdate date
select @CNpullingcdate= max(cdate) from InvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='CN'
select @CNpullingcdate as 'CNpullingcdate'
select @KRPullingcdate= max(cdate)  from InvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='kr'
select @KRPullingcdate as 'KRPullingcdate'
select @TWPullingcdate= max(cdate)  from InvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='TW'
select @TWPullingcdate as 'TWPullingcdate'
select @JPPullingcdate= max(cdate)  from InvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='JP'
select @JPPullingcdate as 'JPPullingcdate'
select @CNTrnasformcdate= max(cdate)  from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='CN'
select @CNTrnasformcdate as 'CNTrnasformcdate'
select @KRTransformcdate= max(cdate) from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='kr'
select @KRTransformcdate as 'KRTransformcdate'
select @TWTransformcdate= max(cdate)  from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='TW'
select @TWTransformcdate as 'TWTransformcdate'
select @JPTransformCdate= max(cdate)  from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='JP'
select @JPTransformCdate as 'JPTransformCdate'
select @cndate=max(inv_dt)  from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='CN' and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=1
select @cndate as 'CN Invdate'
select @krdate = max(inv_dt) from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='kr' and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=1
select  @krdate as 'KR Invdate'
select @twdate= max(inv_dt)  from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='TW' and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=1
select @twdate as 'TW Invdate'
select @jpdate= max(inv_dt)  from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='JP' and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=1
select @jpdate as 'JP Invdate'
select top 100 inv_no,status,inv_dt from TaxInvoices  order by id desc
select @Taxinvdate = max(inv_dt) from taxinvoices where status='issued'
select @Taxinvdate as 'Taxinvdate'
select count(inv_dt) 'cn pendingcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos where inv_dt=@cndate and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=0 
and cny_cd='cn'
select count(inv_dt) 'krpendingcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos where inv_dt=@krdate and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=0 
and cny_cd='kr'
select count(inv_dt) 'tw pendingcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos where inv_dt=@twdate and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=0 
and cny_cd='TW'
select count(inv_dt) 'jp pendingcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos where inv_dt=@jpdate and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=0
and cny_cd='JP' 
select count(inv_dt) 'cn uploadedcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos where inv_dt=@cndate and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=1 
and cny_cd='cn'
select count(inv_dt) 'kr uploadedcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos where inv_dt=@krdate and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=1 
and cny_cd='kr'
select count(inv_dt) 'tw uploadedcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos where inv_dt=@twdate and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=1 
and cny_cd='tw'
select count(inv_dt) 'jp uploadedcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos where inv_dt=@jpdate and IsGeneratedAfterTransform=1 
and cny_cd='JP' 
select count(inv_dt)  as 'CN totalcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='CN' and inv_dt=@cndate
select count(inv_dt) as 'KR totalcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='kr' and inv_dt=@krdate
select count(inv_dt) as 'TW totalcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='TW' and inv_dt=@twdate
select count(inv_dt) as 'JP totalcount' from BilledInvoiceInfos with (nolock) where cny_cd='JP' and inv_dt=@jpdate
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_checkListReport2]
(
    @cny_code varchar(6)
)
AS
BEGIN
    DECLARE @iDate DateTime2;
    DECLARE @uploaded int;
    DECLARE @pending int;
    SELECT @iDate = MAX(inv_dt) FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WHERE cny_Cd = @cny_code AND IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 1;
    SELECT @uploaded = COUNT(inv_dt) FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WHERE cny_Cd = @cny_code AND IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 1 AND inv_dt = @iDate;
    SELECT @pending = COUNT(inv_dt) FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WHERE cny_Cd = @cny_code AND IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 0 AND inv_dt = @iDate;
    SELECT
        UploadDate = @iDate,
        UploadedCount = @uploaded,
        PendingCount = @pending,
        TotalCount = (@uploaded + @pending),
        CountryCode = @cny_code;
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_GUI_BILLINVS]
@CountryCode Varchar(2)
AS 
BEGIN
       SELECT  TOP 2000  i.inv_no AS  InvoicrNumber, i.cny_cd as CountryCode, i.ac_no as AccountNo
		FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
		LEFT JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos b with(nolock) on b.inv_no = i.inv_no and b.cny_cd = i.cny_cd 
		WHERE b.inv_no is null AND i.GUI_NO IS NULL AND I.RCV_NO IS NULL  and i.cny_cd = @CountryCode 
		AND i.cdate >= DATEADD(DAY, -CAST((SELECT VALUE FROM SysConfig WHERE [KEY]='Inv_Trnsfrm_TW') AS INT), GETDATE())
		and (i.ac_no not like '8%' and  i.ac_no not like '9%') 
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_SEQUENCENO]
AS BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	DECLARE  @SNO BIGINT
	SELECT @SNO=Sq_No FROM SEQUENCENO
	UPDATE SEQUENCENO SET  Sq_No=@SNO+1
	SELECT cast(YEAR(getDate()) as varchar(10)) + '' + SUBSTRING(CONVERT(nvarchar(6),getdate(), 112),5,2)+ 'S' + right('00000' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@SNO+1),5) AS SQNO
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
 CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_TaxInvoiceQuery]
    @notOptOutVatInvoiceNumbers NVARCHAR(MAX),
    @optOutVatInvoiceNumbers NVARCHAR(MAX),
    @excludeProductTypes NVARCHAR(MAX)
AS
BEGIN
    SELECT bs.inv_no as InvoiceNumber, 
           bs.orig_inv as OriginalInvoiceNumber, 
           bs.recid as RecId, 
           ic.camount as ChargeAmount, 
           ic.acc_tax as TaxAmount,
           ic.ccode as ChargeCode, 
           bs.discount as Discount, 
           bs.freight as Freight, 
           bs.acc_201 as Acc201, 
           bs.acc_205 as Acc205, 
           bs.acc_adc as AccAdc,
           bs.acc_chg as AccCharge, 
           bs.inv_type as InvoiceType, 
           bs.mvm_drc as MovementDirection, 
           bs.tier_inct as TierInct, 
           bs.vartaxamt as VarTaxAmount, 
           bs.track_11 as Tracking11
    FROM BilledInvoiceShipments bs with(nolock)
    LEFT JOIN InvoiceCharges ic  with(nolock) ON ic.recid = bs.recid
    WHERE bs.inv_no IN (SELECT value FROM STRING_SPLIT(@notOptOutVatInvoiceNumbers, ',')) 
       OR (bs.inv_no IN (SELECT value FROM STRING_SPLIT(@optOutVatInvoiceNumbers, ',')) AND bs.product NOT IN (SELECT value FROM STRING_SPLIT(@excludeProductTypes, ',')))
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_UPDATESLICCODE_INVS]
@CountryCode Varchar(2)
AS 
BEGIN
       SELECT  top 500 i.inv_no AS  InvoicrNumber, i.cny_cd as CountryCode
              FROM InvoiceInfos i with(nolock)
              LEFT JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos b with(nolock) on b.inv_no = i.inv_no and b.cny_cd = i.cny_cd 
              AND  i.slic_num not in (select Slic from UPSInformations where CountryCode = @CountryCode)
              WHERE b.inv_no is null and i.cny_cd = @CountryCode AND i.cdate >= DATEADD(DAY, -CAST((SELECT VALUE FROM SysConfig WHERE [KEY]='Inv_Trnsfrm_CN') AS INT), GETDATE())
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[SumChargesChina]			
   @CountryCode Varchar(2), @inv_no varchar(12), @IsUpload bit = 0
  AS
	BEGIN
if(@IsUpload != 0)
BEGIN 
SELECT
    InvoiceTransactions as InvoiceTransactionsRule,
    InvoiceNo,
	sum(adjustments) as Adjustments,
    SUM(WorldwideShipments) as WorldwideShipments,
    SUM(Discount) as Discounts,
    SUM(OtherCharges) + SUM(taxable2NetChargesTotal) +  (SUM(Discount)) - (SUM(WorldwideShipments)) as OtherCharges,
    (SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + (SUM(OtherCharges) + SUM(taxable2NetChargesTotal) +  (SUM(Discount)) - SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + Sum(adjustments) - SUM(Discount) as totalCharges,
    SUM(VAT) as VAT,
    ((SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + (SUM(OtherCharges) + SUM(taxable2NetChargesTotal) +  (SUM(Discount)) - SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + Sum(adjustments) - SUM(Discount)) +SUM(VAT) as totalAmountDue,
    CASE WHEN (((SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + (SUM(OtherCharges) + SUM(taxable2NetChargesTotal) +  (SUM(Discount)) - SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + Sum(adjustments) - SUM(Discount)) +SUM(VAT)) != (SUM(Amount) /count(Amount)) THEN 1 else 0 END as IsMissCharges
    FROM (
  SELECT
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By InvoiceTransactions,InvoiceNo Order by recid) as RowNo,
	recid,
	ChargeType,
    InvoiceNo,
    InvoiceTransactions,
    CASE
    WHEN InvoiceTransactions = 'R008_01' AND (ccode = 'VAT' AND (ChargeType IS NOT NULL AND (ChargeType !='Address Correction' OR ChargeType != 'BLANK' OR ChargeType != 'Transportation'))
    AND NetCharges >= 0 and TaxableCharges >= 0 and NonTaxableCharges >= 0 and Discount >= 0)
    THEN NetCharges
    WHEN InvoiceTransactions in ( 'R004_01','R005_01') AND (ccode = 'VAT' AND (ChargeType IS NOT NULL AND (ChargeType !='Address Correction' OR ChargeType != 'BLANK' OR ChargeType != 'Transportation'))
    AND NetCharges >= 0  and NonTaxableCharges >= 0 )
    THEN NetCharges
    ELSE VAT END as VAT,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R005_01' and (ChargeType IS NOT NULL AND (ChargeType !='Address Correction' OR ChargeType != 'BLANK' OR ChargeType != 'Transportation')) THEN NetCharges ELSE 0 END  as adjustments,
    CASE 
		WHEN InvoiceTransactions in ('R003_01','R004_01') AND (ChargeType ='Address Correction' OR ChargeType = 'BLANK' OR ChargeType = 'Transportation') THEN (TaxableCharges) 
		WHEN InvoiceTransactions = 'R008_01' AND (ChargeType ='Address Correction' OR ChargeType = 'BLANK' OR ChargeType = 'Transportation') THEN transportationTaxable2Total ELSE 0 END  as WorldwideShipments,
    CASE 
		WHEN InvoiceTransactions in ('R003_01','R004_01') AND (ChargeType ='Address Correction' OR ChargeType = 'BLANK' OR ChargeType = 'Transportation') THEN (Discount)
		WHEN InvoiceTransactions = 'R008_01' AND (ChargeType ='Address Correction' OR ChargeType = 'BLANK' OR ChargeType = 'Transportation') THEN transportationDiscountTaxable2Total
	 ELSE 0 END  as	Discount,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions = 'R003_01' OR InvoiceTransactions = 'R004_01' THEN NetCharges ELSE  0 END AS OtherCharges,
	taxable2NetChargesTotal,
	transportationTaxable2Total,
    transportationDiscountTaxable2Total,
    Amount
    FROM (
    SELECT
    CASE
    WHEN (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') THEN 'Transportation'
    WHEN InvoiceType = 'ADC' THEN 'Address Correction'
    WHEN InvoiceType = 'MIS' THEN 'BLANK'
    END as ChargeType,
    NULL ccode,
    FrtTaxapl,
    InvoiceType,
    VAT as VAT,
    CASE
    WHEN  (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') AND 'Y' = 'N'	THEN (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight)
    WHEN  InvoiceType = 'ADC' AND 'Y' = 'N' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccAdc + NonTaxable1DataAccAdc + Taxable1DataDataAccAdc + Taxable2DataAccAdc)
    WHEN  InvoiceType = 'MIS' AND 'Y' = 'N' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccChg + NonTaxable1DataAccChg + Taxable1DataDataAccChg + Taxable2DataAccChg)
    ELSE 0
    END as NonTaxableCharges,
    CASE
    WHEN (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') AND 'Y' ='Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight)
    WHEN  InvoiceType = 'ADC' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccAdc + NonTaxable1DataAccAdc + Taxable1DataDataAccAdc + Taxable2DataAccAdc)
    WHEN  InvoiceType = 'MIS' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccChg + NonTaxable1DataAccChg + Taxable1DataDataAccChg + Taxable2DataAccChg)
    ELSE 0
    END as TaxableCharges,
    CASE
    WHEN InvoiceType != 'MIS' THEN ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
    ELSE 0
    END as Discount,
    CASE
    WHEN InvoiceType = 'ADC' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccAdc + NonTaxable1DataAccAdc + Taxable1DataDataAccAdc + Taxable2DataAccAdc) - ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
    WHEN InvoiceType = 'MIS' THEN Adjustment2DataAccChg + NonTaxable1DataAccChg + Taxable1DataDataAccChg + Taxable2DataAccChg
    WHEN (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') THEN (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight) - ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
    END as NetCharges,
	 CASE
    WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) THEN (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight) - ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2) THEN -1 * (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight) - ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	ELSE 0
    END as taxable2NetChargesTotal,
	CASE
    WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') AND 'Y' ='Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight)
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2) AND  (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') AND 'Y' ='Y' THEN -1 * (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight)
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND  InvoiceType = 'ADC' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccAdc + NonTaxable1DataAccAdc + Taxable1DataDataAccAdc + Taxable2DataAccAdc)
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2)  AND  InvoiceType = 'ADC' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN -1 *(Adjustment2DataAccAdc + NonTaxable1DataAccAdc + Taxable1DataDataAccAdc + Taxable2DataAccAdc)
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND InvoiceType = 'MIS' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccChg + NonTaxable1DataAccChg + Taxable1DataDataAccChg + Taxable2DataAccChg)
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2) AND InvoiceType = 'MIS' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN -1 *(Adjustment2DataAccChg + NonTaxable1DataAccChg + Taxable1DataDataAccChg + Taxable2DataAccChg)
    ELSE 0
    END as transportationTaxable2Total,
	CASE
    WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND InvoiceType != 'MIS'  THEN ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2) AND InvoiceType != 'MIS'  THEN -1 * ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND  InvoiceType != 'MIS' THEN ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2)  AND InvoiceType != 'MIS'  THEN -1 * ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND InvoiceType != 'MIS' THEN ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2) AND InvoiceType != 'MIS' THEN -1 * ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
    ELSE 0
    END as transportationDiscountTaxable2Total,
    RecId,
    RecIds,
    TrackingNo,
    InvoiceTransactions,
    InvoiceNo,
    Amount
    FROM (
    SELECT
    BilledInvoiceShipments.recid as RecId,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no as TrackingNo,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type as InvoiceType,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.recids as RecIds,
    InvoiceTransactions.China as InvoiceTransactions,
    BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_no as InvoiceNo,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl as  FrtTaxapl,
  CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201 END as Taxable2DataAcc201,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201 ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataAcc201,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201 ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataAcc201,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201 ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataAcc201,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN  (-BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205 END as Taxable2DataAcc205,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR  BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205 ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataAcc205,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205 ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataAcc205,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205 ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataAcc205,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt END as Taxable2DataVarTaxAmt,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataVartaxamt,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataVartaxamt,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataAvartaxamt,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc END as Taxable2DataAccAdc,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd)THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataAccAdc,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataAccAdc,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataAccAdc,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg END as Taxable2DataAccChg,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataAccChg,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataAccChg,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataAccChg,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.freight) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.freight END as Taxable2DataFreight,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.freight ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataFreight,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.freight ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataFreight,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.freight ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataFreight,
    (CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.discount)	ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.discount END) as Taxable2DataDiscount,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.discount ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataDiscount,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.discount ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataDiscount,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.discount ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataDiscount,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct END as Taxable2DataTierInct,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataTierInct,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataTierInct,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataTierInct,
	BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt - BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_var as Amount,
	CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.china != 'R003_01' THEN vartaxamt ELSE 0 END  VAT
    FROM BilledInvoiceInfos
    JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd AND BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.billed_inv_no
    LEFT JOIN InvoiceTransactions ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTransactions.InvoiceType AND BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type = InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType
    WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = 'CN' AND billedinvoiceinfos.inv_no = @inv_no and IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 0
    ) AS TEMPS
        UNION(
	SELECT
    ChargeCodes.EnglishDescription as ChargeType,
    InvoiceCharges.ccode as Ccode,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl as FrtTaxapl,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type,
    0 VAT,
	 CASE
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) = 2) AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN 0
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) != 2) AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN 0
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN 0
	 ELSE InvoiceCharges.publ_amt END as NonTaxableCharges,
    CASE
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) = 2) AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN -1 * InvoiceCharges.publ_amt
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) != 2) AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN InvoiceCharges.publ_amt 
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN InvoiceCharges.publ_amt 
	 ELSE 0 END as TaxableCharges,
	CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) = 2) THEN -1 * InvoiceCharges.inct_amt ELSE InvoiceCharges.inct_amt END as Discount ,
	CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) = 2) THEN -1 * InvoiceCharges.camount ELSE InvoiceCharges.camount 
	END as NetCharges,
	CASE 
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) != 2) THEN  InvoiceCharges.camount
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) = 2) THEN -1*( -1 * InvoiceCharges.camount) ELSE 0
	END as taxable2NetChargesTotal,
	0 as transportationTaxable2Total,
	0 as transportationDiscountTaxable2Total,
    InvoiceCharges.recid as RecId,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.recids as RecIds,
    InvoiceCharges.track_nr,
    InvoiceTransactions.China,
    BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no as InvoiceNumber,
	BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt - BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_var as Amount
    FROM  BilledInvoiceInfos
    JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments ON BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_no AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd
    JOIN InvoiceCharges ON BilledInvoiceShipments.recid = InvoiceCharges.recid and BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd = InvoiceCharges.cny_cd
    JOIN ChargeCodes ON InvoiceCharges.ccode = ChargeCodes.ChargeCodes AND InvoiceCharges.cny_cd = ChargeCodes.CountryCode
    LEFT JOIN InvoiceTransactions ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTransactions.InvoiceType AND BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type = InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType
    WHERE
    BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = 'CN' AND ChargeCodes.CountryCode = 'CN' AND billedinvoiceinfos.inv_no = @inv_no and IsGeneratedAfterTransform = 0
    )
    ) as Charges
    ) as TableMain
    GROUP BY TableMain.InvoiceNo, TableMain.InvoiceTransactions
END
ELSE BEGIN 
SELECT
    InvoiceTransactions as InvoiceTransactionsRule,
    InvoiceNo,
	sum(adjustments) as Adjustments,
    SUM(WorldwideShipments) as WorldwideShipments,
    SUM(Discount) as Discounts,
    SUM(OtherCharges) + SUM(taxable2NetChargesTotal) +  (SUM(Discount)) - (SUM(WorldwideShipments)) as OtherCharges,
    (SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + (SUM(OtherCharges) + SUM(taxable2NetChargesTotal) +  (SUM(Discount)) - SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + Sum(adjustments) - SUM(Discount) as totalCharges,
    SUM(VAT) as VAT,
    ((SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + (SUM(OtherCharges) + SUM(taxable2NetChargesTotal) +  (SUM(Discount)) - SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + Sum(adjustments) - SUM(Discount)) +SUM(VAT) as totalAmountDue,
    CASE WHEN (((SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + (SUM(OtherCharges) + SUM(taxable2NetChargesTotal) +  (SUM(Discount)) - SUM(WorldwideShipments)) + Sum(adjustments) - SUM(Discount)) +SUM(VAT)) != (SUM(Amount) /count(Amount)) THEN 1 else 0 END as IsMissCharges
    FROM (
  SELECT
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By InvoiceTransactions,InvoiceNo Order by recid) as RowNo,
	recid,
	ChargeType,
    InvoiceNo,
    InvoiceTransactions,
    CASE
    WHEN InvoiceTransactions = 'R008_01' AND (ccode = 'VAT' AND (ChargeType IS NOT NULL AND (ChargeType !='Address Correction' OR ChargeType != 'BLANK' OR ChargeType != 'Transportation'))
    AND NetCharges >= 0 and TaxableCharges >= 0 and NonTaxableCharges >= 0 and Discount >= 0)
    THEN NetCharges
    WHEN InvoiceTransactions in ( 'R004_01','R005_01') AND (ccode = 'VAT' AND (ChargeType IS NOT NULL AND (ChargeType !='Address Correction' OR ChargeType != 'BLANK' OR ChargeType != 'Transportation'))
    AND NetCharges >= 0  and NonTaxableCharges >= 0 )
    THEN NetCharges
    ELSE VAT END as VAT,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R005_01' and (ChargeType IS NOT NULL AND (ChargeType !='Address Correction' OR ChargeType != 'BLANK' OR ChargeType != 'Transportation')) THEN NetCharges ELSE 0 END  as adjustments,
    CASE 
		WHEN InvoiceTransactions in ('R003_01','R004_01') AND (ChargeType ='Address Correction' OR ChargeType = 'BLANK' OR ChargeType = 'Transportation') THEN (TaxableCharges) 
		WHEN InvoiceTransactions = 'R008_01' AND (ChargeType ='Address Correction' OR ChargeType = 'BLANK' OR ChargeType = 'Transportation') THEN transportationTaxable2Total ELSE 0 END  as WorldwideShipments,
    CASE 
		WHEN InvoiceTransactions in ('R003_01','R004_01') AND (ChargeType ='Address Correction' OR ChargeType = 'BLANK' OR ChargeType = 'Transportation') THEN (Discount)
		WHEN InvoiceTransactions = 'R008_01' AND (ChargeType ='Address Correction' OR ChargeType = 'BLANK' OR ChargeType = 'Transportation') THEN transportationDiscountTaxable2Total
	 ELSE 0 END  as	Discount,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions = 'R003_01' OR InvoiceTransactions = 'R004_01' THEN NetCharges ELSE  0 END AS OtherCharges,
	taxable2NetChargesTotal,
	transportationTaxable2Total,
    transportationDiscountTaxable2Total,
    Amount
    FROM (
    SELECT
    CASE
    WHEN (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') THEN 'Transportation'
    WHEN InvoiceType = 'ADC' THEN 'Address Correction'
    WHEN InvoiceType = 'MIS' THEN 'BLANK'
    END as ChargeType,
    NULL ccode,
    FrtTaxapl,
    InvoiceType,
    VAT as VAT,
    CASE
    WHEN  (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') AND 'Y' = 'N'	THEN (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight)
    WHEN  InvoiceType = 'ADC' AND 'Y' = 'N' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccAdc + NonTaxable1DataAccAdc + Taxable1DataDataAccAdc + Taxable2DataAccAdc)
    WHEN  InvoiceType = 'MIS' AND 'Y' = 'N' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccChg + NonTaxable1DataAccChg + Taxable1DataDataAccChg + Taxable2DataAccChg)
    ELSE 0
    END as NonTaxableCharges,
    CASE
    WHEN (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') AND 'Y' ='Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight)
    WHEN  InvoiceType = 'ADC' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccAdc + NonTaxable1DataAccAdc + Taxable1DataDataAccAdc + Taxable2DataAccAdc)
    WHEN  InvoiceType = 'MIS' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccChg + NonTaxable1DataAccChg + Taxable1DataDataAccChg + Taxable2DataAccChg)
    ELSE 0
    END as TaxableCharges,
    CASE
    WHEN InvoiceType != 'MIS' THEN ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
    ELSE 0
    END as Discount,
    CASE
    WHEN InvoiceType = 'ADC' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccAdc + NonTaxable1DataAccAdc + Taxable1DataDataAccAdc + Taxable2DataAccAdc) - ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
    WHEN InvoiceType = 'MIS' THEN Adjustment2DataAccChg + NonTaxable1DataAccChg + Taxable1DataDataAccChg + Taxable2DataAccChg
    WHEN (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') THEN (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight) - ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
    END as NetCharges,
	 CASE
    WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) THEN (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight) - ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2) THEN -1 * (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight) - ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	ELSE 0
    END as taxable2NetChargesTotal,
	CASE
    WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') AND 'Y' ='Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight)
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2) AND  (InvoiceType != 'MIS' OR InvoiceType != 'ADC') AND 'Y' ='Y' THEN -1 * (Adjustment2DataFreight + NonTaxable1DataFreight + Taxable1DataDataFreight + Taxable2DataFreight)
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND  InvoiceType = 'ADC' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccAdc + NonTaxable1DataAccAdc + Taxable1DataDataAccAdc + Taxable2DataAccAdc)
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2)  AND  InvoiceType = 'ADC' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN -1 *(Adjustment2DataAccAdc + NonTaxable1DataAccAdc + Taxable1DataDataAccAdc + Taxable2DataAccAdc)
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND InvoiceType = 'MIS' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN (Adjustment2DataAccChg + NonTaxable1DataAccChg + Taxable1DataDataAccChg + Taxable2DataAccChg)
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2) AND InvoiceType = 'MIS' AND 'Y' = 'Y' THEN -1 *(Adjustment2DataAccChg + NonTaxable1DataAccChg + Taxable1DataDataAccChg + Taxable2DataAccChg)
    ELSE 0
    END as transportationTaxable2Total,
	CASE
    WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND InvoiceType != 'MIS'  THEN ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2) AND InvoiceType != 'MIS'  THEN -1 * ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND  InvoiceType != 'MIS' THEN ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2)  AND InvoiceType != 'MIS'  THEN -1 * ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 1) AND InvoiceType != 'MIS' THEN ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions ='R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By TrackingNo,InvoiceType ORDER By recids) = 2) AND InvoiceType != 'MIS' THEN -1 * ((Adjustment2DataDiscount + NonTaxable1DataDiscount + Taxable1DataDataDiscount + Taxable2DataDiscount) + (Adjustment2DataTierInct + NonTaxable1DataTierInct + Taxable1DataDataTierInct + Taxable2DataTierInct))
    ELSE 0
    END as transportationDiscountTaxable2Total,
    RecId,
    RecIds,
    TrackingNo,
    InvoiceTransactions,
    InvoiceNo,
    Amount
    FROM (
    SELECT
    BilledInvoiceShipments.recid as RecId,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no as TrackingNo,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type as InvoiceType,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.recids as RecIds,
    InvoiceTransactions.China as InvoiceTransactions,
    BilledInvoiceInfos.inv_no as InvoiceNo,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl as  FrtTaxapl,
  CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201 END as Taxable2DataAcc201,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201 ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataAcc201,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201 ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataAcc201,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_201 ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataAcc201,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN  (-BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205 END as Taxable2DataAcc205,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR  BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205 ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataAcc205,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205 ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataAcc205,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_205 ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataAcc205,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt END as Taxable2DataVarTaxAmt,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataVartaxamt,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataVartaxamt,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.vartaxamt ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataAvartaxamt,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc END as Taxable2DataAccAdc,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd)THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataAccAdc,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataAccAdc,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_adc ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataAccAdc,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg END as Taxable2DataAccChg,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataAccChg,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataAccChg,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.acc_chg ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataAccChg,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.freight) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.freight END as Taxable2DataFreight,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.freight ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataFreight,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.freight ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataFreight,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.freight ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataFreight,
    (CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.discount)	ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.discount END) as Taxable2DataDiscount,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.discount ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataDiscount,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.discount ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataDiscount,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.discount ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataDiscount,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' THEN 0 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By BilledInvoiceShipments.track_no,BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type ORDER By BilledInvoiceShipments.recids) = 2) THEN( -1 * BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct) ELSE BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct END as Taxable2DataTierInct,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R003_01' OR BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type NOT IN (SELECT TransactionType FROM InvoiceTransactions WHERE InvoiceType = BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd) THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct ELSE 0 END as NonTaxable1DataTierInct,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R004_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct ELSE 0 END as Taxable1DataDataTierInct,
    CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R005_01' THEN BilledInvoiceShipments.tier_inct ELSE 0 END as Adjustment2DataTierInct,
	BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt - BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_var as Amount,
	CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.china != 'R003_01' THEN vartaxamt ELSE 0 END  VAT
    FROM BilledInvoiceInfos
    JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments ON BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd AND BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.billed_inv_no
    LEFT JOIN InvoiceTransactions ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTransactions.InvoiceType AND BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type = InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType
    WHERE BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = 'CN' AND billedinvoiceinfos.inv_no = @inv_no
    ) AS TEMPS
        UNION(
	SELECT
    ChargeCodes.EnglishDescription as ChargeType,
    InvoiceCharges.ccode as Ccode,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl as FrtTaxapl,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type,
    0 VAT,
	 CASE
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) = 2) AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN 0
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) != 2) AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN 0
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN 0
	 ELSE InvoiceCharges.publ_amt END as NonTaxableCharges,
    CASE
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) = 2) AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN -1 * InvoiceCharges.publ_amt
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) != 2) AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN InvoiceCharges.publ_amt 
	 WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China != 'R008_01' AND (InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = 'Y' OR (BilledInvoiceShipments.frt_taxapl = 'Y' AND InvoiceCharges.chg_taxapl = '')) THEN InvoiceCharges.publ_amt 
	 ELSE 0 END as TaxableCharges,
	CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) = 2) THEN -1 * InvoiceCharges.inct_amt ELSE InvoiceCharges.inct_amt END as Discount ,
	CASE WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) = 2) THEN -1 * InvoiceCharges.camount ELSE InvoiceCharges.camount 
	END as NetCharges,
	CASE 
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) != 2) THEN  InvoiceCharges.camount
	WHEN InvoiceTransactions.China = 'R008_01' AND (ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION By track_nr, BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd ORDER By InvoiceCharges.recid) = 2) THEN -1*( -1 * InvoiceCharges.camount) ELSE 0
	END as taxable2NetChargesTotal,
	0 as transportationTaxable2Total,
	0 as transportationDiscountTaxable2Total,
    InvoiceCharges.recid as RecId,
    BilledInvoiceShipments.recids as RecIds,
    InvoiceCharges.track_nr,
    InvoiceTransactions.China,
    BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no as InvoiceNumber,
	BilledInvoiceInfos.bill_amt - BilledInvoiceInfos.tax_var as Amount
    FROM  BilledInvoiceInfos
    JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments ON BilledInvoiceInfos.billed_inv_no = BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_no AND BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd
    JOIN InvoiceCharges ON BilledInvoiceShipments.recid = InvoiceCharges.recid and BilledInvoiceShipments.cny_cd = InvoiceCharges.cny_cd
    JOIN ChargeCodes ON InvoiceCharges.ccode = ChargeCodes.ChargeCodes AND InvoiceCharges.cny_cd = ChargeCodes.CountryCode
    LEFT JOIN InvoiceTransactions ON BilledInvoiceInfos.type_cd = InvoiceTransactions.InvoiceType AND BilledInvoiceShipments.inv_type = InvoiceTransactions.TransactionType
    WHERE
    BilledInvoiceInfos.cny_cd = 'CN' AND ChargeCodes.CountryCode = 'CN' AND billedinvoiceinfos.inv_no = @inv_no
    )
    ) as Charges
    ) as TableMain
    GROUP BY TableMain.InvoiceNo, TableMain.InvoiceTransactions
END 
	END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[TransferTransformationData]
@CountryCode Varchar(2)
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	if(@CountryCode='JP')
	BEGIN
		INSERT INTO BilledInvoiceInfos
		SELECT
		GETDATE() [CreationTime]
		,t.[CreationUser]
		,t.[LastModificationTime]
		,t.[LastModificationUser]
		,t.[DeletionTime]
		,t.[IsDeleted]
		,t.[DeletionUser]
		,t.[ac_no]
		,t.[inv_no]
		,t.[billed_inv_no]
		,t.[inv_dt]
		,t.[inv_date]
		,t.[due_date]
		,t.[period_dt]
		,t.[type_cd]
		,t.[bill_cont]
		,t.[bill_name]
		,t.[bill_state]
		,t.[bill_city]
		,t.[bill_postl]
		,t.[bill_ctry]
		,t.[pmt_term]
		,t.[ust_id]
		,t.[cop_coll]
		,t.[taxable]
		,t.[nontax]
		,t.[tax_amt]
		,t.[bill_amt]
		,t.[inv_curr]
		,t.[filename]
		,t.[cuser]
		,t.[cdate]
		,t.[last_print]
		,t.[no_printed]

		,t.[IsGeneratedAfterDate]
		,0 as [IsMissCharges]
		,0 as  [IsRunUpload]
		,0 as [IsEmailSent]
		,0 as Cmb_Invoices
		,0 as IsconsolidationInvoice
		,null as IsConsolidationDate
		,t.ccard_cd
		,t.GUI_NO
		,t.RCV_NO
		FROM [dbo].[BilledInvoiceInfoTempsJP] t WITH(NOLOCK)
		WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM BilledInvoiceInfos  BII WHERE BII.inv_no=T.INV_NO AND T.CNY_CD=BII.CNY_CD)
		INSERT INTO BilledInvoiceShipments

		,t.[IA_CRD_EXP_DT]

		FROM [dbo].[BilledInvoiceShipmentTempsJP] t WITH(NOLOCK)
		WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM BilledInvoiceShipments BIS WHERE BIS.inv_no=T.INV_NO AND BIS.CNY_CD=T.CNY_CD)
		UPDATE C
		SET
		C.INV_NO = S.INV_NO,
		C.BILLED_INV_NO =  S.BILLED_INV_NO,
		C.SLIC_NUM = S.SLIC_NUM,
		C.LastModificationTime = GETDATE(),
		C.LastModificationUser = 'SYSTEM'
		FROM BilledInvoiceShipmentTempsJP S WITH(NOLOCK)
		JOIN INVOICECHARGES C ON S.RECID = C.RECID AND C.CNY_CD = S.CNY_CD
		WHERE C.CNY_CD = 'JP'
		DELETE TEMP FROM BilledInvoiceInfoTempsJP TEMP
		INNER JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos TI WITH(NOLOCK) ON TI.inv_no=TEMP.inv_no AND TI.cny_cd=TEMP.cny_cd
		DELETE TEMP FROM BilledInvoiceShipmentTempsJP TEMP
		INNER JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos TI WITH(NOLOCK) ON TI.inv_no=TEMP.inv_no AND TI.cny_cd=TEMP.cny_cd
	END

		,t.[OldPresentationRule]
		,t.[HasPrinted]
		,t.[CustomerRuleSumary]
		,t.[FileSequenceNumber]
		,t.[IsGeneratedAfterTransform]
		,t.[IsGeneratedAfterDate]
		,0 as [IsMissCharges]
		,0 as  [IsRunUpload]
		,0 as [IsEmailSent]
		,0 as Cmb_Invoices
		,0 as IsconsolidationInvoice
		,null as IsConsolidationDate
		,t.ccard_cd
		,t.GUI_NO
		,t.RCV_NO
		FROM [dbo].[BilledInvoiceInfoTempsCN] t WITH(NOLOCK)
		WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM BilledInvoiceInfos  BII WHERE BII.inv_no=T.INV_NO AND T.CNY_CD=BII.CNY_CD)
		INSERT INTO BilledInvoiceShipments
		SELECT
		GETDATE() [CreationTime]
		,t.[CreationUser]
		,t.[LastModificationTime]
		,t.[LastModificationUser]
		,t.[DeletionTime]
		,t.[IsDeleted]
		,t.[DeletionUser]
		,t.[recid]
		,t.[inv_no]
		,t.[billed_inv_no]
		,t.[inv_type]
		,t.[exp_dt]
		,t.[exp_date]
		,t.[gcc_num]
		,t.[track_no]
		,t.[track_11]
		,t.[csort]
		,t.[product]
		,t.[service]
		,t.[bill_term]
		,t.[zone]
		,t.[mvm_drc]
		,t.[package]
		,t.[actual_wt]
		,t.[bill_wt]
		,t.[wt_msg]
		,t.[ref_1]
		,t.[ref_2]
		,t.[shp_cont]
		,t.[ship_name]
		,t.[ship_city]
		,t.[ship_state]
		,t.[ship_postl]
		,t.[ship_ctry]
		,t.[cons_cont]
		,t.[cons_name]
		,t.[cons_city]
		,t.[cons_state]
		,t.[cons_postl]
		,t.[cons_ctry]
		,t.[dest_ctry]
		,t.[freight]
		,t.[discount]
		,t.[acc_201]
		,t.[acc_205]
		,t.[frt_rate]
		,t.[acc_adc]
		,t.[acc_chg]
		,t.[orig_inv]
		,t.[inv_total]
		,t.[desc_goods]
		,t.[decl_value]
		,t.[sur_ups]
		,t.[sur_brok]
		,t.[exchg_rate]
		,t.[exrate_cd]
		,t.[custom_no]
		,t.[reason_cd]
		,t.[acct_no]
		,t.[rqst_name]
		,t.[rqst_comp]
		,t.[rqst_phone]
		,t.[rqst_email]
		,t.[trd_cont]
		,t.[trd_name]
		,t.[trd_city]
		,t.[trd_state]
		,t.[trd_postal]
		,t.[trd_ctry]
		,t.[comments]
		,t.[trans_cd]
		,t.[chg_msg]
		,t.[tier_inct]
		,t.[minshp_prc]
		,t.[svc_supp]
		,t.[min_pkgwt]
		,t.[frt_taxapl]
		,t.[vartaxamt]
		,t.[ship_flr]
		,t.[ship_sui]
		,t.[ship_sta]
		,t.[cons_flr]
		,t.[cons_sui]
		,t.[cons_sta]
		,t.[ccard_cd]
		,t.[ccard_4dgt]
		,t.[payor]
		,t.[cny_cd]
		,t.[slic_num]
		,t.[custom_clr]
		,t.[CUSTOM_EXCHG_RATE]
		,t.[IA_CRD_TYP_CD]
		,t.[IA_CRD_NR]
		,t.[IA_CRD_ATH_NR]
		,t.[IA_CRD_EXP_DT]
		,t.[recids]
		FROM [dbo].[BilledInvoiceShipmentTempsCN] t WITH(NOLOCK)
		WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM BilledInvoiceShipments BIS WHERE BIS.inv_no=T.INV_NO AND BIS.CNY_CD=T.CNY_CD)
		UPDATE C
		SET
		C.INV_NO = S.INV_NO,
		C.BILLED_INV_NO =  S.BILLED_INV_NO,
		C.SLIC_NUM = S.SLIC_NUM,
		C.LastModificationTime = GETDATE(),
		C.LastModificationUser = 'SYSTEM'
		FROM BilledInvoiceShipmentTempsCN S WITH(NOLOCK)
		JOIN INVOICECHARGES C ON S.RECID = C.RECID AND C.CNY_CD = S.CNY_CD
		WHERE C.CNY_CD = 'CN'
		DELETE TEMP FROM BilledInvoiceInfoTempsCN TEMP
		INNER JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos TI WITH(NOLOCK) ON TI.inv_no=TEMP.inv_no AND TI.cny_cd=TEMP.cny_cd
		DELETE TEMP FROM BilledInvoiceShipmentTempsCN TEMP
		INNER JOIN BilledInvoiceInfos TI WITH(NOLOCK) ON TI.inv_no=TEMP.inv_no AND TI.cny_cd=TEMP.cny_cd
	END
	DECLARE @EXECUTIONTIME DATETIME,@CDATE DATETIME,@EXPIRATIONDAYS INT,@UPDATETRANSFORMTIME BIT
	SELECT @EXECUTIONTIME=value FROM Settings WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE Type='EBSLatestExecutionTime'+@CountryCode
	SELECT @CDATE=value FROM Settings WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE Type='LatestTransformTime'+@CountryCode
	SELECT @EXPIRATIONDAYS=value FROM SysConfig WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE [KEY]='ExpirationDays'
	SELECT @UPDATETRANSFORMTIME=value FROM SysConfig WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE [KEY]='UPDATETRANSFORMTIME'
	IF (DATEDIFF(DAY,@EXECUTIONTIME,@CDATE)>15 AND @UPDATETRANSFORMTIME=1)
	UPDATE Settings SET Value=(SELECT TOP 1 CDATE FROM BilledInvoiceInfos WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE CNY_CD=@CountryCode ORDER BY cdate DESC) WHERE Type='LatestTransformTime'+@CountryCode
	;WITH CTE AS (
	SELECT bis.inv_no,bis.cny_cd,count(1) AS TotalShipment FROM InvoiceShipmentGroupingData ISGD WITH(NOLOCK)
	JOIN BilledInvoiceShipments BIS WITH(NOLOCK) ON BIS.inv_no=ISGD.inv_no AND BIS.cny_cd=ISGD.cny_cd
	WHERE ISNULL(ISGD.TotalShipment,0)=0
	GROUP BY BIS.inv_no,BIS.cny_cd
	)
	UPDATE ISGD SET ISGD.TotalShipment=C.TotalShipment FROM InvoiceShipmentGroupingData ISGD
	JOIN CTE C ON C.inv_no=ISGD.inv_no AND C.cny_cd=ISGD.cny_cd
END
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[USP_Truncate_Table]
@schemaname varchar(50),
@TableName varchar(100) 
with execute AS 'DBO'
AS
IF (SELECT [TABLE_NAME]  FROM [dbo].[TRUNCATE_CANDIDATES] WHERE SCHEMA_NAME=@schemaname and TABLE_NAME=@TableName and TRUNCATE_CANDIDATE='Y')
  IS NULL
   BEGIN
       PRINT 'ERROR: You must specify a valid table name.';
       RETURN(1);
    END
ELSE  
   Begin
       declare @cmd nvarchar(300);
       set @cmd='truncate table ['+@schemaname+'].['+@TableName+'];'
       Exec sp_executesql @cmd;
IF @@ERROR <> 0 
       PRINT 'ERROR: Error received while truncating'
else if @@ERROR=0
       PRINT 'Table '+ @schemaname + '.' + @TableName+' Truncated successfully'
END
